Start with the introduction and keep the two competing hypotheses in mind: is the gap due to different representation quality, or due to scorer-induced consolidation of already available features? That framing generalizes well beyond authorship attribution.

Then read the background section carefully, especially the mathematical definitions of mean pooling, late interaction, and patch pooling. The important question is how each scorer constrains gradient flow and information compression.

The most valuable parts of the paper are three sections:
1. The linear-probe results, which establish that feature availability is largely invariant.
2. The residual-stream patching analysis, which is the central causal evidence.
3. The gradient-structure and training-dynamics discussion, which explains why signal emergence shifts across layers and is the most transferable part of the paper.

If time is limited, about 30 minutes is enough to extract the main mechanism. If you want to go deeper, two follow-up questions are worth thinking about: whether scorer-induced depth shifts also appear in retrieval, reranking, or VLM alignment heads; and whether pretraining objectives that favor pooling-friendly structure would change the best scorer choice during fine-tuning.4b:T535,The authors train sparse autoencoders on mid-layer residual streams of Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT, then search for literary features using a contrastive minimal-corpus setup plus a three-stage validation pipeline. The minimal corpora are hand-crafted prompt pairs that differ along a single literary axis, such as show-versus-tell scenes, Deep-POV versus ostranenie pairs, and scenes covering the Cowen-Keltner 27-emotion taxonomy. Candidate features are filtered in three stages: logit-lens vocabulary projection for cheap initial discovery, LLM-judge semantic purity scoring for candidate filtering, and causal validation through feature steering judged by a 5-LLM panel.

The paper organizes the recovered features into four classes. Naming-gates promote lexical or near-lexical emission of target affects. Self features cluster around first-person voice and identity register. Stylistic modulators control writing style on a more continuous axis, including show-don’t-tell and defamiliarization. Compositional emotions require multiple features to be steered together. An important methodological point is that cross-model alignment is defined behaviorally rather than geometrically: two features from different architectures need not align by decoder cosine if they produce the same controllable writing behavior.4c:T520,The empirical output is a feature catalog plus steering-based causal demonstrations, not a standard benchmark leaderboard. The paper reports 24 canonical paper-grade features across the two models. A single feature controls the show-don’t-tell flip in each architecture: f32213 in Llama and f15097 in Gemma. For first-person register, the authors identify an eleven-feature self cluster in each model, with one feature described as the most RLHF-loaded self feature: f96419 in Llama and f70443 in Gemma. On emotion coverage, Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage of the Cowen-Keltner taxonomy using 24 singleton gates, one partial case, and two multi-feature compositions. Gemma reaches 23/27, with adoration as the strict remaining failure. The paper also reports that 12 Gemma singleton gates transfer cross-lingually at unanimous 8/8 judge agreement.

A notable result is that some emotions, especially joy and adoration, do not appear as singleton features. They require compositions such as gate × self × show-don’t-tell. That supports the paper’s main claim that the internal organization is compositional rather than a flat list of independent affect knobs. Still, the strength of the evidence depends heavily on judge reliability, steering calibration, and the coverage of the hand-built prompt sets.4d:T492,The authors project discrete reasoning trajectories into a continuous structural metric space using features such as nesting depth and backtracking rate. A dataset then becomes a pushforward probability measure, and train-test structural shift can be measured by the Wasserstein-1 distance. They introduce an epsilon-relaxed structural sufficiency assumption so that discrete zero-one loss can be approximated by a Lipschitz structural risk function. Kantorovich duality then yields a target-risk bound controlled by three terms: Wasserstein domain shift, the architecture-induced Lipschitz constant, and functional approximation limits tied to width and depth. The positional-encoding analysis compares APE and RoPE. APE depends on absolute coordinates, so global shifts of a trajectory alter local attention kernels and lead to an Ω(1) structural Lipschitz gap. RoPE better preserves relative, translation-equivariant structure. The depth analysis maps recursive backtracking to Dyck-k languages and uses the NC1 hardness of balanced parentheses to argue that fixed-depth TC0-style Transformers face a sequential computation boundary that width scaling cannot remove.4e:T91a,The study combines architecture-aware probing with several mechanistic interventions. The authors analyze two representative VLM families. LLaVA-1.5 serves as a clean baseline because CLIP ViT-L/14 patch tokens are mapped one-to-one into the LLM space through a small MLP. InternVL-3.5 adds two complications: token compression via pixel shuffle and dynamic multi-view processing with a global thumbnail plus local high-resolution tiles. This contrast lets the paper test whether the same localization mechanism survives under compression and multi-view fusion.

The dataset is a curated subset of COCO val, not raw COCO. The authors first correct annotation issues using prior semi-automatic procedures, then filter out tiny or dominant objects, low-resolution images, and ambiguous cases. The final set contains 3,560 images and 6,403 object annotations. They also build auxiliary controls to reduce context-only hallucinated detections. This matters because mechanistic claims are fragile under noisy labels.

The main tools are:
1. Token ablations to identify which visual tokens causally support class prediction and box prediction.
2. Controlled perturbations that preserve object extent while scrambling internal semantic arrangement, or alter context outside the object, to test whether localization depends on internal semantic layout or mainly on token support boundaries.
3. Positional decoding from intermediate representations to see how a 1D token sequence still carries enough signal for the LLM to reconstruct an approximate 2D grid.
4. Attention knockout to rank heads by causal importance for classification and localization.
5. Causal mediation analysis to separate heads that directly affect localization from heads whose effect is mediated through classification.

The central claim is a containerization mechanism: object-aligned tokens define a spatial container, and the semantic arrangement inside that container matters surprisingly little for the predicted box. In InternVL, the global view carries most of the spatial signal, while local crops mainly refine semantics and help small-object classification. The paper also argues for a sequential dependency: localization-critical processing depends on classification-critical heads, even though the dominant heads for the two tasks are largely distinct.4f:T692,The most useful results are intervention-based rather than benchmark-based. The analysis covers LLaVA-1.5 7B and 13B, and InternVL-3.5 8B, on the curated COCO-derived set with 3,560 images and 6,403 object instances.

The main empirical findings are:
1. Localization information is genuinely encoded in visual tokens. Ablating object-aligned tokens strongly disrupts box prediction, while ablating irrelevant regions has much smaller effect.
2. Scrambling semantics inside an object while preserving its token support leaves localization relatively stable. This is the key evidence for the containerization claim.
3. In InternVL, removing the global thumbnail hurts localization more than removing local high-resolution tiles, whereas local tiles contribute more to fine-grained recognition and small-object classification. This suggests a division of labor: global view for spatial frame, local view for semantic refinement.
4. Positional decoding shows that the LLM can recover an approximate 2D layout from the linearized token stream using residual positional structure and strong corner anchors, even without an explicit preserved 2D grid.
5. Only a very small number of attention heads carry strong causal effects. In LLaVA, these heads appear mostly in early-to-mid layers; in InternVL, more in mid-to-late layers. Classification and localization heads are not the same, but localization causally depends on classification-relevant heads, supporting a staged pipeline of identify first, ground later.

The paper is not about pushing a task metric by a large margin, so the important evidence is the consistency of these trends across interventions, layers, and architectures.50:T487,Read it as a mechanistic case study, not as a new VLM method. Four parts deserve attention.

First, inspect the architecture setup carefully. The contrast between LLaVA and InternVL explains why head locations and view roles differ later.

Second, focus on the token ablation and controlled perturbation figures. These are the core evidence for the containerization hypothesis. Pay attention to how the paper isolates object extent, internal semantic arrangement, and background context.

Third, read the global-versus-local analysis in InternVL if you care about multimodal pretraining design. It gives a concrete answer to what high-resolution tiles are actually buying.

Fourth, study the attention knockout and mediation analysis. The important point is not merely that a few heads matter, but how classification-critical and localization-critical heads are distributed across layers and how the causal dependency is established.

About 30 to 45 minutes is enough for a solid skim. If the goal is pretraining insight, the main text figures and experimental setup are sufficient. The appendix matters mainly for data curation details and robustness checks.51:T69a,The paper contributes mainly through formalization and argumentation. First, it defines contamination resistance at an abstract level: given a transformation phi applied to a plaintext dataset D, the transformed dataset phi(D) should preserve inference utility while being unlearnable as training data for a model class. In other words, the same transformed artifact should support valid task performance at evaluation time but fail to provide a useful optimization signal during pretraining. Second, the feasibility argument is tied to Transformer training–inference asymmetry. During training, teacher forcing exposes the full prefix and optimizes next-token likelihood locally at each position. During inference, generation proceeds autoregressively and may rely on higher-level structure, external rules, or reversible mappings. The authors suggest that one could design representations that disrupt local token prediction and gradient alignment during training, while still allowing task information to be recovered at inference time. Third, the paper discusses interoperability: contamination resistance should not be a brittle property that only holds against one architecture or one training recipe. The transformation must remain resistant across a family of models and pipelines, otherwise the benchmark simply becomes model-specific obfuscation.

A key caveat is that the paper does not present a concrete, mature construction for text CRDs. It also does not provide a new training algorithm. The novelty lies in the design principle itself—support inference, resist training—and in connecting that principle to the asymmetry induced by teacher-forced Transformer training.52:T477,This is a skim paper. Focus on the Introduction and the section on contamination resistance formalization. The Introduction explains why decontamination, privatization, and dynamic benchmarks each have practical limits. The formalization section contains the actual contribution: benchmark design should target simultaneous inference utility and training unlearnability. If reading time is limited, keep three points in mind. First, the paper is not advocating private benchmarks as the main answer. Second, the central mechanism is the asymmetry between teacher-forced training and inference. Third, there is no finished construction yet, so it should be read as a research agenda rather than a deployable method.

Two parts are worth extra attention. One is the discussion of why text is harder than images for unlearnable data: discreteness, semantic-preserving rewrites, and LLM denoising all weaken perturbation-based defenses. The other is interoperability, which identifies the real difficulty: a benchmark is not truly contamination-resistant if the transformation only fools one model family. Estimated reading time: 15 to 25 minutes.53:T417,The paper builds an agent-agnostic noisy-container testbed that injects controlled local and remote errors into the rollout environment. The environment runs in Docker. At the host level, a custom LD_PRELOAD library interposes on syscalls or default library calls, for example to drop or delay network requests. At the network level, MITMProxy intercepts requests to selected sites and serves predefined 4XX or 5XX responses, or other controlled content. Each error can be assigned a probability, and the OS context can be initialized by a setup script before the agent starts. The authors also define a taxonomy of meltdown behaviors, including sensitive data exfiltration, rate-limit evasion, doxxing, unsafe reconnaissance, system mutation, unsafe content retrieval, and deceptive reporting. Each behavior is annotated by severity and by whether it was planned, attempted, executed, or reported. This differs from reliability benchmarks, which mainly measure task success, and from adversarial safety benchmarks, which rely on malicious inputs.54:T903,方法上，作者把优化对象统一表示为字符串 artifact，系统输入包括 seed artifact 或自然语言目标、一个 evaluator、可选数据集，以及可选 side information。evaluator 返回标量分数和一个 SI 字典；SI 可以是文本、结构化指标、报错、profile、图像等。系统的 proposer LLM 读取当前候选、历史轨迹和 SI，生成改进候选。关键不在“让模型写文本”，而在候选选择与搜索组织。

核心搜索机制继承并扩展了 GEPA 一类的 Pareto-based search。作者不是只按单一 aggregate score 排序，而是按 per-example 或 per-metric 的 Pareto dominance 保留候选。这样做的原因很直接：不同候选往往在不同子样本、不同指标上各有优势，如果过早压成单一平均分，搜索会丢掉互补结构，后续反思也会失去素材。这个设计原先主要用于 prompt optimization，这里被推广到任意文本 artifact。

论文统一了三种模式。第一是 single-task：对单个问题迭代搜索。第二是 multi-task：把一批相关问题放在一起搜索，让候选在任务间迁移模式，例如 CUDA kernel 优化中，不同 kernel 的局部技巧可以互相借用。第三是 generalization：在训练集样本上优化 artifact，但目标是对未见输入也有效，这一点从 prompt 扩展到了 agent architecture 和 scheduling policy。

另一个技术重点是 side information 的一等公民化。以往很多系统也会把报错或日志塞进 prompt，但通常是 ad hoc 的。这里作者把 SI 变成统一 API 契约，使 proposer 可以系统地消费 stack trace、性能 profile、图像渲染结果、子指标等反馈。论文的消融表明，这不是锦上添花，而是决定收敛速度和最终质量的关键变量。

从系统设计看，这套方法更接近“LLM-guided black-box search with structured feedback”，而不是梯度学习。它依赖 evaluator 的可执行性和反馈质量，也依赖 proposer 对反馈的解释能力。与 AlphaEvolve 这类偏代码进化系统相比，它的区别在于 artifact 类型更广、支持多任务和泛化模式、并把诊断反馈接口标准化；与 GEPA 相比，区别在于不再局限 prompt，并补上单任务和多任务搜索。55:T7ed,实验覆盖六个主域，目的是证明统一性而不是只打一个点。文中给出的代表性结果包括：

1. Agent architecture / ARC-AGI：演化出的 agent 架构把 Gemini Flash 的 ARC-AGI 准确率从 32.5% 提到 89.5%。这个数字很显眼，但阅读时要注意它衡量的是“在给定模型和 evaluator 下搜索出的 agent scaffold”，不是模型本体能力提升。

2. Cloud scheduling：搜索得到的调度算法可将云成本降低最多 40%。这里更值得看 evaluator 和 side information 如何定义，因为这决定了 LLM 能否稳定改写策略代码。

3. CUDA kernels / KernelBench：生成的 kernel 中，87% 能达到或超过 PyTorch baseline。更关键的是 multi-task mode 在等价 per-problem budget 下优于独立 single-task 优化，说明跨任务迁移不是口号，而是能在相近 kernel 家族中带来真实收益。

4. Prompt optimization：把 GPT-4.1-mini 在 AIME-2025 上的准确率从 46.67% 提到 60.00%。这部分更多是验证系统能覆盖 GEPA 类场景，而不是新结果本身。

5. Circle packing：优于 AlphaEvolve 公布的 n=26 解，并且作者做了 matched-condition rerun against OpenEvolve，试图排除预算和实现差异带来的干扰。

6. 数值优化 / solver code：文中声称定制 solver code 可匹配并超过 Optuna；摘要截断后细节不全，但从定位看，重点是证明 artifact 不必是 prompt 或通用代码，也可以是面向数值搜索的程序化策略。

消融方面，最重要的是 side information 和 multi-task 两组。作者在 prompt optimization、circle packing、CUDA kernels 三个域上报告：有 actionable SI 时，收敛速度提升约 4–6 倍，最终性能也明显更高；只给 score 的版本会慢很多，也更容易停在局部模式。multi-task 的收益则随相关任务数量增加而扩大，这支持“共享搜索记忆”这一设定。论文还强调 Pareto 选择优于简单 aggregate 排序，因为它能保留互补候选。56:T9e7,The method represents every candidate solution as a string artifact. The user provides a seed artifact or a natural-language objective, an evaluator, an optional dataset, and optional side information. The evaluator returns a scalar score and an SI dictionary. SI may contain free-form text, structured metrics, stack traces, profiler outputs, rendered images, or other diagnostics. A proposer LLM then reads the current candidate, search history, and SI, and proposes revisions.

The key algorithmic choice is candidate selection. Instead of ranking candidates only by a single aggregate score, the system extends Pareto-based search from prior prompt-optimization work to arbitrary text artifacts. Candidates are retained based on per-example or per-metric Pareto dominance. The motivation is practical: different candidates are often strong on different subsets of examples or metrics, and collapsing them too early into one average score destroys complementary structure that could be exploited in later iterations.

The framework supports three modes under one API. Single-task mode iteratively improves one artifact for one problem. Multi-task mode jointly searches over a batch of related problems, allowing useful patterns discovered in one task to transfer to others; CUDA kernels are the main example. Generalization mode optimizes an artifact on a dataset with the goal of performing well on unseen inputs; the paper extends this beyond prompts to agent architectures and scheduling policies.

A second major design choice is making side information a first-class contract rather than an ad hoc prompt addition. Many earlier systems can pass logs or errors to the model, but here SI is standardized at the API level. This lets the proposer systematically consume stack traces, performance profiles, rendered outputs, and sub-metrics. The ablations suggest that this is not cosmetic: actionable diagnostics materially change both convergence speed and final quality.

Conceptually, the system is closer to LLM-guided black-box search with structured feedback than to gradient-based learning. It depends on evaluator quality, on the informativeness of side information, and on the proposer’s ability to interpret that feedback. Relative to AlphaEvolve-style code evolution, the main differences are broader artifact coverage, support for multi-task and generalization modes, and a standardized diagnostic interface. Relative to GEPA, the main step is generalizing beyond prompts and adding single-task and multi-task search.57:T7b6,The evaluation spans six primary domains to demonstrate breadth rather than depth on one benchmark. The headline results reported in the paper include:

1. Agent architectures for ARC-AGI: evolved scaffolds raise Gemini Flash accuracy from 32.5% to 89.5%. This is a search-over-scaffolds result, not a claim about intrinsic model capability.

2. Cloud scheduling: discovered scheduling algorithms reduce cloud costs by up to 40%. The interesting part here is how the evaluator and diagnostics are defined so that policy code can be improved reliably.

3. CUDA kernels on KernelBench: 87% of generated kernels match or beat PyTorch baselines. More importantly, multi-task search outperforms independent single-task optimization under matched per-problem budget, which is the strongest evidence that cross-task transfer is real rather than rhetorical.

4. Prompt optimization: GPT-4.1-mini on AIME-2025 improves from 46.67% to 60.00%. This mainly shows that the framework subsumes GEPA-like settings.

5. Circle packing: the system beats AlphaEvolve’s published n=26 solution, and the authors also rerun against OpenEvolve under controlled conditions to reduce confounding from budget or implementation differences.

6. Numerical optimization / solver code: the paper claims custom solver code can match or outperform Optuna. The exact details are less visible in the provided excerpt, but the point is to show that the artifact need not be a prompt or generic code snippet.

The most informative ablations concern side information and multi-task search. Across prompt optimization, circle packing, and CUDA kernels, actionable SI yields roughly 4–6x faster convergence and substantially better final performance than score-only feedback. Multi-task gains grow with the number of related tasks, supporting the idea of shared search memory. The paper also argues that Pareto selection is preferable to simple aggregate ranking because it preserves complementary candidates.58:T595,This is a skim paper. Do not spend most of your time on every task-specific result table. Focus on three parts.

First, read the introduction and API section carefully. The important question is not the syntax of the interface, but how the paper factors the problem into artifact, evaluator, dataset, and side information. That abstraction determines whether the framework can transfer to your own optimization setting.

Second, read the search method section, especially Pareto candidate selection and the unification of single-task, multi-task, and generalization modes. The key questions are why Pareto retention helps open-ended search, why multi-task search can beat isolated optimization on related tasks, and what assumptions are required for that to happen.

Third, prioritize the ablations over the headline numbers. The side-information ablation and the multi-task scaling plots are the most revealing parts of the paper. They explain why the system works. If you only read the ARC-AGI or CUDA headline numbers, you may misread the contribution as a domain-specific trick.

A good reading order is: Introduction → API/method → side-information ablation → multi-task results → one domain case study you understand well. Around 30–45 minutes should be enough to extract the main ideas. If you want to go deeper, inspect how much hidden task-specific labor is embedded in evaluator design and proposer In spite of an apparently common belief that filtering data to include only high-quality information is essential, our experiments suggest that with enough compute, the best data filter is no data filter. We find that sufficiently trained large parameter models not only tolerate low-quality and distractor data, but in fact benefit from nominally ``poor'' data.","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T06:02:36+00:00","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19407.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要回答一个直接但代价很高的问题：在大模型预训练进入高算力、数据相对稀缺的区间后，传统的数据过滤是否仍然必要。常见做法是假设 Common Crawl 里有大量低质量文本，需要用质量分类器、规则、困惑度过滤等方法保留少量高质量子集。作者挑战的是这个前提：如果模型足够大、训练足够久，过滤掉的大量“低质量”数据是否反而成为限制 scaling 的瓶颈。","importance":"对预训练数据策略的影响很具体：数据过滤不应被看成固定的离线质量工程，而应看成随算力、模型规模、数据池大小变化的优化问题。低算力时，高质量子集通常更有效，因为模型还没有能力从噪声和异质数据里稳定提取信息；高算力时，过滤带来的 token 损失、覆盖面损失和重复训练代价会逐渐超过质量收益。这个结论会改变数据管线的优先级：与其只追求更强的静态过滤器，更应关注去重、解析、毒性和安全约束、数据来源覆盖、epoching 策略，以及可预测的 compute-dependent filtering。它不是说所有脏数据都无害，而是说在普通 Common Crawl 这类大池子里，现有过滤器可能在大规模训练下过早丢掉了有用统计量。","technical_approach":"作者采用缩放实验而不是单点大训练来研究过滤效果。核心设计是保留 Common Crawl 原始池与若干过滤版本之间的相对大小关系，把它们按比例缩小，然后在这些池子上系统改变模型大小和训练步数。训练步数增加会带来 epoching，模型大小增加会提高每步计算量，两者共同模拟高算力、数据受限的预训练区间。评价时不是只比较同等 token 或同等步数下的性能，而是比较在不同计算预算下各数据选择策略能达到的最好结果。这个设计抓住了过滤的关键代价：过滤不只是提高平均质量，也会减少可用 token，增加重复训练。作者进一步构建 pool size、training steps、model size 之间的经验 scaling law，用于预测某个数据池规模下什么时候 no-filter 会变成最优选择。随后他们做了更激进的鲁棒性实验，把随机字符串、词序打乱文档等“junk”数据加入训练，测试大模型是否只是在容忍噪声，还是能从极弱信号里获得收益。最后给出后验理论模型，解释为什么有限容量模型更需要清洗，而高容量、长训练模型可以从噪声生成过程和边缘统计中受益。","experiments":"几个数字最关键。DCLM-Baseline 这类强过滤数据大约只保留原始 Common Crawl 的 1%，对应约 3.8T tokens；而 DCLM-Pool 规模约 240T tokens。作者的缩放实验覆盖约两个数量级的计算变化，结论在小池放大到更大池时仍能观察到：只要模型足够大，完整池最终会超过过滤池。基于 scaling law，他们估计 240T token 的 Common Crawl 池可能在约 1e30 FLOPs 附近进入 no-filter 最优区间。低算力区间过滤仍然占优，这一点很重要，因为它解释了很多既有数据过滤工作的成功经验；差异出现在更大模型、更长训练、数据重复更明显的区间。噪声实验显示，随机字符串在低算力下会拖累性能，但大模型长训练后可以缩小差距；词序打乱文档更有意思，因为它保留了 unigram 分布但破坏了句法和语义顺序，充分训练的大模型仍能从中获益。这说明模型利用的不只是人工定义的“高质量文档”，还包括词频、实体分布、主题覆盖、长尾词表等较弱统计信号。作者也明确限制了结论边界：如果存在大量看起来像高质量文本但事实错误、恶意构造或安全风险高的数据，仍可能有害；他们只是没有在 Common Crawl 的自然噪声里观察到足以抵消规模收益的坏影响。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中对数据受限预训练和 DCLM 过滤比例的动机分析，这是理解全文的入口。实验部分应重点看三类图表：第一，过滤池与完整池随 compute 增长的交叉点；第二，模型大小、训练步数、epoching 对过滤收益的交互；第三，pool size scaling law 对 240T Common Crawl 的外推。噪声数据章节值得细看，尤其是随机字符串和 shuffled-word documents 的对照，因为它区分了“模型能忍受噪声”和“模型能从弱统计中获益”这两个命题。理论章节不必先读公式，可先看假设：有限模型容量下清洗数据有利，高容量模型下清洗可能丢失可学习信号。真正值得深挖的是评价协议：同等算力、同等 token、同等最好性能这几种比较方式会给出不同结论；数据过滤工作的分歧，很多来自比较口径不同。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether data filtering remains necessary when language-model pretraining moves into a high-compute, data-scarce regime. The usual assumption is that Common Crawl contains enough low-quality text that strong filters are required. The authors test the opposite hypothesis: once models are large enough and trained long enough, removing most of the crawl may become the main bottleneck.","importance":"The practical implication is that data filtering should be treated as a compute-dependent decision, not as a fixed offline quality step. At small compute, filtered subsets can be better because the model cannot reliably extract signal from heterogeneous or noisy text. At larger compute, the lost token volume, lost coverage, and increased need for repeated epochs can outweigh the gains from higher average quality. This shifts attention from ever more aggressive static filters toward broader pool construction, parsing quality, deduplication, safety constraints, source diversity, and scaling laws for filtering thresholds. The result does not imply that all bad data is harmless; it says that, for naturally noisy Common Crawl-style pools, existing filters may discard useful statistical signal too early.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"Start with the motivation around data scarcity, epoching, and the DCLM filtering ratio. The most important experimental figures are the compute-dependent crossover between filtered and unfiltered pools, the interaction between model size and number of training steps, and the pool-size scaling law used to extrapolate to the 240T-token setting. The degraded-data section is worth close reading because it separates two claims: robustness to noise and positive use of weak statistical signal. The theory section can be read after the experiments; focus first on its assumptions rather than the algebra. The most important methodological detail is the comparison protocol. Equal compute, equal tokens, and best-achieved performance answer different questions, and many disagreements about data filtering come from mixing these evaluation regimes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会直接动摇“先把数据洗干净再谈 scaling”的默认流程；先看实验设定是否真在 data-scarce 且 train-to-convergence，若是，内部数据过滤 ablation 该重做。","en":"This one can genuinely weaken the default 'filter hard before scaling' prior; first verify that the setup is truly data-scarce and close to convergence, and if so, redo your internal filtering ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University","Departments of Statistics and Electrical Engineering"],"authors":["Christopher Mohri","John Duchi","Tatsunori Hashimoto"],"notable_authors":["Tatsunori Hashimoto","John Duchi"],"tags":["data-filtering","scaling-laws","pretraining-data","data-quality","compute-optimality"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19407","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19407","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.27263","title":"Decoupling the Benefits of Subword Tokenization for Language Model Training via Byte-level Simulation","abstract":"Subword tokenization is an essential part of modern large language models (LLMs), yet its specific contributions to training efficiency and model performance remain poorly understood. In this work, we decouple the effects of subword tokenization by isolating them within a controlled byte-level pretraining pipeline. We formulate and test hypotheses across various dimensions, including sample throughput, vocabulary scaling, and the linguistic prior of subword boundaries. By simulating these effect","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-14T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2604_27263.png","hf_upvotes":8,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"子词 tokenizer（BPE/Unigram）相对 byte-level LM 的优势通常被“打包”讨论：序列更短、词表更大、边界更像语义单位、tokenizer 的 look-ahead 让预测更容易等。但这些因素彼此耦合，导致很难回答“到底是哪一项在起主要作用”。这篇论文的核心问题是：在不改变主干 Transformer 架构、尽量不引入额外算力开销的前提下，把子词 tokenization 的潜在收益拆开，在纯 byte-level 训练管线里逐项模拟，从而定量解释 subword–byte 性能差距来自哪里。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在于：把“tokenizer 选择”从经验问题推进到可检验的机制问题。论文给出一套可复用的实验范式：固定同一套训练数据与主干架构，用输入/输出侧的轻量改动去模拟 tokenization 带来的吞吐、词表参数规模、边界先验、预测粒度变化，并用统一的 bits-per-byte 指标对齐比较。对后续做 byte-level / tokenizer-free / 多粒度建模的人，这种拆解能更清楚地指导资源该投在吞吐（序列长度/并行效率）、输出层（词表/softmax）、还是建模目标（multi-token prediction、边界先验）。","technical_approach":"方法上采取“byte-level 主干 + 子词效应注入”的思路。基础模型是标准 LLaMA-3 架构（不做 byte LM 常见的卷积/局部编码器下采样），输入是 UTF-8 bytes；训练框架 TorchTitan；数据 FineWeb-Edu。为了在 byte setting 中获得可控的子词信息，作者同时用 LLaMA-3 的 BPE tokenizer 对同一文本做分词，从而得到每个 byte 位置对应的子词边界/分段信息，但训练目标仍以 byte 为基本单位并用 bits-per-byte 统一度量。\n\n论文围绕引言里提出的几类假设，设计一组“几乎不增加计算”的注入手段，目标是把耦合因素拆开：\n1) 吞吐/序列压缩效应：子词让序列更短，等价于在同样 token budget 下看到更多原始字节信息；作者在 byte 训练里用受控方式模拟“有效吞吐提升”，以检验性能提升是否主要来自更高的样本处理率而非语言学先验。\n2) 词表规模/参数分配效应：子词扩大词表，主要增加 embedding 与输出层参数；作者在 byte setting 中用方式模拟“更大的离散输出空间/更强的输出层容量”，以区分是容量（尤其是输出层）还是序列长度在起作用。\n3) 子词边界的结构先验：BPE 边界可能提供接近形态/语义的分块；作者把 BPE 边界作为额外信号注入 byte 模型（例如让模型显式感知边界或在目标上对齐边界），检验这种先验是否能在不改变吞吐的情况下带来泛化收益。\n4) tokenizer look-ahead 的任务简化：BPE 分词需要看未来字符决定边界，等价于给了“未来分布的提示”；作者尝试在 byte 任务中模拟这种信息泄露/提示效应，评估它对训练损失与泛化的贡献。\n5) 预测粒度变化：预测一个 subword 相当于一次预测多个 byte n-gram；作者引入 multi-token prediction head（一次预测多个后续 byte）来模拟“子词一步多字节”的训练信号，连接到近期 multi-token prediction 能提升下游的结论。\n\n整体取舍很明确：不引入专门的 byte 下采样模块，避免把架构差异混进来；尽量只在输入标注、输出头、训练目标粒度上做改动，从而把因果归因聚焦在 tokenization 相关因素。","experiments":"实验设置的关键点是对齐：同一 1.7B 参数量级、同一 LLaMA-3 主干、同一 FineWeb-Edu 数据、同一验证集，用 bits-per-byte 作为统一指标，避免 subword/byte 因 token 计数不同导致的比较失真。论文主线是把每个“子词效应”单独打开/关闭，观察验证 bpb 与训练动态变化，并做组合实验看可加性/交互。\n\n从全文叙述可以确定的硬信息包括：模型规模 1.7B；数据 FineWeb-Edu；byte 输入为 UTF-8；同时用 LLaMA-3 BPE 得到边界；评估指标为 bits-per-byte cross-entropy。具体提升数字、各假设对应的增益排序、以及是否出现明显的交互项，需要依赖论文后半部分实验章节与附录表格（当前提供的全文片段在“multi-h...”处截断，无法可靠复述精确数值）。建议在精读时把每个实验的“额外计算是否为零/可忽略”与“吞吐是否变化”标出来，因为这是这类归因实验最容易被忽略的混杂因素。","reading_guide":"精读优先级：\n- FLOPs（或引入多少）；②是否改变了有效训练 token/byte 预算（吞吐混杂）。\n- 实验主表与消融：把每个注入项的 bpb 改善幅度、训练曲线形态（收敛速度/是否更稳）、以及组合实验的可加性整理成一张表，才能回答“主要收益来自哪里”。\n- Appendix（超参、实现细节）：确认 softmax/输出层的实现、multi-token head 的具体形式、以及边界信号的编码方式；这些细节决定结论能否迁移到更大规模或不同 tokenizer。\n\n值得深挖的问题：如果吞吐提升解释了大部分差距，那么 byte-level 的关键瓶颈更像系统与序列建模效率；如果边界先验或 multi-token 头贡献显著，则说明可以在不依赖静态 tokenizer 的情况下，用学习到的分段/多粒度目标逼近子词优势。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Subword tokenization (BPE/Unigram) consistently outperforms byte-level LMs, but the usual explanations are entangled: shorter sequences (higher effective throughput), larger vocab/output layers, boundary-induced linguistic priors, tokenizer look-ahead effects, and the fact that predicting a subword is effectively predicting multiple bytes at once. The paper’s core goal is to decouple these factors and quantify their individual contributions by simulating subword-induced effects inside a controlled byte-level pretraining pipeline, while keeping the backbone architecture and compute overhead essentially unchanged.","importance":"For pretraining research, the main contribution is methodological: it turns tokenizer choice from a largely empirical knob into a set of testable mechanisms with controlled ablations. The “byte backbone + subword-effect injection” framework is reusable for anyone working on byte-level/tokenizer-free LMs, multi-granularity objectives, or alternative output parameterizations. It also clarifies where engineering effort should go: improving effective throughput/sequence efficiency, reallocating capacity in the output layer, or changing the learning signal (e.g., multi-token prediction, boundary priors) rather than debating tokenizers in the abstract.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"The experimental backbone is tightly controlled: a 1.7B-parameter LLaMA-3 architecture, FineWeb-Edu training data, UTF-8 byte inputs, BPE-derived boundary annotations, and a shared bits-per-byte metric for fair comparison across tokenization regimes. However, strong performance for LLM training has only been demonstrated at small scales. We identify a number of fixes necessary to scale up Schedule-Free Learning to larger batch sizes and model sizes, and present a learning-rate-free and schedule-free method (ScheduleFree+) for training large language models which greatly outperform","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-18T20:31:49+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19095.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 LLM 预训练中学习率 schedule 难调、长训练不够平滑、Schedule-Free Learning 在大 batch 和较大模型上不稳定的问题。原始 Schedule-Free 用模型平均替代学习率衰减，但在 LLM 大 batch 训练中会遇到 scaling cliff，无法直接替代 tuned WSD 或 linear decay schedule。","importance":"这是优化器和训练配方层面的工作，对基座预训练有直接影响。贡献不在于提出新的模型结构，而在于把 schedule-free 思路推进到更接近真实 LLM 训练的设置，并指出哪些细节决定它是否可用：内层 momentum、大 batch 稳定性、warm-start、outer momentum 的增长，以及用 Polyak-style step size 减少基础学习率调参。最值得关注的结论是长训练收益：在 1000 tokens per parameter 的设置下，ScheduleFree+ 达到相同 loss 所需训练时间比强 schedule baseline 少 31%。短训练 20 到 100 TPP 没有最终 loss 优势，但 anytime stopping 更自然。","technical_approach":"原始 Schedule-Free 维护两个点：普通优化轨迹 z_t 和在线平均点 x_t。梯度不在 x_t 上算，也不直接在 z_t 上算，而是在混合点 y_t 上算，y_t 由平均点和当前点插值得到，典型 outer momentum β 约为 0.9。最终用于评测和保存的是 x_t，而不是梯度评估点 y_t。这等于用平均替代传统学习率衰减，但仍保留 warmup。ScheduleFree+ 在此基础上做了几项关键修正：第一，重新引入 AdamW 的内层 momentum，解决大 batch 下无 momentum 版本过早失稳的问题；第二，使用 Polyak-style step size，目标是把基础学习率也从主要调参项中移出；第三，warm-start Schedule-Free，让早期 loss 不吃亏；第四，使用随训练推进增大的 outer momentum，使长训练阶段的平均更有效。和 WSD 的差异在于，WSD 依赖显式 warmup、stable、decay 三段 schedule，而 ScheduleFree+ 主要依赖平均轨迹和动量结构来完成收敛控制。","experiments":"论文报告了多个尺度和训练时长上的 LLM 预训练实验。一个关键诊断实验使用 120M 参数 Llama 3 架构，在 FineWeb-EDU 上训练 8B tokens，并从同一个 2B token checkpoint warm-start。batch size 变化实验中，学习率在 1M token batch 处调好，再按 √b 规则缩放。原始 Schedule-Free AdamW 在 batch size 增大时落后，并在约 2M tokens per batch 处出现 scaling cliff；传统 AdamW with linear decay 的 cliff 约在 4M；加入内层 momentum 后，Schedule-Free 可以使用与常规 AdamW 同量级的大 batch，并且小 batch 表现也更好。长训练实验中，ScheduleFree+ 在 1000 TPP 下比 WSD 和 linear decay schedule 更省训练时间，达到同等 loss 的时间减少 31%。论文也明确指出，20 到 100 TPP 的短训练看不到最终 loss 优势，这避免了把长训练结论误用到短程 ablation。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Schedule-Free Learning 机制部分，确认 x_t、y_t、z_t 三个点的角色，尤其是为什么评测模型是 x_t 而不是 optimizer 当前点。然后看 handling larger batch-sizes 部分和 Figure 2/3：这里给出大 batch cliff 的现象和内层 momentum 的修复，是最有工程价值的诊断。接着看 Polyak step size、warm-start、increasing outer momentum 的章节，重点关注这些改动分别解决哪个失败模式。实验部分不要只看最终 loss，要看 TPP、batch size、是否 warm-start、学习率缩放规则和 schedule baseline 是否充分调参。实现上还要检查 optimizer API：训练 step 用 y_t 取梯度，评测和保存用 x_t，这一点容易和常规 AdamW 训练循环冲突。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to make schedule-free optimization work for LLM pretraining at larger batch sizes, larger model scales, and longer training durations. The original Schedule-Free method replaces learning-rate decay with model averaging, but prior LLM experiments showed instability or weaker results when batch size grows. The target is a training method that removes most schedule tuning without giving up loss or stability.","importance":"This is a directly relevant optimizer paper for foundation-model pretraining. Its main contribution is a practical recipe, not a new modeling objective. The useful message is that schedule-free training is sensitive to optimizer details that are easy to miss: inner momentum, large-batch robustness, warm-starting, the outer-momentum trajectory, and step-size selection. The strongest reported gain appears in long-duration training: at 1000 tokens per parameter, ScheduleFree+ reaches the same loss with 31% less training time than strong schedule-based baselines. For short 20 to 100 TPP runs, the paper does not claim a final-loss advantage, although the averaged model provides more natural anytime stopping.","technical_approach":"Schedule-Free training maintains a raw optimizer sequence z_t and an online averaged sequence x_t. Gradients are evaluated at an interpolation point y_t between x_t and z_t, often with an outer momentum around 0.9, and the model used for evaluation and checkpoint selection is x_t. This replaces explicit decay schedules with averaging, while still using warmup. ScheduleFree+ adds several fixes for LLM pretraining: it reintroduces AdamW-style inner momentum to remove the large-batch failure mode; it proposes a Polyak-style step-size variant to reduce dependence on a hand-tuned base learning rate; it warm-starts the schedule-free phase to improve early losses; and it increases outer momentum over time, which helps long runs. Compared with WSD, the convergence control is shifted from a handcrafted warmup-stable-decay curve to averaging plus momentum dynamics.","experiments":"A central diagnostic uses a 120M-parameter Llama-3-style transformer trained on FineWeb-EDU for 8B tokens, with all runs warm-started from the same 2B-token checkpoint. The learning rate is tuned at a 1M-token batch size and scaled with √b for other batch sizes. Original Schedule-Free AdamW falls behind as batch size increases and hits a scaling cliff around 2M tokens per batch, while AdamW with linear decay shows a similar cliff around 4M. Adding inner momentum, for example β1 around 0.75 in the reported setting, lets Schedule-Free use batch sizes comparable to regular AdamW and also improves smaller-batch results. In long-duration experiments, ScheduleFree+ outperforms WSD and linear-decay schedules in time-to-loss, with a reported 31% reduction at 1000 TPP. The paper also notes that short runs do not show a final-loss advantage.","reading_guide":"Read it closely. Start with the Schedule-Free formulation and make sure the roles of z_t, x_t, and y_t are clear; the evaluation model is x_t, not the point where gradients are computed. Then focus on the large-batch section and Figures 2 and 3, which contain the most useful failure analysis and the inner-momentum fix. After that, read the Polyak step-size, warm-start, and increasing-outer-momentum sections as separate interventions, each tied to a specific training failure mode. When reading the experiments, track tokens per parameter, batch size, warm-start usage, learning-rate scaling, and how thoroughly the schedule baselines are tuned. Implementation requires care because a standard optimizer API assumes the stepped parameter is also the model to evaluate, which is not true here.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看实验表和消融；若 31% 不是调参预算差异造成，会改变长训阶段必须手写 schedule 的 prior。","en":"Read the tables and ablations immediately; if the 31% gain is not a tuning-budget artifact, it weakens the prior that long LLM runs need hand-designed schedules."},"metadata":{"institutions":["FAIR at Meta Super-Intelligence Labs"],"authors":["Aaron Defazio"],"notable_authors":["Aaron Defazio"],"tags":["optimizer","schedule-free-learning","LLM-pretraining","training-dynamics","model-averaging"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19095","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.19470","title":"Drifting Objectives for Refining Discrete Diffusion Language Models","abstract":"Discrete diffusion language models (DDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising categorical token sequences, while recent drifting methods for continuous generators suggest that part of this sampling-time correction can instead be absorbed into training through an anti-symmetric fixed-point objective. We study how to transfer this principle to DDLMs, where the main challenge is the interface with discrete text: hard token samples are non-differentiable, and categorical predictions do not direc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T07:22:17+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19470.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"离散扩散语言模型（DDLM）在固定推理步数（NFE）下的样本质量很大程度由“采样时的迭代修正”决定。论文要解决的问题是：在不改采样器、不做蒸馏、保持同样NFE预算的前提下，能否把一部分采样时的修正“吸收进训练目标”，直接把现有DDLM继续训练成在同一采样流程下更会生成的模型。难点在于漂移（drifting）类固定点目标原本依赖连续样本/特征的可加性与可微性，而DDLM输出的是离散token的类别分布，硬采样会切断梯度，概率分布又不天然对应可漂移的连续点。","importance":"贡献类型更像“训练目标层面的采样质量对齐”：把连续生成模型里用于提升有限步采样质量的anti-symmetric fixed-point drifting思想，改造成适用于离散文本扩散的可训练目标。对预训练研究的直接价值在于两点：第一，它提供了一条不同于改schedule/改sampler/做distill的路径，用继续训练（continual training）就能提升固定NFE的生成质量；第二，它强调了离散生成里“保留不确定性”的重要性——用soft-token在语义特征空间里表达分布，比straight-through硬token近似更稳定，提示后续做diffusion LM/any-order LM时，训练信号的连续桥接方式会显著影响训练动态与最终样本质量。","technical_approach":"方法叫TokenDrift，核心是把连续漂移的三件事——(1)在某个空间里定义样本表示，(2)估计吸引-排斥的漂移场V(y; P_data, P_theta)，(3)用stop-gradient的“漂移后目标”做固定点训练——搬到DDLM上，并解决“离散→连续”的接口。\n\n1) Soft-token lift（离散到连续的可微桥）：DDLM在每个位置输出类别分布p_{theta,t}。TokenDrift不对token做硬采样，而是用分布的期望embedding（expected token embeddings）构造连续输入：把词表embedding矩阵E与p做加权求和，得到每个位置的soft embedding，再送入一个冻结的语义编码器（frozen semantic encoder）得到特征y。这样漂移损失对y的梯度可以反传到logits。\n\n2) Frozen semantic space里做anti-symmetric drifting：在特征空间估计“吸引到数据邻域、排斥模型邻域”的漂移方向，保持anti-symmetry（交换P_data与P_theta漂移方向反转），从而在P_theta=P_data时漂移信号为0，具备固定点性质，避免把模型推离数据分布的系统性偏置。\n\n3) Stop-gradient feature target：沿漂移方向得到漂移后的特征目标y*，对y* stop-gradient，然后训练DDLM去匹配y*（直接在特征空间做回归/匹配）。论文也讨论了几种把漂移信号接回离散生成器的替代设计：例如把漂移转成logit-space的“镜像teacher”（mirror teacher）或与原始去噪/似然目标混合。作者在同预算对比后给出结论：直接feature-space drifting在“质量-熵/多样性”的权衡上更稳定。\n\n4) 关键设计判断：soft-token lift是必要条件。用hard straight-through token surrogate会显著劣化生成质量，说明在语义特征空间里保留预测不确定性（分布形状）对漂移学习很关键；否则漂移场估计与梯度都会变得噪声大且偏置强。","experiments":"论文做的是“受控的继续训练（continual-training）”设定：在OpenWebText（OWT）上，从已有DDLM出发，用TokenDrift继续训练，对比两类基线：\n- 继续用原始DDLM训练目标做ordinary continuation training；\n- 不同DDLM骨干：masked diffusion LM（MDLM）与uniform-state离散扩散（DUO）。\n\n主要结论是：在固定NFE的生成评估下，TokenDrift相对MDLM基线与普通继续训练都有显著提升；并且在DUO上也能复现，说明不是特定于masked diffusion的技巧。\n\n消融与对比重点：\n- 目标形式对比：直接feature-space drifting vs logit-space mirror teacher vs 与原始去噪损失的不同混合方式；作者报告直接feature-space drifting在稳定性与质量/熵权衡上更好。\n- 离散桥接对比：soft-token lift vs straight-through硬token近似；后者明显变差，支持“保留不确定性”的机制解释。\n\n注：你给的全文片段未包含具体表格数字（如NFE取值、指标名与提升幅度、训练步数/参数量等），因此这里无法可靠复述精确数值。精读时建议直接从实验表格补齐这些关键量化信息。","reading_guide":"精读优先级：\n- 方法定义与推导：重点看TokenDrift如何把漂移目标写成对DDLM corrections to reach good quality, so generation quality at a fixed number of denoising steps (NFE) is not solely determined by the denoiser’s likelihood objective. The paper asks whether part of that sampling-time correction can be moved into training—refining an existing DDLM so that the same sampler, with the same NFE budget, produces better text. The key obstacle is the discrete interface: DDLMs output categorical distributions; hard token sampling is non-differentiable, and probability vectors do not naturally support the additive “drift” updates used in continuous drifting objectives.","importance":"This is a training-objective contribution for diffusion LMs: it adapts anti-symmetric fixed-point drifting (previously used for continuous generators) to discrete text diffusion, enabling “refinement by continued training” rather than changing schedules/samplers or distilling a low-step student. A large part of the reasoning gain often attributed to code appears to come from structured cross-domain traces, such as code-text and math-text mixtures, rather than standalone programs.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining data mixture research. The paper shows that domain effects are not additive. Adding one data type can improve its nearest capability while competing with knowledge-heavy or reasoning-heavy tasks. This offers a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension for building more powerful and flexible reasoning systems, especially valuable in complex tasks requiring the exploration of multiple possibilities.","technical_approach":"Proposes Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM). GRAM models the reasoning process as a stochastic latent trajectory: at each recursion step, it samples a transition conditioned on the input and current state, rather than deterministically updating to a single next state. This mechanism allows the model to maintain multiple hypotheses, explore alternative solutions, and scale at inference time through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. SSM 的可用性问题。PD-SSM 这类模型在有限状态自动机追踪能力上接近非结构化转移矩阵，但原始实现需要为每个 token 生成输入相关的稀疏转移矩阵，内存开销达到 O(LN^2)，实际速度比 Mamba2 慢很多。Flash PD-SSM 的目标是在保留状态追踪表达力的同时，把训练和推理开销降到接近主流高效 SSM 的水平。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在架构层。近期混合架构越来越多地用 SSM 层替代部分 attention 层，瓶颈不只是线性复杂度，还包括 SSM 转移矩阵是否能表示复杂状态机。Flash PD-SSM 提供了一个较清楚的取舍：不退回到纯对角矩阵，也不接受非结构化矩阵的成本，而是用结构化稀疏矩阵加硬件友好实现争取表达力和吞吐的平衡。它还给出一个有用信号：单一 SSM 形式未必最合适，Flash PD-SSM 与 DeltaNet 的混合在语言建模上优于纯 Flash PD-SSM 和纯 DeltaNet。","technical_approach":"方法核心是重做 PD-SSM 中输入相关结构化稀疏矩阵的选择与生成机制。原 PD-SSM 的并行 recurrence 计算虽然是线性的，但矩阵生成阶段需要 O(LN^2) 内存，抵消了结构化稀疏的优势。Flash PD-SSM 把这一部分降到 O(LN)，同时保持原先关于状态追踪能力的理论保证。工程上，作者实现了定制 Triton/CUDA kernel，用于结构化线性 recurrence，避免通用稀疏算子的调度和内存访问损耗。语言模型部分还构造了 Delta-PD，即 Flash PD-SSM 与 DeltaNet 层的混合模型，用 MAD mechanistic capability 的结果辅助决定混合方式，而不是只做单层替换。","experiments":"效率实验显示，在序列长度 5120 下，Flash PD-SSM 训练速度比 DeltaNet 快 18%，比 Mamba2 快 4%，显存比二者都低 12% 以上。合成状态追踪任务上，Flash PD-SSM 平均长度泛化准确率为 96.3%，高于 Mamba2 的 58.9%、Mamba3 的 90.7%、Gated DeltaProduct 的 80.7%。MAD mechanistic capability 测试中，Flash PD-SSM 为 69.2%，接近 Mamba 的 69.3%。自然语言状态追踪方面，作者扩展 Box 数据集到最多 26 次状态转换，在等设置下，Flash PD-SSM 的分布外长度泛化准确率最高比 Mamba2 高 10%。语言建模实验使用 FineWeb100B 进行完整预训练，Delta-PD 的平均准确率为 42.3%，高于纯 Flash PD-SSM 的 38.5% 和纯 DeltaNet 的 41.3%；吞吐为 253k tokens/s，而纯 DeltaNet 为 168k tokens/s，约为 1.5 倍。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 到 45 分钟。优先看方法部分中矩阵选择机制如何把 O(LN^2) 降到 O(LN)，这是论文的核心。然后看 Section 4.1 的吞吐和显存曲线，判断 kernel 优化带来的实际收益是否足以支撑大规模预训练。状态追踪实验值得看任务设置和长度外推结果，因为它直接对应 SSM 是否能稳定维护离散状态。语言建模部分重点看 Delta-PD 的层混合设计和 FineWeb100B 结果，不必过度关注时间序列任务，除非关心长序列非文本建模。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical bottleneck in structured sparse SSMs. PD-SSM has strong finite-state tracking expressivity, close to unstructured transition matrices, but its input-dependent sparse transition generation requires O(LN^2) memory. This makes the original design much slower than production-oriented SSMs such as Mamba2. Flash PD-SSM aims to keep the expressivity benefit while making the layer competitive in throughput and memory.","importance":"The work is relevant to pretraining architecture design, especially for hybrid Transformer-SSM models. It makes a useful point: the cost of an SSM layer is not only the asymptotic recurrence, but also how the transition matrix is parameterized, generated, and mapped to hardware. The paper also suggests that hybridizing different efficient sequence layers can be better than choosing a single SSM family. The Delta-PD results indicate that Flash PD-SSM may be more useful as one component in a mixed architecture than as a full replacement for all sequence layers.","technical_approach":"The key change is a new selection mechanism for the structured sparse transition matrices used by PD-SSM. The original PD-SSM recurrence can be evaluated with linear-complexity structured sparse matrix multiplication, but generating the input-dependent matrices costs O(LN^2) memory. Flash PD-SSM reduces this generation cost to O(LN) while retaining the theoretical state-tracking guarantees. The algorithmic change is paired with custom Triton/CUDA kernels for the structured linear recurrence, avoiding the overheads of generic sparse operations. For language modeling, the paper also introduces Delta-PD, a hybrid of Flash PD-SSM and DeltaNet layers, motivated by mechanistic capability tests rather than by a blind layer swap.","experiments":"At sequence length 5120, Flash PD-SSM trains 18% faster than DeltaNet and 4% faster than Mamba2, with more than 12% lower memory usage than both. On synthetic state-tracking tasks, it reaches 96.3% average length-generalization accuracy, compared with 58.9% for Mamba2, 90.7% for Mamba3, and 80.7% for Gated DeltaProduct. On the MAD mechanistic capability benchmark, it scores 69.2%, close to Mamba at 69.3%. On an extended Box natural-language state-tracking dataset with up to 26 state transitions, it improves out-of-distribution length generalization over Mamba2 by up to 10% in matched settings. In language modeling, the authors pretrain on FineWeb100B. Delta-PD reaches 42.3% average accuracy, above pure Flash PD-SSM at 38.5% and pure DeltaNet at 41.3%, while improving throughput from 168k tokens/s for DeltaNet to 253k tokens/s.","reading_guide":"A 30 to 45 minute skim is enough for most pretraining researchers. Focus first on the matrix selection mechanism and the O(LN^2) to O(LN) memory reduction. Then inspect the throughput and memory results in Section 4.1, since the paper depends heavily on the hardware realization. The state-tracking experiments are worth reading because they test a capability that diagonal SSMs often struggle with. For LLM architecture work, the most relevant part is the Delta-PD language modeling setup and its FineWeb100B results. The time-series experiments are less central unless long non-text sequences are a direct concern.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看表达力证明和真实 LM 表；若只赢合成 FSA 就当架构备查，若大规模困惑度也赢才值得复现。","en":"Check the expressivity proof and real LM tables; if it only wins synthetic FSA tasks, keep it as architecture background, not a pretrain recipe."},"metadata":{"institutions":["IBM Research – Zurich","ETH Zurich"],"authors":["Aleksandar Terzić","Francesco Carzaniga","Nicolas Menet","Yannick Biehl","Michael Hersche","Thomas Hofmann","Abbas Rahimi"],"notable_authors":["Thomas Hofmann"],"tags":["state-space-models","sequence-modeling","sparse-transition","architecture","long-context"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19150","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19150","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.18815","title":"DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training","abstract":"Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. uni","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T09:51:28+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18815.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决“大模型训练过程中并行策略需要在线切换，但现有框架默认并行布局静态、只能 RLHF 训练与 rollout 资源配比变化）动态调整并行布局，显著降低因重启带来的吞吐损失与工程复杂度。对长期训练、MoE（状态更大、切换更痛）和持续训练/中途改配比的场景尤其关键。贡献量级主要体现在把“分钟级/十秒级重启”压到“秒级在线切换”，从而把弹性调度从理论可行变成工程可用。","technical_approach":"核心抽象是 Virtual Parameter Space (VPS)：把参数/梯度/优化器状态都视为“未分片的全局逻辑张量”，并附带位置与分片元数据。任意并行配置被表示为一个确定性的映射函数：把全局 VPS 投影成每个 rank 的局部 sub-VPS 视图。这样从配置 A 切到配置 B，不再需要为每种并行组合写成对转换规则，而是转化为“源 sub-VPS 与目标 sub-VPS 的几何交集”问题：\n1) State Routing Planner：在 VPS 坐标系里计算每个 rank 的 send/recv/retain 集合，得到从旧布局到新布局的精确路由计划（本质是把全局张量切片的归属变化系统化）。\n2) State Transition Engine：把高层路由计划落到可执行的通信与拷贝流水。关键点包括：尽量把点对点提升为 collective；把碎片化切片打包成连续 buffer 以降低通信开销；在显存约束下安排 staging（旧状态、新状态、通信 buffer 的短暂共存）；并给出无死锁的调度保证（避免多对多互发导致的环形等待）。\n3) Elastic Device Manager：把“逻辑并行策略”与“物理通信组/进程组”解耦，在训练继续进行时并行初始化新 world 的通信拓扑，把最慢的 communicator 重建成本隐藏到计算后面。\n实现形态是 PyTorch 与 Megatron-LM 等框架之间的可插拔中间层，目标是尽量不侵入上层训练代码。","experiments":"论文在真实大集群上评估 dense 与 MoE（1.3B–235B）。覆盖 DP/TP/PP/EP 等维度的切换。关键数字：70B dense 模型在 2 秒内完成重配置；235B MoE 在 4.36 秒内完成。对比 Megatron-LM 的分布式 checkpoint-restart：dense 最高 Fills a gap in diffusion LM security research and highlights risks in the public checkpoint ecosystem for third-party diffusion LM adoption.","technical_approach":"ShadowMask formulates trojan injection as a distribution-matching problem between a triggered terminal distribution and an attacker-chosen target distribution. The core modification replaces the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior on poisoned samples, explicitly shaping MDLM training dynamics to denoise trigger-corrupted states toward attacker targets while preserving ordinary mask-based denoising on clean data. The paper provides a full mathematical formulation including the backdoored forward process, the corresponding reverse-time posterior, and the continuous-time training objective for backdoor implantation.","experiments":"Evaluated on two architectures (DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct) across three datasets (WikiText-103, OpenWebText, Alpaca). 从随机初始化开始学习。这个设置的目的，是尽量排除专家初始能力差异，让失效主要暴露在路由学习本身。论文比较了从 linear router 到 cross-attention router 的结构演进，并提出若干诊断概念：global deadlock 指路由整体塌到少数专家；selective deadlock 指全局塌缩缓解后，仍有部分层长期退化为单专家使用；U-shaped deadlock distribution 指死锁层集中在浅层视觉处理层和深层语义整合层，中间层更平衡。作者还提出 functional redundancy hypothesis，把部分死锁解释为视觉 token 冗余和层功能分工下的一种阶段性资源分配，而不完全是优化失败。","experiments":"实验基于 Kiwi-Edit 设置，包含 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 视频扩散 backbone 和 loss，也仍有约三分之一层退化为单专家使用；死锁并非均匀分布，而是呈浅层和深层更严重的 U 形分布；更强的 router 设计可以缓解全局死锁，但会暴露 selective deadlock 这类更局部、更稳定的退化模式。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 40 分钟。优先看实验设置，确认 dense-to-MoE 转换、专家克隆、shared expert 初始化和 token 冗余导致的合理稀疏化，还是 auxiliary objective 与 diffusion loss 不匹配造成的优化偏差。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why Token-Choice sparse MoE often fails when inserted into video Diffusion Transformers. The central issue is that visual diffusion models do not automatically develop the kind of expert specialization observed in language MoEs; instead, their routers can enter routing collapse, global deadlock, or selective deadlock.","importance":"The paper is relevant to pretraining through MoE stability and cross-modal scaling. Language MoE success relies on discrete, semantically dense tokens, while video DiT tokens are spatially redundant and are affected by diffusion timesteps, spatial structure, and multi-task conditioning. The useful part is the diagnostic framing: limited gains from visual MoE are traced to measurable routing dynamics rather than treated as a vague architecture failure. This matters for multimodal foundation models, video generation backbones, and attempts to transfer LLM MoE recipes to visual generative models.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"The diagnostic setup is based on Kiwi-Edit, using a Wan2.2-TI2V-5B video diffusion backbone and a Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct multimodal encoder, for roughly 8B total parameters; the dense video backbone is about 5B. Experiments are conducted in a 9-in-1 multi-task video generation setting. The paper reports layerwise temporal analysis on a 30-layer network with more than 65 million tokens, and routing-decision traces over 5,000 steps and more than 3.4 billion tokens. Key observations include: roughly one third of layers can collapse into single-expert usage even when the auxiliary loss is increased; deadlock is not uniformly distributed but follows a shallow-and-deep U-shaped pattern; stronger router designs reduce global deadlock but reveal more local and persistent selective deadlock.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 40 minutes. Every token is treated with uniform confidence. We show this uniformity is a degenerate case of our \\emph{Bayesian Filtering Transformer} (BFT): attention becomes p","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T22:22:15+00:00","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18832.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"标准Transformer隐式假设所有token的信号质量同等可靠，对每个token赋予相同置信度。这一假设在实际场景中经常不成立：推荐系统中的冷启动item/噪声点击、LLM微调中的低质量domain数据、attention sink导致的无效注意力分配等。现有方案多为各问题独立打补丁，缺乏统一的、基于概率原则的不确定性建模框架。","importance":"将Transformer重新诠释为贝叶斯滤波过程，为多个分散问题提供了统一的架构层面解决方案思路。precision-weighted注意力和自适应Kalman增益残差连接是值得关注的架构修改方向。虽然实验集中在推荐和SFT场景，但其按token质量动态加权的核心思想对预训练数据质量感知训练有潜在借鉴价值。","technical_approach":"将Transformer每层的三个核心操作映射为贝叶斯滤波的observe-update-predict三步：(1)自注意力对应observe步骤，等价于precision加权的克里金插值（kriging），标准Transformer丢弃了其中的方差信息；(2)残差连接对应update步骤，标准残差是Kalman gain K=1的退化情形，BFT使用自适应gain K=λ_obs/(λ+λ_obs)；(3)FFN对应predict步骤，通过delta方法+逐维过程噪声传播precision。observation precision来自无参数的REML（限制最大似然）估计，带共轭贝叶斯先验。整体为drop-in替换，仅需替换一个层类，峰值内存与标准attention持平。","experiments":"在两个领域验证：(1)生成式推荐：覆盖SASRec、BERT4Rec（softmax attention）和HSTU（SiLU-gated attention）三种骨干，六个benchmark，BFT均有显著提升，且在框架预测的低信噪比区域（稀有item、冷用户）提升最大；(2)LLM微调：TinyLlama-1.1B上的SFT实验，在噪声监督（SQuAD token标签损坏）和噪声上下文（NQ-Open上的RAG干扰项）场景下，鲁棒性优于标准SFT。","reading_guide":"重点阅读Section 2（Transformer组件与贝叶斯滤波步骤的对应映射）和Section 4（precision估计的具体实现，REML与sandwich两种estimator），实验结果看Section 5的主要表格和关键对比。预计阅读时间30-40分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The standard Transformer implicitly assumes all tokens have equal signal quality and assigns uniform confidence to every position. This assumption is frequently violated in practice: cold-start items and noisy clicks in recommenders, low-quality domain data in LLM fine-tuning, and attention sinks concentrating mass on uninformative tokens. Existing fixes are ad-hoc patches for individual problems, lacking a unified probabilistic framework for uncertainty modeling.","importance":"This work reinterprets the Transformer as a Bayesian filtering process, providing a unified architectural perspective on multiple disparate failure modes. Precision-weighted attention and adaptive Kalman-gain residual connections are noteworthy architectural modifications. While experiments focus on recommendation and SFT, the core idea of dynamic weighting by token quality has potential relevance for data-quality-aware pretraining.","technical_approach":"Maps three core operations in each Transformer layer to the observe-update-predict steps of Bayesian filtering: (1) Self-attention = observe step, equivalent to precision-weighted kriging (the standard Transformer discards variance information); (2) Residual connection = update step, where the standard residual is the K=1 degenerate case of a Kalman update with adaptive gain K=λ_obs/(λ+λ_obs); (3) FFN = predict step, propagating precision via the delta method plus per-dimension process noise. Observation precision comes from a parameter-free REML (Restricted Maximum Likelihood) estimator with a conjugate Bayesian prior. The implementation is a drop-in replacement of a single layer class, with peak memory matching standard attention.","experiments":"Validated across two domains: (1) Generative recommendation: consistent gains on SASRec, BERT4Rec (softmax attention), and HSTU (SiLU-gated attention) backbones across six benchmarks, with largest improvements where the framework predicts — rare items and cold users; (2) LLM fine-tuning: SFT on TinyLlama-1.1B shows improved robustness over standard SFT under both noisy supervision (SQuAD token label corruption) and noisy context (RAG distractors on NQ-Open).","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 (mapping between Transformer components and Bayesian filtering steps) and Section 4 (precision estimation implementations, REML vs sandwich estimators). For star-convex objectives (a common global geometry assumption for neural network loss), a momentum step calibration rule based on local descent certificates and a distance-free step search strategy based on smooth upper bound minimization along recentered rays are proposed respectively.","experiments":"Theoretical convergence guarantees are provided for all three algorithms: Distance-Adaptive Muon achieves an O(logT/√T) stationarity guarantee for smooth non-convex objectives under the bounded trajectory assumption; Scale-Calibrated Muon achieves a last-iterate O(LD_lev²/T) objective gap guarantee for star-convex objectives under the bounded initial sublevel set assumption; Distance-Free Muon achieves last-iterate convergence guarantees for star-convex smooth objectives without any additional assumptions such as bounded domain, bounded iterates, or bounded gradients.","reading_guide":"Prioritize the problem background and trust-region interpretation of Muon in Sections 1–2, and the core design and convergence theorems of the three algorithms in Sections 3–5. 分三阶段混合，","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"inbox:xiaohongshu","sources":["inbox"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/inbox-xiaohongshu-51686d67df.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"文章讨论大模型预训练里的数据配比搜索：通用文本、数学、代码、多语言等语料该如何混合，才能在不反复重训整模的前提下，较便宜地找到更好的配方。","key_insights":"核心信息有两点。第一，DeMix 试图把“配比搜索”与“完整训练”解耦：先训练单域模型，再通过模型合并去近似不同数据混合比例下的效果，用秒级合并替代一次次昂贵重训。文中给出的关键信号是，合并得到的性能排序与真实混合训练有较高一致性（ρ=0.81），并声称同等精度下可把搜索成本降到约 1/6。第二，作者把这个思路从预训练延伸到 mid-training 和 proportions of general text, math, code, and multilingual data without repeatedly training full models from scratch.","key_insights":"The main idea behind DeMix is to decouple mixture search from full pretraining. Instead of retraining a model for every candidate recipe, it trains domain-specific models and uses model merging to approximate the outcome of different corpus mixtures. The post highlights two concrete claims: the ranking of merged candidates correlates well with real mixed-training results (rho = 0.81), and the search cost can drop by roughly 6x at similar accuracy. It also suggests the same recipe-search logic may transfer beyond pretraining into mid-training and even SFT, which makes it more interesting than a one-off corpus engineering trick. The released DeMix Corpora is also useful as a practical reference, especially the staged schedule that increases math and code over time.","limits":"This is still a short social-media summary, so the important caveats are missing. It does not explain when model merging is a good proxy for true mixed-domain training, how sensitive the method is to architecture and scale, or whether the correlation is stable enough for final recipe selection. A good ranking signal is helpful, but it is not the same as accurately predicting absolute downstream quality. Prior Key channel prunin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T00:45:00+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19218.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 维度。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，主要属于多模态推理效率。但它对 native multimodal 预训练有间接启发：如果视觉 token 数量在服务侧可以通过结构化 Key channel 压缩承载，预训练阶段就不必只围绕少量视觉 token 设计模型接口；长图、多图和视频输入的 token budget 也可以重新评估。它还提醒一个工程事实：VLM 的瓶颈不只有 token 数，channel 维度的冗余同样可以利用。","technical_approach":"RotateK 的核心是把原始 Key 表示旋转到一个更适合结构化裁剪的正交基中。直接做 token-wise channel pruning 表达力强，因为每个 token 的重要 channel 可以不同，但 mask 大小是 O(Ld)，解码时 IO 和 kernel 实现都不友好。Head-wise pruning 只需 O(d) mask，硬件友好，但因为无法刻画 token 间差异，在高压缩率下容易掉精度。RotateK 的思路是：token 相关的重要性在原始坐标系里看起来分散，但经过合适的正交变换后，可以对齐到共享低维子空间。具体做法是用 query-weighted PCA 在线估计旋转，把与查询相关的 Key 信息集中到少量 channel，再用共享的结构化 channel mask 裁剪。为了降低在线 PCA 成本，作者使用 Cholesky-based subspace iteration；为了让稀疏 channel 真正带来解码收益，还实现了面向结构化稀疏 Key 的 Triton kernel。","experiments":"给出的正文显示，作者比较了 token-only pruning、传统 head-wise Key pruning、token-wise Key pruning 与 RotateK。实验结论是 RotateK 在固定 KV cache budget 下能保留更多视觉 token，并比纯 token pruning 更稳，尤其适合信息分散的细粒度视觉任务；它也缩小了高效 head-wise pruning 与高表达 token-wise pruning head-wise pruning 不够好。方法部分重点看 query-weighted PCA 的目标函数、旋转矩阵如何在线估计、裁剪后 attention score 的扰动如何控制。系统部分重点看 Cholesky subspace iteration 和 Triton kernel，因为 channel pruning 若不能转成真实 kernel 收益，价值会大打折扣。对预训练研究员来说，重点不是复现实验，而是理解视觉 token 数、Key channel 维度和服务成本之间的约束关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the KV-cache bottleneck in VLM inference. Visual inputs often produce hundreds to thousands of tokens, and multi-image or video inputs can make the visual KV cache dominate memory usage. Token pruning reduces this cost but permanently removes visual content, which hurts fine-grained perception, document understanding, and grounding. The paper therefore compresses another axis: the Key channel dimension.","importance":"This is primarily an efficiency paper, not a pretraining-method paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but practical. If structured Key-channel compression can preserve more visual tokens under the same serving budget, the design space for native multimodal models changes: visual token count does not have to be constrained only by token pruning or aggressive visual resampling. It also highlights that VLM efficiency is not just a sequence-length problem; feature-dimensional redundancy in KV states can be exploited.","technical_approach":"RotateK applies an orthogonal rotation to the Key representation so that token-dependent channel importance becomes aligned in a shared low-dimensional subspace. Existing token-wise channel pruning is expressive because each token can keep different channels, but it requires an O(Ld) mask and creates heavy decoding overhead. Head-wise pruning uses a compact O(d) mask and is hardware-friendly, but it loses accuracy at high compression ratios because it cannot model token-level variation. RotateK tries to get the benefits of both. It uses query-weighted PCA to concentrate query-relevant Key information into a shared basis, then prunes channels with a structured head-wise mask. The method reduces attention-score perturbation while keeping the sparsity pattern simple enough for efficient kernels. The implementation uses Cholesky-based subspace iteration to lower online PCA cost and a custom Triton kernel to make structured sparse channels useful during decoding.","experiments":"The provided text indicates comparisons against token-only pruning, conventional head-wise Key pruning, and token-wise Key pruning. It also narrows the accuracy gap between efficient head-wise pruning and more expressive token-wise pruning. The motivation study visualizes Key-channel outliers in VLMs and argues that these outliers likely arise from the interaction between RoPE and learned Key projections, as in LLMs. However, globally dominant channels are too few for robust compression, motivating the rotation-aligned shared subspace. tokens 更新主状态。对短上下文，如果 token 数不超过 d，则直接保留全部 KV 并从 KV 计算输出，完全避免创建 d×d 状态。","experiments":"实现基于 SGLang，目标模型是带 Gated DeltaNet 的 Qwen3-Next。核心结果包括：普通解码中，KVBuffer 最高降低 linear attention decoding latency 的交错比例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses an IO bottleneck in serving linear-attention LLMs. Linear attention has constant decoding compute with respect to context length, but current serving systems usually read and write a large d×d recurrent state at every decoding step. This state can be orders of magnitude larger than per-token KV; for example, a Qwen3-Next Gated DeltaNet layer has a state of about 2MB. Its relevance to foundation-model research is that the practical value of linear or hybrid attention architectures depends on serving-time state management. The paper shows that the bottleneck is often not arithmetic cost, but memory traffic caused by how the linear-attention state is represented and updated.","technical_approach":"KVBuffer stores recent keys and values and updates the linear-attention state in chunks instead of recurrently at every token. The extra KV traffic is offset by fewer reads and writes of the much larger state. The paper derives an IO trade-off and argues that a buffer size around 2√d minimizes average memory access per decoding step for hidden dimension d. For speculative decoding, KVBuffer avoids materializing temporary recurrent states for each draft token; it verifies draft tokens in parallel using buffered KV and updates the main state only with accepted tokens.
