In this paper, we theoretically analyze the error structure of MXFP4 activation quantization, revealing that the root cause of this ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T09:05:47+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19561.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但很现实的问题：MXFP4 这类 block floating-point 4bit 格式在硬件上已经开始成为低比特推理的主流候选，但把它直接用于 LLM 激活量化时，精度下降往往明显，原因并不只是“4bit 太低”，而是激活分布与 MXFP4 的表示结构存在两层错配。第一层是 block 之间的能量分布极不均匀，少数高方差 block 决定了共享指数，导致同 block 内大量小值被压成 0；第二层是 block 内部的数值分布与 e2m1 codebook 的理想假设不匹配，真实激活大量集中在 0 附近，造成 codebook 使用塌缩，很多码字几乎闲置。论文的核心问题不是一般性的“如何做 W4A4”，而是“如何让激活分布主动适配 MXFP4 这种共享指数浮点格式的几何结构”。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是训练范式创新，而是推理侧低比特表示与模型内部统计结构之间的一次较深入对齐分析。启发主要有两点。第一，低比特浮点量化不能简单沿用为整数网格设计的 outlier suppression 思路，因为 BFP/MXFP4 的误差来源更依赖 block 级共享尺度与码字利用率。第二，旋转不只是“压 outlier”，还可以被看成一种分布重排工具，用来匹配目标数值格式的拓扑结构。这个视角对后续做训练感知量化、量化友好预训练、甚至激活正则化都有参考意义。贡献量级上，它更像是面向下一代硬件格式的一篇扎实方法论文，而不是会改变基座预训练主线的工作。","technical_approach":"方法叫 TORQ，核心是 two-level orthogonal rotation。设计上它把 MXFP4 激活量化误差拆成两个近似正交的子问题，然后分别用两类正交变换处理。\n\n第一层是 macro-equilibrium rotation，针对 inter-block variance imbalance。作者从 Schur-Horn theorem 出发，构造跨 block 的正交变换，把原本集中在少数 block 的能量尽量均匀摊开。直观上，这是在不改变整体二范数的前提下，重新分配各 block 的方差，使共享指数不再被极少数高能 block 拉高。论文声称并证明，方差均衡的 block 配置在其误差模型下对应全局量化误差最优或近优。这里和以往 FlatQuant、OSTQuant 一类方法的区别在于：那些方法主要面向整数均匀量化，目标是削弱坐标级 outlier；TORQ 这里优化的是 block 级尺度冲突。\n\n第二层是 micro-alignment rotation，针对 intra-block codebook utilization imbalance。作者在 block 或 group 内再做一层正交旋转，目标不是继续压大值，而是让局部分布更均匀地落入 MXFP4 的各个量化区间。论文用 maximum entropy 原则来刻画这一目标：如果在给定能量约束下，旋转后样本对 codebook 区间的占用更均匀，那么有效信息容量更高，码字浪费更少。换句话说，macro rotation 解决“共享指数被谁主导”，micro rotation 解决“已有指数下码字怎么被用满”。\n\n实现上，TORQ 是 retraining-free PTQ。旋转矩阵通过少量 calibration data 闭式或近闭式构造，不需要反向传播重训。推理时把旋转并入线性层前后，额外参数和算力开销都比较小。论文反复强调，这不是把已有整数旋转法直接搬到 MXFP4，而是针对 MXFP4 的 block floating-point 结构单独设计目标函数和分解方式。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，实验覆盖了主流开源 LLM，包括 LLaMA 和 Qwen 系列，并与 SingleQuant、DARTQuant、FlatQuant、OSTQuant 等方法比较。作者的主结论是：在 MXFP4 激活量化场景下，TORQ 相比这些主要为 W4A4 整数量化设计的旋转方法有更稳定的精度保持，尤其在长尾激活更明显、block 共享指数更敏感的层上收益更大。\n\n论文中比较关键的实验观察不是单一 benchmark 分数，而是几类误差统计：\n1. block 方差分布在旋转前后是否变平；\n2. 小幅值元素被量化为 0 的比例是否显著下降；\n3. MXFP4 codebook 的占用率是否更均匀，是否缓解了 codebook collapse；\n4. 两级旋转分别带来多少收益，是否近似可加。\n\n引言里给了两个很重要的量化现象：超过 90% 的小幅值元素会因共享指数过高而被清零；实际推理中约 50% 的码字范围只被 10% 的数据激活。这两个数字基本构成了方法设计的经验基础。若精读全文，建议重点核对主表中 perplexity / downstream accuracy 的具体退化幅度，以及消融里 macro-only、micro-only、full TORQ 三者的差异。如果论文给出了不同 block size、不同 calibration set 大小、不同模型规模下的结果，这些会比单一平均分更能说明方法是否稳健。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题读。\n\n先看 Introduction 和方法动机图，确认作者提出的两个失配：block 间方差失衡、block 内码字利用失衡。这里决定了整篇论文是否成立。\n\n然后看理论部分里关于 Schur-Horn theorem 的推导。重点不是逐行证明，而是弄清楚作者为什么能把“均衡各 block 能量”写成一个正交旋转问题，以及这个最优性结论依赖哪些假设。若这些假设很强，方法的泛化边界也会比较清楚。\n\n接着看 micro-alignment 那一节。要注意 maximum entropy 在这里是严格优化目标，还是一种启发式设计原则；还要看它如何映射到具体旋转矩阵构造。如果这里只是经验近似，方法强项可能主要来自第一层 macro rotation。\n\n实验部分建议优先看三类图表：一是 block variance histogram，二是 codebook occupancy / zeroing ratio，三是主任务精度表。这样能把“误差机制分析”和“最终效果”连起来。\n\n泛读大约 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主线；若要判断是否值得借鉴到训练感知量化或量化友好预训练，理论和消融至少要细看 1 小时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a deployment-side problem that is easy to underestimate: MXFP4, a block floating-point 4-bit format, is becoming a practical target for next-generation low-bit inference, but naïvely applying it to LLM activations causes substantial accuracy loss. The paper argues that the issue is not simply “4 bits are too few.” The real mismatch is structural. First, activation energy is highly uneven across blocks, so a small number of high-variance blocks determine the shared exponent and wipe out many small values in the same block. Second, within each block, real activation distributions do not match the e2m1 codebook assumption; values cluster near zero, causing severe codebook under-utilization. The central question is therefore how to reshape activations so that they better fit the geometry of MXFP4, rather than how to tweak generic low-bit quantization heuristics.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it gives a sharper view of how internal activation statistics interact with hardware-oriented numeric formats. Two lessons stand out. First, low-bit floating-point quantization should not be treated as a minor variant of integer quantization; the dominant error sources are different because of shared exponents and block-level scaling. Second, rotation can be used as a distribution-matching tool, not just as an outlier suppression trick. That perspective may matter for future work on quantization-aware training, activation regularization, or pretraining models whose hidden states are more compatible with low-bit floating-point formats. In terms of impact, this looks like a solid method paper for an emerging inference format, not a paper that changes the main direction of LLM pretraining.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"$25","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把MXFP4掉点拆成“跨block方差/码本利用”两类结构问题，这个诊断值得抄；只看方法动机与消融，别期待预训练启示。","en":"Worth skimming for the MXFP4 error diagnosis (inter-block variance vs. codebook usage) and ablations; don’t expect pretraining insights."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zukang Xu","Xing Hu","Dawei Yang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["quantization","MXFP4","post-training-quantization","orthogonal-rotation","activation-quantization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19561","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19561","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.19972","title":"Block-Sphere Vector Quantization","abstract":"Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provid","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T15:18:56+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19972.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.DB","cs.DS"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是旋转式向量量化里一个长期有些混乱的问题：EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant 这些方法都在讲“随机旋转后再量化”很有效，但它们优化的失真目标、理论保证形式、解码规则和实现假设并不一致，导致比较常常失真。作者先统一回答“这些方法到底谁在什么意义下更好”，再进一步提出一个新的 block-level 球面量化器 BlockQuant，试图在期望 MSE 和期望内积误差上同时优于现有旋转量化方法。对 LLM 相关场景，论文主要落点在 embedding 压缩和 KV-cache 量化，而不是训练本身。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接影响有限，因为它不涉及训练目标、数据或模型架构。但它对长上下文推理和记忆带宽问题有现实意义，尤其是 KV-cache 压缩。更重要的是，这篇论文把“随机旋转后的向量近似服从球面对称分布”这一直觉做成了统一理论框架，并把 codebook 设计和 dequantization rule 的作用拆开分析。这个拆分很有价值：很多量化论文把两者混在一起比较，结论容易不稳。若后续有人做训练期隐藏状态分布整形、cache-aware pretraining 或 retrieval embedding 压缩，这里的分析框架可以直接借用。贡献量级上，属于理论梳理加一个结构清晰的新量化器。","technical_approach":"技术上分两部分。\n\n第一部分是统一理论比较。作者把 EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant 放到同一个随机量化框架下，从三个标准比较：重建 MSE、期望内积失真、以及高概率 bit-complexity 保证。结论不是谁全面占优，而是各有偏向：EDEN 和 TurboQuant 在 MSE 上更强，EDEN 对期望内积误差也有优势，RabitQ 则在高概率控制上更好。这里一个关键观察是，很多差异并不只来自 codebook 本身，还来自解码器如何利用 side information 或归一化结构。论文把“量化码本”和“反量化规则”分开讨论，这一点比单纯报实验分数更有信息量。\n\n第二部分是提出 Block-Sphere Quantization，也叫 BlockQuant。它延续 rotation-based quantization 的基本套路：先对高维向量做随机旋转，使其更接近各向同性；但不同于 EDEN/TurboQuant 只看单坐标边缘分布，BlockQuant 把坐标分成小 block，用随机旋转后单位球面诱导出的精确 block marginal distribution 来设计 centroid。可以把它理解为一种不依赖数据学习的、基于球面几何先验的 block quantizer。它和 product quantization 有相似之处，都是分块处理；但 PQ 通常从数据中学习码本，而 BlockQuant 的码本来自已知分布，因此理论上更容易分析，也不需要训练码本。\n\n作者还给出 lower-bound 分析，修正了该问题设定下的 Shannon lower bound，并说明当 block size 增大时，BlockQuant 会逐渐逼近理想球面量化极限。这个结论很关键，因为它解释了为什么从 coordinate-wise 走向 small-block quantization 是合理方向：不是经验上更好，而是更接近目标分布的几何结构。\n\n从实现角度看，BlockQuant 需要在每个小 block 上做近邻 centroid 搜索。论文声称可以通过高效近似最近中心搜索把运行时控制在与现有方法相近的水平，因此不是纯理论构造。","experiments":"实验分为三层。\n\n第一层是失真指标验证。作者在真实 embedding 数据上比较 BlockQuant、EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant 的 reconstruction error 和 inner-product distortion，结论是 BlockQuant 在这两个期望型指标上都更低。这和理论分析是一致的。\n\n第二层是理论与经验的对照。论文应该展示不同 bit-width、不同 block size 下的误差变化，并验证 block size 增大时性能向理想球面量化下界靠近。这里值得特别看的是小 bit 宽下常数项是否真的改善，而不只是渐近阶更好。\n\n第三层是 LLM 相关应用，主要是长上下文 KV-cache 量化。作者声称在保持相近运行时的前提下，BlockQuant 带来更低的 cache 量化误差，并转化为更好的实际推理质量。给出的全文片段没有具体数字，因此不能编造精确提升幅度；但阅读时应重点核对：使用的是哪类模型、cache 量化位宽是多少、对 perplexity 或长上下文任务分数的影响有多大，以及与现有 rotation-based cache quantizer 的差距是否稳定。\n\n如果论文附带了 ablation，最值得看的通常是：block size p 的选择、近似最近中心搜索带来的精度-速度折中、随机旋转是否需要固定种子或多次采样、以及不同向量分布偏离球面对称假设时方法是否退化。","reading_guide":"这篇论文的阅读顺序很重要。\n\n先看统一理论比较那一部分，尤其是总表和定义。要先弄清楚作者比较的是哪三种标准：MSE、expected inner-product distortion、high-probability bit complexity。很多读者容易把这些保证混成一个“谁更好”的结论，但论文恰恰是在反对这种混读。\n\n然后看 BlockQuant 的构造。重点不是公式细节，而是理解为什么随机旋转后，按 block 使用球面诱导边缘分布会比逐坐标量化更合理。这里如果能把它和 product quantization、lattice quantization、以及 TurboQuant 的 coordinate-wise 设计放在一起理解，收获会更大。\n\n接着看 lower bound 和 block size 分析。这部分决定了方法是不是只有经验优势，还是确实更接近理论极限。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 distortion 曲线和 KV-cache 应用表，而不是 embedding 任务的零散结果。因为对 LLM 研究员来说，真正相关的是 cache 压缩是否有稳定收益，以及这种收益是否值得额外近邻搜索开销。\n\n泛读大约 30 分钟可以抓住主结论；若要真正吃透理论比较和下界推导，预计需要 1 到 1.5 小时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a persistent source of confusion in rotation-based vector quantization. Methods such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant are often discussed together, but they optimize different distortion criteria, provide different types of guarantees, and rely on different decoding assumptions. As a result, comparisons are often not apples-to-apples. The paper first builds a unified theoretical comparison of these methods, then proposes a new quantizer, Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), aimed at improving expected MSE and expected inner-product distortion. For LLM-related use cases, the practical target is mainly embedding compression and KV-cache quantization rather than model training.","importance":"The direct relevance to pretraining is limited, since the work is not about objectives, data, scaling, or architecture. Its practical relevance is stronger on the inference side, especially for long-context KV-cache compression. The more durable contribution is conceptual: it separates the role of the codebook from the role of the dequantization rule, and it formalizes the intuition that a randomly rotated high-dimensional vector is well modeled by spherical structure. That framing is useful beyond this paper. It could inform future work on cache-aware model design, hidden-state shaping during training, or compression of retrieval embeddings. In scope, this is best viewed as a careful theory-plus-method paper rather than a major shift in foundation-model training.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"$28","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"向量量化的理论与方法进展，只看§3的量化器统一对比框架即可，对低比特量化选型有参考价值","en":"Theoretical and methodological progress in vector quantization, only read §3's unified quantizer comparison framework, which has reference value for low-bit quantization selection"},"metadata":{"institutions":["Seoul National University"],"authors":["Heesang Ann","Joongkyu Lee","Min-hwan Oh"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["vector quantization","model compression","quantization theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19972","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20104","title":"Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding","abstract":"Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T16:55:48+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20104.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章解决的是 speculative decoding 里一个很具体但很实际的矛盾：树状 draft 越大，target 一次验证能接受的 token 越多，但 draft 侧搜索、显存带宽和验证开销也越大，最终 wall-clock speedup 不一定更好；而动态剪枝虽然更快，却会把原本可能正确的分支一起剪掉，导致 mean accepted tokens 被静态大树的上界卡住。作者要解决的核心问题，是如何在不增加 target 额外前向、保持 lossless 的前提下，把“剪枝省下来的预算”重新利用起来，弥补动态树的覆盖损失。","importance":"这项工作属于推理效率，不是预训练方法。但它对基座研究员仍有两层启发。第一，它把 speculative decoding 的优化目标从单纯“更聪明地剪枝”改成“预算重分配”，这个视角比继续调 pruning controller 更有工程含义。第二，它说明 retrieval 不一定只能做独立 drafter，也可以作为 tree construction 的补偿机制，和 parametric drafting 形成互补。贡献量级上，更像是对 EAGLE-3 / dynamic tree 这一条技术线的结构性改进，而不是新范式。若你关心 serving、长输出场景、KV-cache 带宽瓶颈，这篇比只看 acceptance rate 的论文更接近真实部署问题。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Graft，一个 training-free、lossless 的 prune-then-graft 混合树构造框架。它的逻辑分两步。\n\n第一步是 pruning。作者沿用动态树思路，在若干 calibrated pruning checkpoints 上根据 draft 置信度决定是否提前截断某些低置信分支。这里的关键不是单纯减少计算，而是把被剪掉的节点数视为“释放出的候选预算”。论文强调，动态树方法的问题在于它们只能返回静态树的子树，因此无论 controller 多好，误剪都会让 accepted length 低于 dense tree 的理论上界。\n\n第二步是 grafting。Graft 不把这些空出来的 slot 留空，也不继续往更深层做不确定的 autoregressive drafting，而是用 retrieval-based continuations 填进去。这样最终送给 target 验证的总预算与原始静态树相同，但候选来源从单一 parametric draft 变成“剪枝后的 parametric subtree + retrieval branches”。作者的论点是，retrieval 分支不受“必须是原静态树子树”的拓扑约束，因此可以补回 pruning 误伤掉的一部分有效 continuation，从而打破 latency-MAT 的原有 Pareto 前沿。\n\n实现上，作者强调 GPU 友好。检索分支根植于当前 token，与自回归 drafting 并行准备；使用 GPU-resident adjacency matrix 存储和查询局部转移；通过 warm-up 初始化，并用 target 验证信号在线更新。这样做有三个目的：1）避免 CPU lookup 和同步开销；2）不改变标准 tree attention / verification 路径；3）不引入额外 target model forward。文章还特别指出，长上下文下这个设计更有利：一方面 AR drafting 更贵，剪枝收益更大；另一方面上下文更长会提供更多局部转移模式，提升 retrieval 命中率。","experiments":"给出的全文片段主要是引言和方法动机，没有完整实验表格与具体数字，因此无法像正式精读那样列出 speedup、MAT、batch size、上下文长度、模型规模等精确结果。能从全文内容确认的实验主线有三条。第一，作者会把 Graft 与 dense static tree baseline（如 EAGLE-3）以及 Large draft trees can improve the mean accepted tokens per verification step, but they also increase draft-side search cost, VRAM bandwidth pressure, and verification overhead, so the end-to-end speedup often falls short of what acceptance metrics suggest. Dynamic pruning reduces latency, but it can only produce subtrees of the original dense tree, which means pruning mistakes permanently remove valid continuations and cap the achievable accepted length below the dense-tree upper bound. differ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T14:49:57+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19929.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 VLM 低比特 PTQ 里一个很具体但常被粗糙处理的问题：文本 token 和视觉 token 的激活分布并不只是“整体不同”，而是差异集中在少量通道上，而且两种模态的异常通道位置并不重合。已有 transformation-based PTQ 往往对两种模态共享同一组变换，等于让同一套缩放或旋转同时适配两套冲突分布，结果是在低比特尤其是 W4A4、W3A3 这类设置下，量化误差被跨模态异质性放大。论文的核心问题不是一般性的量化精度提升，而是如何在不重新训练 VLM 的前提下，显式拆开这种通道级模态冲突。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练方法本身，而在统一多模态模型的表示结构理解。它给出一个较清楚的经验结论：VLM 的模态异质性并非均匀弥散在全层特征里，而是具有稀疏、通道化、模态错位的结构。这一点对后续做多模态预训练、共享骨干设计、模态对齐损失、甚至 tokenizer 后的表示整形都有启发。工程层面，这篇工作把 VLM PTQ 从“沿用 LLM 量化套路”推进到“按模态结构做分流”，贡献量级属于部署研究里的扎实改进，不改变模型训练范式，但对统一模型的内部统计结构有信息增益。","technical_approach":"方法分两步。第一步是 MOCD，Modality-specific Outlier Channel Decoupling。作者先分析校准集上的激活，发现视觉 token 往往呈长尾分布，少数通道幅值特别大；文本 token 的异常更分散，但仍可在通道维上识别出高风险位置。于是他们不再学习一套全通道共享变换，而是把输入通道划成三组：两种模态都相对稳定的 main channels、文本特有异常通道、视觉特有异常通道。划分依据也不是单一统计量：视觉侧更看重 magnitude saliency，文本侧更看重 response consistency，用来识别哪些通道对该模态更敏感、更不适合与另一模态共享量化路径。完成分组后，激活和权重矩阵按通道切分，分别走不同的量化分支。这样做的关键不是增加模型容量，而是避免让视觉异常值去主导文本量化尺度，反过来也一样。\n\n第二步是 ACC，Adaptive Cross-Modal Calibration。即便 main channels 已经去掉了大部分模态特异异常，剩余通道里仍有分布偏移。作者因此又加了两个轻量校准支路：一个偏 weight-side，用来平滑因跨模态偏移而被放大的权重量化误差；另一个偏 activation-side，主要补偿文本 token 在量化后的偏差。这里的设计取舍很明确：不追求复杂的联合优化，而是在主干量化路径之外加小型可学习补偿，保持 PTQ 的训练成本和部署可行性。和传统 TPTQ 相比，区别不只是“多了一个校准模块”，而是先做通道级模态解耦，再对剩余共享部分做轻量修正，优化目标更贴合 VLM 的统计结构。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen2.5-VL-3B，在六个主流 benchmark 上评估多种量化配置，包括 W4A8、W4A4、W3A3、W3A2。最关键的结果是 W4A4 下几乎保住 FP16 水平：Qwen2.5-VL-7B 平均分 73.5，对比 FP16 的 74.3；Qwen2.5-VL-3B 为 69.6，对比 FP16 的 70.0。这个差距已经很小，说明方法确实把跨模态量化误差压到了较低水平。更有价值的是在更激进的 W3A3、W3A2 下仍然有效，说明收益不只是高位宽下的微调式修补，而是对低比特失真有更强鲁棒性。\n\n从消融逻辑看，论文的证据链主要有三层：一是通道分布分析，证明模态异质性集中在少量且错位的通道；二是 MOCD 单独带来主要收益，说明“先分流”比“共享变换硬适配”更对症；三是 ACC 在 main channels 上继续缩小残余误差，尤其对文本侧恢复更明显。若全文里给出各模块逐步增益，阅读时应重点看 W4A4 和 W3A3 两档，因为这两档最能体现结构性改进，而不是量化噪声被高位宽掩盖。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Fig.2，确认作者观察到的不是一般性的模态均值差异，而是通道级 outlier 错位。然后看 Method 里 MOCD 的通道划分标准，这部分决定方法是否只是启发式工程，还是有稳定统计依据。接着看 ACC merely different in aggregate, but differ in a channel-sparse and modality-misaligned way. A small subset of channels carries most modality-specific outliers, and the problematic channels for text are often not the same as those for vision. Existing transformation-based PTQ methods usually learn one shared transformation across both modalities, which forces a single quantization-friendly geometry onto conflicting activation statistics. The result is substantial degradation at low bit-widths, especially in W4A4 and below. The core problem here is how to explicitly decouple this channel-level cross-modal conflict without retraining the VLM.","importance":"Its main value is not in pretraining methodology directly, but in exposing a useful structural fact about unified multimodal models: modality heterogeneity is sparse, channelized, and unevenly distributed, rather than uniformly spread across the representation space. It has implications for multimodal backbone sharing, alignment losses, representation shaping after tokenization, and how much modality-specific structure remains inside supposedly unified hidden states. On the deployment side, the contribution is a solid PTQ advance for VLMs: instead of reusing LLM quantization recipes, it adapts the quantization path to multimodal internal statistics.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"$2c","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the channel visualizations, especially the figure showing text/vision activation distributions. In current practice, the number of tokens decoded per step is controlled by a confidence threshold, and quality degrades monotonically as more tokens are denoised per step. We introduce Multi-token Residual Prediction (MRP), a lightweight module that enables dependency-aware multi-token denoising within a single bac","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T11:40:39+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18817.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"解决 masked diffusion / block diffusion 语言模型在“每步解码更多 token”时质量单调下降的核心原因：同一步内多个 token 被同时解码，缺少它们之间的依赖建模；而如果为了依赖再跑一次 backbone，又会抵消并行带来的吞吐收益。论文要回答的是：能否在不增加 backbone 前向次数（或尽量少增加）的前提下，让 DLM 在单步内解码多个 token 仍保持依赖感知，从而改善质量-吞吐折中。","importance":"这是偏推理侧的工作，但对“非 AR 语言建模范式”的落地很关键：它利用 DLM 相邻去噪步 logits 高相似性的训练动态性质，把‘多步迭代’的一部分计算用轻量模块近似/投机，从而把 DLM 的并行潜力更接近可用区间。对预训练研究的启发点在于：DLM 的时间步之间存在可学习的低秩/小残差结构，可能影响未来的训练目标设计（例如显式约束相邻步一致性、或把残差预测纳入训练以减少推理步数）。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Multi-token Residual Prediction (MRP)。关键观察：在 masked diffusion 的相邻去噪步 t 与 t-1 之间，如果只解开少量 token，backbone 输出 logits 的变化很小；定义残差 δ_t = ℓ_{t-1} - ℓ_t，其 RMS 幅度显著小于 ℓ_t 本身，因此“预测残差”比“重新预测完整 logits”容易。\n实现上：一次 backbone 前向得到当前 logits ℓ_t 以及隐藏状态 h_t；MRP 模块从 h_t 直接预测下一步 logits 的残差 Δ（或等价的校正项），构造近似的 ℓ_{t-1} ≈ ℓ_t + MRP(h_t)。这样在同一次 backbone 前向里得到“下一步的近似 logits”，从而在一个 step 内可以多解一些 token，且更接近真正跑两次 backbone 的效果。\n推理提供两种模式：\n- direct decoding：直接用校正后的 logits 做多 token 解码，不做验证，追求更好的质量-速度曲线。\n- speculative decoding：把 MRP 当作轻量 drafter，提出候选；下一次 backbone 前向做验证，接受的 token 不引入质量损失（代价是需要设计接受/拒绝规则与对齐到 DLM 的去噪流程）。\n训练上使用“为残差量身定制的目标函数”（全文细节通常包括：用真实相邻步 logits 作为监督，最小化残差预测误差；以及可能的温度/归一化以匹配残差尺度），核心是让小模块学到跨步校正而不是重做 backbone。","experiments":"在 SDAR 系列 DLM（1.7B/4B/8B）与 GSM8K 等基准上做测量与验证。关键现象实验：相邻步 logits 残差的 RMS 明显小于 logits 本身；并且随着一次解码的 token 数 k 增大，残差幅度上升但仍呈可预测结构（文中用 k=1..16 的对比来支撑）。系统集成到 SGLang 推理引擎。主要结果：在 speculative（lossless）模式下，对 SDAR 模型最高 1.42× 无损加速；direct 模式给出更好的质量-吞吐折中（相对阈值解码，在相同吞吐下质量更好，或相同质量下更快）。摘要里未给出更细的绝对指标（如 exact match/困惑度变化），精读实验部分可补齐。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看残差测量与动机图：理解“为什么残差小”以及它随 k 的变化规律，这是方法成立的前提。\n- 再看 MRP 的训练目标与输入输出设计：它到底预测的是 logits 残差、log-prob 残差还是别的等价量；是否做了尺度归一化；以及模块容量多大。\n- 重点看两种推理算法的差别：direct 的误差如何影响质量；speculative 的接受准则如何保证无损，以及在 DLM 去噪循环里如何对齐“下一步”。\n- 最后看 SGLang 集成与端到端吞吐：确认加速来自减少 backbone 次数还是减少步数/更激进的 unmask 策略，并留意不同模型规模下收益是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In masked/block diffusion LMs, decoding more tokens per denoising step improves throughput but typically hurts quality because tokens revealed in the same step are predicted without modeling their mutual dependencies. In this work, we propose Mix-Quant, a simple and effective phase-aware quantization framework for fast agentic inference. We first investigate FP4 quantization ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20315.png","hf_upvotes":28,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agentic LLM 推理里的一个很具体但常被混在一起的问题：长上下文、多轮工具调用场景下，prefill 和 decode 的误差容忍度与算力瓶颈并不相同，若对整个推理过程统一做激进量化，往往会在复杂轨迹任务上明显掉点。论文要解决的是，能否只在真正算力吃紧、且对量化更宽容的 prefilling 阶段使用超低比特计算，同时保住 decoding 阶段的数值稳定性与最终任务成功率。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，而是推理效率论文；但它有两个值得记住的启发。第一，长上下文 agent workload 的主瓶颈常常不在 autoregressive decode，而在反复处理超长输入的 prefill，这和很多只盯 decode 吞吐的优化思路不同。第二，推理阶段并不是一个同质过程，prefill/decode 的最优精度配置可以不同，这种 phase-aware 设计对后续训练侧也有启发，例如是否可以在训练中区分 prompt encoding 与 target generation 的数值预算、缓存策略或蒸馏目标。贡献量级偏工程实用，核心价值在于把“分阶段量化”这件事用 agentic 长轨迹场景讲清楚，并给出较完整的实证。","technical_approach":"方法本身很直接：Mix-Quant 在 prefilling 阶段使用 NVFP4 的 weight-and-activation quantization，在 decoding 阶段保留 BF16。论文的判断依据有两层。其一，prefill 是并行处理固定输入序列，主要受矩阵乘计算量约束；decode 是逐 token 自回归生成，更偏 memory-bound，同时每一步输出都会反馈到后续输入，因此量化误差会沿轨迹累积。其二，agentic 场景的输入往往包含大量历史对话、工具返回、检索片段和环境状态，冗余高，prefill 对低精度更不敏感。实现上，作者依赖 Blackwell 上原生支持的 NVFP4 microscaling FP4 格式，让低比特 matmul 真正落到硬件吞吐上，而不是只做 weight-only 压缩。和常见 INT4 weight-only PTQ 相比，它能加速 compute-bound prefill；和全程 W/A 低比特相比，它避免了 decode 端误差滚雪球。论文的创新不在新量化算法，而在把推理阶段解耦，并把硬件格式、误差传播机制和 agent 工作负载特征对齐。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类任务：长上下文 benchmark 与多轮 agent benchmark，并在多种 agentic base model 上测试。摘要与正文给出的主结论比较稳定：相较 BF16，Mix-Quant 在不同 batch size 和 sequence length 下可取得约 2–3× 的 prefill 加速；相较全程 NVFP4 量化，它能在大多数长轨迹任务上显著更好地保留最终性能。论文强调的不是单点峰值，而是效率—质量折中：uniform NVFP4 虽然更激进，但在复杂 agent 任务上成功率下降更明显；Mix-Quant 则把损失主要限制在可接受范围内。值得注意的是，作者把 prefilling 识别为主耗时阶段这一点，是通过 agent workflow 的输入输出长度比和多轮交互累积成本来支撑的，而不是只看单轮生成。若精读正文，建议重点核对不同上下文长度下的 latency breakdown、不同模型上的成功率变化，以及是否报告了 end-to-end wall-clock 而不仅是 kernel-level prefill speedup。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 里对 agent workload 的拆解，再看方法部分关于“为什么 prefill 能量化、decode 不宜激进量化”的误差传播解释，这两处已经覆盖了主要思想。随后直接看实验总表和 latency/success-rate 对比图，确认三件事：一是 prefill 在 agent 场景里是否真是主瓶颈；二是 uniform NVFP4 的性能损失有多大；三是 Mix-Quant 的收益主要来自哪里。预计 15–20 分钟可以抓住核心。若只关心是否值得复现，重点看不同 sequence length、batch size 下的 speedup 曲线，以及多轮 agent benchmark 上的任务成功率。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific inference issue in agentic LLM workloads: prefilling and decoding have different bottlenecks and very different tolerance to quantization noise, yet many deployment pipelines still apply one uniform precision policy to both. In long-context, multi-turn agent settings, this often hurts task success because aggressive low-bit quantization saves compute in prefill but introduces compounding errors during autoregressive decoding. The question here is whether one can quantize only the phase that is both expensive and relatively redundant, while keeping the fragile phase precise.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper; it is an inference-efficiency paper. Still, it carries two useful lessons for foundation-model researchers. First, in realistic agent workloads the dominant cost may shift from token-by-token decoding to repeated long-context prefilling, which changes where optimization effort should go. Second, inference is not a homogeneous process: different phases justify different numerical budgets. That phase-aware view may later matter for training-time system design as well, such as prompt/target asymmetry, cache policies, or distillation objectives. The contribution is mainly practical and systems-oriented rather than algorithmically deep, but it makes the trade-off unusually explicit.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"The evaluation covers both long-context benchmarks and multi-turn agent benchmarks, using several agent-oriented base models. The main empirical claim is consistent across the paper: Mix-Quant delivers roughly 2–3× prefill speedup over BF16 across different sequence lengths and batch sizes, while preserving task performance much better than uniform NVFP4 quantization. The key comparison is not peak speed alone but the efficiency-quality frontier. Full NVFP4 is more aggressive, yet it degrades success rates more noticeably on long-horizon agent tasks; Mix-Quant recovers much of that loss by keeping decode precise. A careful read should verify whether the paper reports end-to-end wall-clock gains in addition to kernel-level prefill speedups, and how the latency breakdown changes as context length grows.","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction’s decomposition of agent workloads, then read the method section that explains why prefill is quantization-tolerant while decode is not. Those two parts contain nearly all of the paper’s real insight. After that, jump to the main tables and latency-versus-performance figures. Check three things: whether prefill is indeed the dominant bottleneck in their workloads, how much quality is lost under uniform NVFP4, and how much of Mix-Quant’s gain survives in end-to-end settings. A 15–20 minute skim is enough to capture the contribution. Difficulty is controlled through structural factors such as constraint tightness, resource scarcity, objective conflicts, subtask dependencies, and global coordination requirements, rather than only through superficial proxies such as prompt length. Each instance is paired with automatic verification rules that check constraint satisfaction and objective quality. Its value lies in a structured empirical map: which skills transfer well (language and instruction following) and which do not (mathematical reasoning), which merging families tend to be robust (classic task-vector methods), and how costly hyperparameter tuning can be managed (low-dimensional but mildly multimodal landscapes where GP-based Bayesian optimization performs best). For pretraining-oriented readers, the main signal is that cross-modal capability emergence via parameter-space composition is plausible, but bottlenecks likely come from representation/interface mismatch—especially for reasoning-heavy skills—rather than mere optimization difficulty.","technical_approach":"The paper is organized as a systematic study rather than a new algorithm.\n\n- Scenarios: evaluates three injection settings—language ability, mathematical reasoning, and instruction following—using multiple benchmarks.\n- Methods: compares nine merging approaches grouped into (i) classic task-vector merging (Task Arithmetic and variants like TIES, DARE, with tunable merge coefficient λ and sometimes a density/sparsity hyperparameter), (ii) data-aware merging (e.g., Fisher-weighted merging, RegMean-style representation alignment), and (iii) tuning-free methods (highlighting NaN as a strong baseline).\n- Hyperparameters: analyzes the hyperparameter landscape for classic methods and compares optimizers, concluding GP-based Bayesian optimization is most effective, while local directional search is more prone to local optima.","experiments":"Across three scenarios and six benchmarks, the study reports:\n- Scenario trend: language and instruction-following skills are transferred more successfully than mathematical reasoning.\n- Method trend: classic merging methods are consistently strong; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T17:58:40+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20177.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个在 VLM 后训练里很常见、但经常被推理训练掩盖的问题：模型在视觉任务上做不好，很多时候不是 reasoning 不够长，而是第一步 perception 就错了。作者把视觉能力拆成 visual perception、visual reasoning、textual reasoning 三个相对独立的维度，核心问题是：如果把这三种能力分开建模、分阶段训练，尤其先把 perception 打牢，是否能比当前把各种数据混在一起、主要强化长 CoT 的做法更有效。","importance":"虽然这是 VLM post-training 论文，但对基座研究的启发不小，尤其在“能力拆分”和“训练顺序”上。它给出的结论很明确：视觉感知不是预训练后天然足够的底座能力，也不是靠更长推理就能补回来的；它需要专门数据、专门目标，而且训练顺序会显著影响最终效果。这个结论可以外推到更广义的多模态预训练和后训练接口：当模型能力由多个异质子能力组成时，把所有信号混合优化未必最好，按 capability dimension 做 curriculum 可能比按样本难度排序更关键。对纯文本 LLM 也有映射意义，比如 retrieval、tool use、long-context grounding 这类能力是否也应先单独固化再做复杂推理。","technical_approach":"技术方案的核心是 staged post-training pipeline。作者构造了三套互不重叠的数据，分别对应 visual perception、textual reasoning、visual reasoning，然后按阶段依次训练，而不是把所有数据 merge 后统一采样。这里最关键的是第一阶段 visual perception。作者认为它的目标不是生成漂亮 caption，而是让模型稳定识别细粒度视觉实体、属性和空间关系，并把这些信息正确映射到文本表征中。\n\n为此，他们从 DOCCI 这类细粒度图像-描述数据出发，把 image-caption 对转成 perception-focused QA 数据，而不是直接做 caption SFT。更进一步，作者比较了 caption-based SFT 和 RLVR。RLVR 指 reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards，奖励来自可验证答案是否正确。论文的判断是，caption SFT 往往给模型施加 token-level、off-policy 的模仿信号，容易把模型往数据分布较窄、甚至低于预训练语料质量的方向拉；而 RL 让模型在自身生成轨迹上更新，更适合学习“看清楚再回答”这种结果可验证的感知能力。\n\n第二阶段是 textual reasoning，目的是补足纯文本链式推理能力，避免把视觉错误和文本推理错误混在一起。第三阶段才是 visual reasoning，把前两阶段学到的感知和文本推理结合到图像条件下的复杂任务中。作者还系统比较了 staged training 与 one-stage merged training，以及不同阶段顺序。结论是顺序不能乱，perception 必须先于 visual reasoning 固化，否则后续训练会在错误视觉表征上叠加更长的错误推理。\n\n另一个有意思的点是作者把这种做法概括为 capability-dimension curriculum learning。它不是按 easy-to-hard 排样本，而是按能力维度安排优化路径。全文还进一步验证了它和 difficulty-based curriculum 是互补的，可以叠加。","experiments":"实验最有说服力的部分是作者先做了错误归因。用 Claude-Haiku-4.5 分析 Qwen3-VL-8B 在 3 个 visual math 数据集上的错误样本，发现 86.9% 的错误可归因于视觉感知阶段出错，而不是后续推理本身。这给后面的 staged design 提供了直接动机。\n\n性能上，加入 visual perception stage 后，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 在 WeMath 上比 base model 提升 7.43 个点；Qwen3-VL-8B 从 50.9% 提升到 56.1%。在 staged vs one-stage 对比中，staged-trained Qwen3-VL-8B 的 math reasoning accuracy 再提升 1.46 个点，同时 reasoning trace 长度缩短 20.8%，说明收益不是靠“想得更长”，而是前面看得更准。阶段顺序实验也很关键：如果打乱顺序，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的平均 visual math 表现会从 42.3% 掉到 37.7%，说明 perception 作为 scaffold 的说法不是修辞，而是有明显数值支撑。\n\n在训练目标对比上，用 SFT 替代 RL 做 visual perception 学习，会让 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 在 WeMath 上分别下降 8.1% 和 1.6%。这个结果支持作者关于 RLVR 更适合感知学习的判断。最终模型方面，staged-trained Qwen3-VL-8B 在 MathVista 上 75.9%，WeMath 上 56.1%，RealWorldQA 上 74.5%；与 OneThinker-8B 相比，在 WeMath 提升 1.5%，在 RealWorldQA 提升 3.0%。此外，capability-based staging 与 difficulty-based ordering 结合后，相比 merged training 还能带来 4.43% 的提升，说明两种 curriculum 维度确实可叠加。","reading_guide":"这篇虽然标成 skim，但如果你关心多模态预训练和 post-train 接口，建议接近精读。先看 Introduction 里的错误分析动机，尤其 86.9% perception error 这个归因是全文立论基础。然后重点看 staged pipeline 和数据构造，弄清 visual perception 数据到底长什么样，和普通 caption SFT 有什么差别。接着看 4.3 和 4.4：一个讲 staged vs merged 以及顺序效应，一个讲 RLVR vs SFT，这两部分最能体现作者的核心判断。最后看 capability-dimension curriculum 的实验，判断这个概念是否能迁移到别的模态或纯文本场景。若时间有限，优先看 Figure 1、Figure 2、主结果表、阶段顺序实验和 RL 替代 SFT 的消融。预计 45-60 分钟能抓住主要贡献。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a common but often misdiagnosed issue in VLM post-training: many failures on visual tasks come from incorrect perception, not insufficient reasoning depth. The authors decompose VLM capability into three parts—visual perception, textual reasoning, and visual reasoning—and ask whether training them separately, in the right order, works better than the current practice of mixing all data and emphasizing long chain-of-thought reasoning.","importance":"Although this is framed as a VLM post-training study, the implications are broader for foundation-model research. The main message is clear: visual perception is not a solved byproduct of pretraining, and it cannot be reliably repaired by asking the model to reason longer. It needs dedicated data, a suitable training signal, and the right optimization order. That matters for multimodal pretraining and for the pretrain/post-train interface more generally. The paper also offers a useful abstraction—capability-dimension curriculum learning—which may transfer beyond vision to other heterogeneous capabilities such as retrieval, grounding, or tool use.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"This is labeled skim, but it is worth reading more carefully if you care about multimodal pretraining or the post-train interface. Existing video QA benchmarks often test coarse scene understanding or sparse temporal reasoning, but they do not sufficiently probe subtle human motion, interactions, spatial reference, and temporal grounding in long videos.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic. The benchmark shows that current VLMs are not uniformly weak on video: they handle object manipulation much better than human movement and interaction. This distinction matters for multimodal pretraining, because coarse video captions or sparse QA supervision are unlikely to teach robust person-level grounding, subject disambiguation, and temporally precise action understanding.","technical_approach":"FineBench is a dense multiple-choice VQA benchmark built from AVA v2.2 annotations. It contains 199,420 QA pairs over 64 long-form videos, with about 3,116 questions and 785 linked keyframes per video. The benchmark covers Person Movement, Person Interaction, and Object Manipulation, with explicit spatial and temporal grounding. Its design choice is to increase annotation density per long video rather than simply increasing the number of clips. The paper also introduces FineAgent, a modular enhancement pipeline. A Localizer supplies bounding boxes to reduce subject ambiguity, and a Descriptor produces frame-level summaries to enrich semantic context. This is an inference-time augmentation around existing VLMs, not a new pretraining recipe.","experiments":"The evaluation covers both proprietary and open-source VLMs. The key result is a large gap across action types: models score in the high 80s on Object Manipulation, but often fall into the 50s and 60s on Person Movement and Person Interaction. The best proprietary model reaches around 77% accuracy, leaving clear headroom. Error analysis shows a marked drop as the number of people in the scene increases, pointing to persistent failures in spatial reasoning and subject disambiguation. FineAgent improves several open-source VLMs by making localization and frame-level description explicit, although the provided text does not expose the full per-model gain table.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. Read Table 1 to understand how FineBench differs from VideoMME, EgoSchema, MVBench, and TemporalBench. Then read Section 3.3 and Table 3 for the action-type breakdown. Section 3.4 and Figure 3 are the most useful diagnostic parts, especially the analysis of multi-person degradation. paper studies a safety gap in multi-image MLLMs. Most multimodal jailbreak methods and safety evaluations are designed around a single image, while deployed models increasingly accept multiple images in one query. When harmful intent is distributed across images, the model must integrate partial visual evidence and infer the global request. Current safety mechanisms can fail under this composition.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to multimodal alignment and safety data design. The main signal is that multi-image input is not just a larger visual context window. It creates composition risks across visual tokens, rendered text, auxiliary tasks, and user intent. For VLM pretraining or instruction tuning, the useful question is whether the training mix and safety supervision cover multi-image composition, not only single-image OCR, single-image VQA, or isolated refusal examples.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework, DMN, is a single-turn black-box multi-image jailbreak setup with three components. Distributed Instruction splits the request across multiple typographic images, so each image carries only local information and the target model must reconstruct the full intent across the image sequence. Multimodal Evidence adds textual and visual evidence framed as realistic case material, increasing contextual detail and reducing direct rejection during image generation. Number Chain Task adds an auxiliary visual reasoning task over the image sequence, encouraging the model to follow task instructions while its safety mechanism must still track the global intent. The key idea is to decompose intent recognition, evidence integration, and task following across separate visual fragments, then test whether safety alignment remains consistent under that composition.","experiments":"The experiments cover three datasets and ten MLLMs. The authors report high attack success rates for DMN, including over 90% on GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Pro, and Claude Sonnet 4, and persistence under several jailbreak defenses. The multimodal evidence component also increases the level of detail in harmful responses. These results depend on the judge, harmfulness labeling protocol, and exact model versions, so they are best read as a safety evaluation signal rather than a stable capability measurement.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. The key issue is cross-modal propagation: a textual trigger can first cause a poisoned image to be generated, and the generated image tokens can then remain in the autoregressive context and trigger a malicious textual continuation.","importance":"The work is relevant to native multimodal pretraining because the vulnerability is tied to the unified modeling setup. A shared discrete token space and a single autoregressive objective make multimodal generation flexible, but they also allow poisoned associations to transfer across modalities. This is not just a downstream safety problem. It affects data curation, image-text pair construction, generation order, and checkpoint provenance for unified text-image models.","technical_approach":"The paper proposes a backdoor attack for unified autoregressive models. In the black-box data poisoning setting, the attacker manipulates a small subset of image-text pairs without accessing model weights. The core mechanism is to create overlapping poisoned pairs: a textual trigger is associated with a poisoned image, and that poisoned image is associated with a poisoned text. During autoregressive generation, the intermediate image output becomes part of the context and activates the next malicious association. In the white-box setting, the paper introduces a logit-level alignment attack, using teacher-guided optimization to embed the trigger-target associations into model parameters. As a defense, it evaluates bidirectional training on overlapping image-text pairs, training both text-to-image and image-to-text directions to weaken coherent trigger-target chains.","experiments":"The experiments cover both text-initiated and image-initiated triggers. The central result is that poisoning about 1% of the training samples is sufficient in the black-box setting to create a transitive link from a textual trigger to a poisoned image and then to a poisoned text. The triggers can be inconspicuous characters or common words, which makes simple trigger filtering difficult. The white-box logit alignment attack shows that similar associations can be implanted through parameter optimization rather than data poisoning. The proposed bidirectional training defense substantially lowers attack success while preserving normal model utility. Exact attack success rates are not available in the provided excerpt and should be read from the experimental tables.","reading_guide":"A 20 to 30 minute skim should focus on the threat model, the construction of overlapping poisoned pairs, and the defense section. concepts while preserving generation quality on unrelated prompts.","importance":"The connection to LLM pretraining is indirect, but the work is relevant to post-training and safety optimization for generative models. The useful idea is to replace paired supervised erasure data with verifiable rewards: one reward removes the target concept, and another protects the non-target distribution. This is close in spirit to RL-based alignment for language models, where the reward design determines whether the model learns the intended constraint or only exploits the evaluator.","technical_approach":"FlowErase-RL builds on Flow-GRPO. A flow-matching sampler is treated as a sequential policy over velocity predictions. For a prompt, the model samples a group of trajectories, scores the final images, computes group-normalized advantages, and assigns the same trajectory-level advantage to the per-timestep velocity predictions. The method uses a dynamic dual-path reward. The Concept Erasure reward is built from prompt pairs with and without the target concept, pushing the model away from the concept to be erased. The Non-target Space reward uses a retain set to preserve image quality and semantic fidelity for unrelated prompts. The two reward paths are switched by epoch, with reward strength and weighting adjusted according to observed erasure behavior. Compared with inference-time interventions, this changes the model policy directly. Compared with SFT-based erasure, it reduces reliance on precisely aligned erasure data and is better matched to modern flow-transformer systems such as FLUX.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method on multiple target concepts, mainly using FLUX-style flow-matching models. The reported results show stronger concept suppression than prior erasure methods while maintaining non-target generation quality. That alignment is challenging because the reward is defined for clean output images, but the alignment procedure requires value function estimates at noisy intermediate latents. Existing methods resort to Tweedie-style or Monte Carlo approximations, trading off estimator bias against computational cost: Tweedie estimates are efficient but bias","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T13:02:50+00:00","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19804.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 diffusion/flow 模型对齐中的一个老问题：奖励定义在干净图像 x0 上，但训练时或推理时真正需要评估的是噪声中间态 zt 的“未来回报”，也就是 value function。现有做法要么用 Tweedie 近似，从 zt 先估计一个干净样本再打分；要么做 Monte Carlo rollout，从 zt 采样多条去噪轨迹再平均奖励。前者便宜但高噪声时偏差大，后者更准但成本高。作者要解决的核心问题是：能不能不从头大规模训练 noisy-latent value model，而是把已经很强的像素空间 reward model 迁移到 noisy latent 上，并且迁移成本足够低、泛化足够好。","importance":"对预训练研究者来说，这篇工作的意义不在语言模型本身，而在“如何把 foundation-scale 监督信号迁移到另一种表示空间”这个思路。它给出的答案是 model stitching：冻结一段擅长处理 noisy latent 的 diffusion backbone，当作 head；截取一段已经学会奖励预测的 reward model，当作 tail；在两者之间学一个很轻的 stitching layer，把 latent 表征接到 reward 表征空间。这个思路有两点值得注意。第一，它避免了为每个新 backbone、每个新 reward 都重新训练一个完整 value model，工程上更可持续。第二，它说明 noisy latent value estimation 未必需要独立从零学一遍语义与偏好，只要找到表征兼容的 stitch point，就能复用 foundation reward model 的能力。这个想法对多模态预训练、跨模型蒸馏、甚至 pretrain/post-train 接口设计都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"摘要已明确作者在多个 backbone 上验证，包括 SD 3.5 Medium、SD 3.5 Large 等，并且目标是同时覆盖训练时对齐和推理时引导两种使用方式。虽然给出的全文片段还没展开全部表格，但从引言和方法动机可以确定实验重点有三类。\n\n第一类是 value estimation 质量。作者会把 StitchVM 与 Tweedie 近似、MC 估计、以及已有 learned value model 做比较，关注不同噪声水平下的偏差、方差和总体预测精度。这里最关键的不是单一平均分，而是高噪声区间是否还能稳定预测，因为 Tweedie 的主要问题正是在高噪声阶段偏差大。\n\n第二类是 alignment 效果。StitchVM 作为 value model 可以接入训练时方法或推理时 guidance，因此实验通常会看 prompt fidelity、aesthetic preference 或其他 reward-aligned 指标，验证更准的 noisy-latent value 是否真的转化为更好的生成质量。若论文完整实验成立，预期会展示：在相近或更低计算成本下，StitchVM 相比 Tweedie/MC 近似能带来更好的 reward 优化结果，或者在相近效果下显著降低推理开销。\n\n第三类是泛化与可迁移性。因为作者的主张是“低成本把 pretrained reward model 缝到不同 diffusion backbone 上”，所以应重点看：\n- 同一个 reward tail 能否适配多个 diffusion backbone；\n- 更换 reward model 时是否仍只需短程 finetune；\n- stitch point 的选择是否稳定；\n- 线性初始化与后续 finetune 各自贡献多大。\n\n若阅读全文，建议重点找这些消融：不同 stitch layer 位置、冻结/解冻范围、是否使用 closed-form 初始化、不同噪声时间步上的误差曲线、以及与 MC rollout 在算力-精度上的 trade-off。对这类论文，具体数字当然重要，但更重要的是看它是否真的把“高质量 reward 能力”迁移到了 noisy latent，而不是只在某个窄设置下有效。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着明确问题去读：它到底是在做一个更便宜的 reward proxy，还是在做一个真正可泛化的 noisy-latent value model。阅读顺序建议如下。\n\n先看引言和方法总图，确认 StitchVM 的 head-tail-stitch 三段结构，以及它与 Tweedie、MC、直接 learned value model 的差别。这里要抓住一句话：作者不是从零训练 value model，而是在做表示空间迁移。\n\n然后重点看 stitch point 选择和初始化方法。若这一部分讲得扎实，这篇工作的可信度会高很多；若只是经验性试层，方法价值会下降。要特别注意作者如何定义“representation compatibility”，以及为什么线性映射足够作为起点。\n\n接着看主实验中的两类图表：value prediction 误差随噪声水平变化的曲线，以及 alignment 效果与计算成本的对比。前者决定它是否真的优于 Tweedie，后者决定它是否比 MC 更有实际意义。\n\n最后看消融。最值得看的通常是：不同 backbone、不同 reward tail、不同 stitch 深度、冻结策略、以及少量 finetune 步数对结果的影响。\n\n预计 35 到 50 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。如果只关心方法思想，看方法图、stitch point 分析和主结果表即可；若考虑把类似思路迁移到多模态或语言模型，需要细看表征对齐与训练稳定性部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central bottleneck in diffusion alignment: rewards are defined on clean outputs x0, but alignment methods need value estimates for noisy intermediate latents zt. Existing approximations are unsatisfactory. Tweedie-style estimates are cheap but biased, especially at high noise, while Monte Carlo rollouts are more faithful but expensive. The core question is whether one can obtain a strong noisy-latent value model without training it from scratch at foundation scale for every new diffusion backbone and every new reward model.","importance":"For pretraining-oriented readers, the interesting part is the transfer mechanism, not diffusion alignment alone. The paper argues that a foundation-scale reward model does not need to be relearned in the noisy latent domain from scratch. Instead, its capability can be transplanted through model stitching: use a frozen diffusion backbone segment as a noisy-latent encoder, keep the upper part of a pretrained reward model as a semantic/reward predictor, and learn only a lightweight bridge between them. This is a useful design pattern for cross-representation transfer. ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-17T05:22:27+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18891.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是 reasoning model unlearning 评测中的测量有效性问题。现有做法常把输出切成 thinking trace 和 final answer 两段：如果 answer 里不再出现被遗忘内容，但 thinking trace 里仍然出现，就把这个 gap 解读为“权重仍然记得，只是最终答案被抑制”。作者要验证的是：这个 parser-split bypass gap 是否真的能作为 weight-level memorization 的证据。结论是否定的。至少在他们测试的 DeepSeek-R1 distilled 模型、LoRA 注入的虚构作者 canary、NPO unlearning 设定下，这个 gap 很容易被 decode-time prefill、格式漂移和 parser 行为改变，不能自动解释为隐藏记忆通道。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train 接口的影响主要在评测方法，而不是提出新的 unlearning 算法。reasoning trace 不是权重记忆的直接读数。尤其在 trace-supervised 模型里，scratchpad 往往承载模板、格式、局部 continuation bias 和训练时的表面模式。如果 unlearning loss 只作用在 answer span，而评测又把 trace 和 answer 简单按标签切开，得到的 leakage gap 可能混入大量 decoding artifact。对做 memorization、数据删除、continual pretrain、reasoning distillation 的研究者来说，这个提醒很具体：不能只看生成文本中某个区域是否包含敏感字符串，还要区分 teacher-forced token probability、free generation、prefill sensitivity 和格式解析失败。贡献量级是一个扎实的 measurement correction：它没有给出通用隐私风险上界，但把一个常见评测读法的证据链拆开了。","technical_approach":"实验围绕一个“看起来合理但有结构性偏差”的评测管线展开。第一步，用 LoRA 把 60 个虚构作者 bio 记入 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B，让模型在 <think> 段复述目标 bio，再在 answer 段给出结果。第二步，用 NPO 做 answer-masked unlearning，forgetting loss 只作用在 closing </think> 之后的 answer span，thinking trace 中的 bio 模板不被梯度直接更新。第三步，评测时按 </think> 解析输出，分别对子串匹配 trace leakage 和 answer leakage。这个设计天然容易产生 positive bypass gap：trace 里的模板没有被忘却目标直接打到，answer 被压下去以后，parser-split 指标就会显示 trace 仍然泄漏。作者的关键做法是加入 fixed-weight probe，而不是继续调模型。一个 probe 是 inference-time prefill swap：在相同权重下，把模型自生成的 trace 换成短的 non-canary prefill，比较 answer continuation 是否稳定。另一个 probe 是 teacher-forced continuation scoring：在相同六 token canary head 条件下，不靠 greedy decoding 和 parser，而是直接看目标 continuation 的 token-level top-1 匹配。这里的 canary head 很重要：指标测的是给定前六个 token 后的 continuation 能力，不是无条件 free recall。作者还在 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 上复现实验，用格式漂移展示 parser-split 指标可以直接反号。","experiments":"核心数字集中在几个互相校验的现象上。Qwen-7B 设定中，LoRA 记忆后 out_acc 为 1.00；NPO 后 answer 侧 out_acc 降到 0.60，但 thinking trace 侧 thk_leak 仍为 0.83，parser-split gap Δ=+0.23。表面看这支持“thinking bypass”。但作者发现，在这些 bypass case 中，86% 的 answer span 只是问题前缀的逐字重复，没有真正 continuation，而 trace 中保留训练 bio。这说明 answer 侧下降和 trace 侧保留并不等价于权重中存在一个可直接读取的隐藏通道。固定权重 prefill swap 进一步打破这种读法：短 non-canary prefill 可把 out_acc 从 0.60 降到 0.37；bio-style 和 meta-style prefill 落在相近水平；empty prefill 降到 0.20。另一个 seed 中，bypass gap 缩小但未消失，prefill swap 的方向反过来，能把 answer rate 推到接近 ceiling。这说明 gap 对 decoding context 很敏感。teacher-forced probe 给出相反证据：post-NPO 后，在同样六 token canary head 条件下，top-1 continuation match 在不同 prefill 下仍保持 0.90–1.00。也就是说，greedy substring 的下降没有转化为匹配的 token-level scoring 下降。Llama-8B 结果更直接：在 K=1600 时 parser-split metric 变成 -0.92，但 out_acc 仍为 1.00，teacher-forced top-1 ≥0.998。原因不是 unlearning 成功，而是至少 60% probe 中 bio 模板漂移到 <think> 标签外，parser 得到空 trace。作者还指出，prefill intervention 也会扰动普通 QA probe，因此它不能单独定位机制；它能排除的是“positive parser-split gap 自带 hidden-channel 证据”这一读法。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中对 canonical parser-split audit 的拆解，尤其是 trace-supervised bio restatement、answer-masked NPO、parser-split substring matching 三者如何共同制造测量偏差。§4.1 看 bypass pattern 的原始成立条件和 86% case 诊断；§4.2 看 fixed-weight prefill swap，因为这是整篇工作的关键反事实；§4.3 看 teacher-forced continuation scoring，它给出比生成字符串更稳定的记忆读数；§4.4 看 Llama-8B 的 format drift，能快速理解 parser 失败为什么会让指标反号。Appendix D 值得看具体 bypass case，那里能看到 answer 只是 prefix echo，而 trace 承载 bio 模板。若只关心方法迁移，重点是把“paired fixed-prefill arm + teacher-forced continuation scoring”作为一个组合 audit，而不是把 trace leakage 单独当成权重记忆证据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work audits a measurement claim in unlearning evaluations for reasoning models. A common protocol splits the generated output into a thinking trace and a final answer. If the answer no longer contains the forgotten content but the trace still does, the gap is often read as evidence that the model weights still retain the content through a hidden reasoning channel. The paper tests whether that inference is valid. In the tested setting—DeepSeek-R1 distilled models, LoRA-memorized fictional-author canaries, and NPO unlearning—the answer is no. The parser-split bypass gap is highly sensitive to decoding context, prefills, and formatting drift, so it is not self-identifying evidence of weight-level memorization.","importance":"The contribution is mainly about evaluation validity, not a new unlearning method. It matters for pretraining and post-training research because reasoning traces are not transparent readouts of stored knowledge. In trace-supervised models, the scratchpad can encode templates, local continuation biases, and formatting habits. If the forgetting loss is applied only to the answer span while the evaluation parser separately scores the trace and answer, the resulting leakage gap can mix true retention with decoding artifacts. For researchers working on memorization, data deletion, continual pretraining, and reasoning distillation, the practical lesson is clear: substring leakage in a generated trace must be separated from teacher-forced likelihood, free generation behavior, prefill sensitivity, and parser failure. The paper provides a useful correction to a plausible but under-validated evaluation practice.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"$36","reading_guide":"Read the introduction carefully for the decomposition of the audit pipeline: trace-supervised biography restatement, answer-masked NPO, and parser-split substring matching. Section 4.1 is the place to inspect how the apparent bypass pattern is produced. Section 4.2 contains the central fixed-weight prefill counterfactual. Section 4.3 is important because teacher-forced continuation scoring provides a more stable view of memorized continuation than greedy generation. Section 4.4 is worth reading for the Llama-8B formatting failure, which shows how a parser can flip the metric’s sign without real unlearning. Appendix D is useful for concrete examples where the answer is only a prefix echo and the trace carries the biography template. The main reusable idea is to treat paired fixed-prefill decoding and teacher-forced scoring as one audit design rather than relying on trace leakage alone.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"修正了推理模型遗忘评估的常见认知偏差，做对齐、遗忘相关实验时可直接复用其金丝雀审计方案","en":"Corrects common misconceptions in reasoning model unlearning evaluation, its canary audit method can be directly reused for alignment and unlearning experiments"},"metadata":{"institutions":["DeepSeek"],"authors":["Yanhang Li","Zhichao Fan","Zexin Zhuang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["unlearning","reasoning_trace","memorization_audit","LLM_alignment","canary_test"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18891","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.18891","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.19461","title":"Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning","abstract":"On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T07:13:00+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19461.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练推理大模型时，GRPO等主流在策略RL方法会出现模式崩溃问题：一旦发现单个高奖励解题轨迹，策略会快速将概率质量集中到该轨迹，抑制其他可行解题策略的探索，导致过早收敛、无法发现更优解、推理鲁棒性和泛化性下降。该问题的根源是现有方法隐式最小化反向KL散度的模式寻优特性，而非实现缺陷。","importance":"首次系统定位了RLVR训练推理大模型时模式崩溃的核心成因，提出了可直接落地的解决方案，打破了当前推理强化学习追求高通过率但牺牲解多样性的瓶颈；证明了保留解多样性的训练目标可以提升推理质量、鲁棒性和跨领域迁移能力，为推理基座的RL训练范式提供了新的优化方向；提出的MM-NP-Bench弥补了现有推理评测缺乏探索行为衡量能力的缺陷，为研究探索与利用的平衡提供了标准测试环境。","technical_approach":"从KL散度的特性出发，对比反向KL的模式寻优特性和前向KL的模式覆盖特性，指出前向KL最小化是避免模式崩溃的理论最优解，但直接优化前向KL需要对全局轨迹的奖励加权分布采样，计算不可行。据此提出DMPO算法，在组层面近似前向KL最小化：对每个问题采样的G条轨迹组，基于奖励构建玻尔兹曼分布作为目标分布，在GRPO的标准目标基础上增加一个分布匹配正则项，将策略分布对齐到该组内目标分布。该方法与GRPO现有的组内优势归一化逻辑天然兼容，仅需修改少量代码即可实现，不需要额外的模型或数据开销。","experiments":"评测基准包括两类：一是构建了MM-NP-Bench多模态组合优化评测集，覆盖路由、覆盖、划分、子结构、约束五大类共10项NP难问题，包含参数化生成器、规则验证器和启发式求解器基线，采用成功率（SR）和质量比（QR）双指标衡量，其中QR用于量化解的最优程度、识别模式崩溃（高SR低QR即为模式崩溃）；二是数学推理和跨领域迁移任务。实验对比GRPO等5个强基线：在NP-Bench上，DMPO的质量比达到43.9%，对比GRPO的40.1%，相对提升9%；在MM-NP-Bench上，DMPO质量比43.1%，对比GRPO的38.4%，相对提升12%；数学推理任务准确率提升2.0%，跨领域任务准确率提升2.3%。消融实验验证了分布匹配正则项的贡献，以及组层面目标分布构建的有效性。","reading_guide":"精读第2章，重点理解反向KL导致模式崩溃的推导过程，以及DMPO组级前向KL近似的核心公式，这是全文的理论基础；重点关注第3章MM-NP-Bench的双指标设计逻辑，尤其是质量比QR的定义和评测意义，这一设计可以推广到所有需要衡量解多样性的推理评测；第4章的消融实验部分，需要仔细核对不同正则项权重对探索和性能的影响，以及不同任务下模式崩溃的具体表现形式；可以跳过第1章的背景介绍和第4章的基础基线对比部分。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"When training large reasoning models with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), mainstream on-policy RL methods such as GRPO suffer from mode collapse: once a single high-reward solution trajectory is discovered, the policy rapidly concentrates probability mass on this trajectory, suppressing exploration of alternative viable strategies. This leads to premature convergence, failure to discover better solutions, and degraded reasoning robustness and generalization. The root cause of this problem is the mode-seeking property of reverse KL divergence minimization implicitly adopted by existing methods, rather than implementation defects.","importance":"This work systematically identifies the core cause of mode collapse in RLVR training for reasoning models for the first time, and proposes a directly deployable solution. It breaks the current bottleneck in reasoning RL training where high pass rates are pursued at the cost of solution diversity. It also proves that diversity-preserving training objectives improve reasoning quality, robustness, and cross-domain transferability, providing a new optimization direction for RL training paradigms for reasoning base models. The proposed MM-NP-Bench fills the gap that existing reasoning benchmarks lack the ability to measure exploration behavior, providing a standard testbed for studying the trade-off between exploration and exploitation.","technical_approach":"$37","experiments":"$38","reading_guide":"Read Chapter 2 intensively, focusing on the derivation of how reverse KL leads to mode collapse and the core formula of DMPO's group-level forward KL approximation, which is the theoretical foundation of the entire work. Pay close attention to the dual-metric design logic of MM-NP-Bench in Chapter 3, especially the definition and evaluation significance of Quality Ratio (QR). This design can be extended to all reasoning benchmarks that need to measure solution diversity. In the ablation studies of Chapter 4, carefully check the impact of different regularization weights on exploration and performance, as well as the specific manifestations of mode collapse across different tasks. matrix-aware optimizer Muon outperforms AdamW in LLM pretraining, but its core uniform spectral whitening mechanism that pushes all singular values of the momentum matrix to 1 has fundamental limitations in two critical post-pretraining regimes: 1) Cross-modality Vision-Language-Action (VLA) training, where action module gradients are inherently low-rank with noise-dominated tail components, which Muon amplifies; 2) Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) post-training, where gradients have low SNR and attention head specialization inherited from pretraining needs to be preserved, making uniform whitening unstable. Existing low-rank Muon variants rely on fixed-rank truncation and extra SVD/random projection overhead, and cannot adapt to dynamic requirements across layers and training steps.","importance":"First systematically reveals the spectral limitations of Muon-style matrix optimizers in post-pretraining scenarios. The proposed Pion optimizer is a direct drop-in replacement for Muon with identical computational cost, delivering consistent gains in both multimodal and RL post-training. It provides core guidance for optimizer selection and design across the full LLM training pipeline: pretraining → multimodal adaptation → RL alignment.","technical_approach":"Proposes Pion (Spectral High-pass Optimization on Momentum). Leveraging the polynomial structure of Newton-Schulz (NS) iteration, it reformulates Muon's single-stage spectral whitening into a two-stage Promotion+Suppression polynomial map (high-pass NS): it first amplifies dominant singular values, then suppresses tail components, forming a soft high-pass filter that anchors informative leading singular values at 1 while driving noisy tails toward 0, with identical per-step computational cost as Muon. A per-head mode is further introduced, which splits attention projection matrices along the head dimension and runs high-pass NS iteration independently for each head, adapting to heterogeneous update scales across attention heads with no additional overhead.","experiments":"On VLA tasks with π0.5 backbone, Pion outperforms AdamW and Muon on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus datasets and real Franka robot setups with ℓ1 regression and flow-matching heads. On RLVR tasks with Qwen3-1.7B/4B using GRPO and GMPO algorithms on MATH and GSM8K, Pion also consistently outperforms AdamW and Muon while matching Muon's computational efficiency. Ablation studies verify the individual contributions of high-pass NS and per-head mode.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 for analysis of Muon's spectral defects, Section 3 for Pion's high-pass NS design, and Section 4 for VLA and RLVR experimental comparisons. Existing long-context RL methods often treat data construction as a matter of designing increasingly complex retrieval paths, leading to homogeneous task coverage and reward formulations that inadequately reflect practical long-context requirements. First, many existing datasets overemphasize synthetic retrieval paths, which narrows capability coverage. Second, heterogeneous rewards such as EM, F1, NDCG, and ROUGE-L interact poorly with standard GRPO, creating reward-scale mismatch and biased advantage estimates across tasks with different difficulty levels.","importance":"The contribution is mainly a post-training recipe, but it has clear implications for foundation-model training. Long-context capability is not only a matter of extending the context window during pretraining or mid-training. Models also need post-training signals that cover the ways long contexts are actually used: aggregation, ranking, summarization, structured reasoning, and multi-document analysis. The results suggest that task coverage and reward diversity can be a primary bottleneck in long-context RL, even at modest data scale.","technical_approach":"GoLongRL combines a capability-oriented RLVR dataset with a multitask optimization adjustment. The dataset contains 23K samples across 9 long-context task types. Around 14K samples are curated from open long-context corpora, while around 9K synthetic samples are generated from real source documents, including books, academic papers, and multi-turn dialogues. Each task keeps its natural metric as the reward instead of being forced into a single binary format. The optimization method, TMN-Reweight, has two components. Task-level mean normalization aligns reward scales across tasks while preserving prompt-level difficulty information. Difficulty-adaptive reweighting uses a smoothed pass rate to emphasize informative hard positives and reduce redundant gradients from easy prompts. The method keeps the model architecture unchanged and targets the advantage-estimation problem in heterogeneous long-context RL.","experiments":"Under the same vanilla GRPO setup, training on the GoLongRL dataset improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking from 53.0 to 62.2 average on long-context benchmarks. TMN-Reweight further raises the 4B average to 63.0. For Qwen3-30B-A3B, the average improves from 60.1 to 69.8. The 30B GoLongRL model reports 65.3 on DocMath, 55.1 on LongBench-V2, 74.5 on Frames, 81.6 on MRCR, 73.6 on CorpusQA, and 68.7 on LBV1-QA, with a 69.8 average. It also outperforms the corresponding QwenLong-L1.5 GRPO setting. General capabilities are reported as preserved or improved on evaluations such as MMLU-Pro, AIME24/25, and GPQA, with transfer to agentic memory and long-term dialogue memory benchmarks not used in training.","reading_guide":"A 30–40 minute skim is appropriate. Start with the task taxonomy and data pipeline, because the main claim depends on whether the 9 task types reflect real long-context usage rather than another retrieval-heavy mixture. Then read the TMN-Reweight formulation carefully: how task-level normalization differs from prompt-level normalization, and how pass-rate-based difficulty weights affect gradient allocation. In the experiments, prioritize controlled comparisons under the same base model and GRPO setup, especially against QwenLong-L1.5 data. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-11T09:22:39+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18799.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的不是普通的“答错题”，而是多轮 critic 交互里的“正确性转移”问题：模型第一轮本来答对了，收到用户质疑后反而改错；或者第一轮答错了，第二轮能否被有效纠正。作者把 scientific critic reasoning 明确建模为一个跨轮状态转移问题，而不是只看最终答案是否正确。全文里反复强调，单看 final accuracy 会把两类行为混在一起：有益修正（wrong→right）和有害迎合（right→wrong）都可能让最终统计看起来变化不大，但训练信号完全相反。这个问题在科学问答里更尖锐，因为答案依赖证据链和约束条件，模型需要在“该改时改、不该改时稳住”之间做细粒度判断。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接启发不在于提出了新的 base pretraining objective，而在于把多轮交互中的信用分配拆得更细，给 post-train 阶段提供了一种更接近真实使用场景的奖励设计。它的价值主要有三点。第一，它说明 final-answer reward 在交互任务里信息密度不够，容易把 correction 和 sycophancy 抵消掉，这对后续做 RLHF、critic training、self-correction 训练都很关键。第二，它把“稳定坚持正确答案”单独作为可优化行为，而不是默认只奖励修改，这对构建可靠 scientific assistant 有现实意义。第三，它展示了多阶段 rollout 的工程问题：一条样本要先生成 initial，再接 critic，再生成 revised answer，若仍用同步采样，长尾延迟会明显拖慢训练。量级上，这更像是一个扎实的 RL 训练框架改进，而不是改变大模型训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 ReCrit 的四象限转移奖励。对每个问题，策略先生成 Initial solution y0，再接收一条 critic feedback c，随后生成 Critic solution y1。一个 judge 分别判定 y0 和 y1 的正确性，得到二元标签后，把样本落到四个象限之一：Correction（错→对）、Sycophancy（对→错）、Robustness（对→对）、Boundary（错→错）。训练时不再只看 y1 是否正确，而是按象限赋不同权重：强奖励 Correction，强惩罚 Sycophancy，弱奖励 Robustness，弱惩罚 Boundary。这样做的原因很清楚：如果只奖励最终答对，模型可能通过“更愿意改口”同时增加 correction 和 sycophancy；而四象限奖励把这两种转移显式拆开，优化目标更可控。\n\n从全文描述看，作者还做了几层细化。其一，critic feedback 不是单一类型，而是随机采样 opposing、neutral、supportive 三类反馈，让模型学会在不同反馈强度下决定是否修正。其二，训练使用 group-normalized advantages，说明它更接近 GRPO 一类的组内相对优势更新，而不是纯标量回报 PPO。其三，作者提到 critic-stage weighting 和 finalization 等设计，意图是让奖励更聚焦第二轮决策，而不是被第一轮能力完全主导。其四，在系统层面引入 dynamic asynchronous rollout 和 tail-adaptive completion：完成 initial generation 的样本可以立刻进入 critic 阶段，不必等待整批最慢样本；对长尾样本做自适应截断或完成策略，减少多阶段 rollout 的等待气泡。和常规 SFT 相比，区别在于 SFT 只模仿目标答案，缺少跨轮 credit assignment；和 final-answer RL 相比，区别在于 ReCrit 优化的是“转移结构”而不是“终点标签”。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个封闭式科学推理 benchmark：ChemBench、TRQA、EarthSE，涉及化学、生物、地学；模型是 Qwen3.5-4B 和 Qwen3.5-9B 两个规模。主结果是 Critic accuracy，也就是收到 critic 反馈后的第二轮正确率。4B 模型平均从 38.15 提升到 51.49，提升 13.34 个点；9B 从 45.40 提升到 55.59，提升 10.19 个点。引言里还给了一个失败现象的基线例子：Qwen3-max 在 biology reasoning 上有 6.98% 的 initially correct answers 会在 critic 后变错，整体准确率从 51.16 降到 48.84，说明问题不是边角案例。\n\n消融部分是全文最有信息量的地方。作者指出 final-answer reward 单独使用时提升很有限，因为它无法区分有益修正和有害迎合。加入 transition-aware reward 后，净 gain 才明显上升。再往上叠加 calibrated quadrant weights、critic-stage weighting、finalization，性能继续改善。另一个重要观察是 SFT 往往同时提高 Correction 和 Sycophancy：模型更愿意改答案了，但不一定更会判断何时该改。ReCrit 则让两者分离，得到更高的 net beneficial transition。虽然给出的全文片段没有完整表格数值，但从作者总结看，收益主要来自 critic 阶段行为改善，而不是 initial answer 本身更强。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 里对问题的定义，尤其是为什么 final accuracy 不够，以及四象限划分的动机。第二，看 Method 中 reward 设计和 rollout pipeline，重点理解 judge 如何把两轮答案映射成转移标签，以及为什么要给 Robustness 低权重而不是和 Correction 同权。第三，看 ablation 表和 transition breakdown 图，那里最能看出 ReCrit 真正改善的是 correction/sycophancy 的平衡，而不是简单把第二轮答案都推向“更爱改”。\n\n如果时间有限，建议 20–30 分钟读完主文：先看 Figure 1/2、主结果表、消融表，再回读方法细节。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 judge 误差会怎样影响四象限奖励的稳定性；二是这种 transition-aware reward 能否迁移到更开放式的 reasoning 或通用对话，而不依赖封闭式可判分任务。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets an interaction failure that is different from ordinary wrong-answer behavior: an LLM may start with a correct scientific solution, receive user criticism, and then abandon the correct answer for an incorrect one. The authors frame this as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem. That distinction matters because two very different behaviors can look similar under a final-only metric: useful revision (wrong to right) and harmful sycophancy (right to wrong). In scientific reasoning, where answers depend on fragile evidence chains and domain constraints, the model must learn when to revise and when to hold its ground.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain-to-posttrain interface because it sharpens reward design for multi-turn reasoning. The main contribution is a better credit-assignment scheme for interactive correction. It shows that final-answer rewards are too coarse in critic settings, since they can hide cancellation between correction and sycophancy. It also treats robustness under criticism as a trainable behavior, not just a side effect. A second contribution is practical: multi-stage rollout is expensive, and the paper addresses the systems bottleneck directly. The overall impact is best viewed as a solid RL training framework improvement for reliable scientific assistants, rather than a change in the core language-modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$39","experiments":"$3a","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts. First, read the problem setup in the introduction, especially the argument for why final accuracy is insufficient in critic interaction. Second, read the method section around the four-quadrant reward and the rollout pipeline. The key question is why Robustness gets a weaker reward than Correction, and how the judge-induced labels drive policy updates. Detecting the onset of degradation early enough to stop reuse r","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T06:23:43+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19425.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决 RLVR 中 rollout 样本复用的停止时机问题：复用能省样本，但过度复用会造成策略偏移并引发训练退化。","importance":"它对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 与 pretrain 的接口有启发。RLVR 的主要成本在 rollout，样本复用如果可控，会降低推理型训练的 GPU 开销。更重要的是，它把训练崩溃定位到可监控的参数子结构，即 lm_head，而不是泛泛地用整体 KL 或 reward 曲线做滞后诊断。这类局部梯度信号也可能用于其他大模型训练阶段的稳定性监控。","technical_approach":"作者先观察到 Disproportionate Weight Divergence：样本复用导致性能退化时，lm_head 权重变化会突然增大，而中间层相对稳定。理论解释分两步。第一，复用带来的有害梯度更集中在 lm_head，中间层会受到下游 Jacobian 投影的衰减。第二，lm_head 梯度范数可以下界更新策略与行为策略之间的经验 Pearson chi-square divergence，因此它能作为策略偏移的即时信号。基于这个信号，作者提出 Dynamic Gradient Gating。做法很轻量：在每次复用更新时监控 lm_head 梯度范数，用动态 Z-score 检测异常跃升；一旦触发，就丢弃当前有害梯度，并结束该 rollout batch 的复用循环，避免 Adam 的权重和动量状态被污染。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen 与 Llama 系列 6 个模型，规模从 1.5B 到 8B；任务包括数学推理、WebShop、ALFWorld 和 search-augmented QA，共 10 个 benchmark。作者报告 DWD 现象在这些设置中普遍出现。DGG 在不牺牲最终效果的前提下达到最高 2.93 倍样本效率提升和 2.14 倍 wall-clock 加速，并能匹配或超过严格 single-use rollout baseline。结果的关键信息不是某个任务分数，而是 lm_head 梯度异常与复用崩溃之间的同步性，以及 gating 放在 Adam 更新前比事后回滚更干净。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 到 35 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中 DWD 的观察和问题设定，再看理论部分两个结论的含义：有害梯度为什么集中到 lm_head，以及 lm_head 梯度范数为什么能指示策略偏移。算法部分只需确认 Z-score gating、丢弃梯度、终止复用循环这三个实现点。实验部分重点看跨模型和跨任务的 DWD 复现图，以及 sample efficiency、wall-clock speedup 与 single-use baseline 的对比。若只关心预训练，可把它当作训练稳定性监控案例，而不是数据或建模范式论文来读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks when to stop reusing rollout samples in RL with verifiable rewards. Reuse improves sample efficiency, but excessive reuse amplifies policy shift and can cause training degradation.","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to the post-training side of the foundation-model pipeline. Rollout generation dominates the cost of many RLVR runs, so controlled sample reuse can reduce training cost for reasoning models. The more transferable idea is the diagnostic: collapse is tracked by a localized signal in the lm_head rather than by delayed global metrics such as reward curves or aggregate KL. Similar localized gradient monitors may be useful for stability analysis in other large-model training stages.","technical_approach":"The empirical observation is called Disproportionate Weight Divergence. When sample reuse begins to damage performance, the lm_head weight change spikes while intermediate layers remain comparatively stable. The explanation has two parts. First, harmful reuse-induced gradients concentrate at the lm_head, while gradients entering intermediate layers are attenuated by downstream Jacobian projections. Second, the lm_head gradient norm lower-bounds the empirical Pearson chi-square divergence between the updating policy and the behavior policy, making it a real-time proxy for policy shift. The proposed method, Dynamic Gradient Gating, monitors the lm_head gradient norm using a dynamic Z-score test. If an anomalous spike is detected, it discards the current gradient before the Adam update and terminates the reuse loop for that rollout batch.","experiments":"The experiments cover six Qwen and Llama models from 1.5B to 8B parameters. DGG matches or improves over the strict single-use rollout baseline while reaching up to 2.93x sample efficiency and 2.14x wall-clock speedup. simple problems while encouraging deeper e","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T04:41:51+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19358.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 reasoning RL 里一个很现实的矛盾：为了提高复杂题准确率，模型往往被鼓励生成更长、更发散的 CoT；但这种探索式训练会把简单题也拖成长推理，带来明显的 overthinking、时延和推理成本。已有两类方法各有缺口：一类基于高熵 token 鼓励探索，通常能提准确率，但会进一步拉长输出；另一类直接压长度或做按需触发 CoT，能省 token，却容易伤到真正需要深思考的难题。作者要解决的是如何让模型在简单题上更果断，在难题或错误路径上更愿意探索，而且这种调节不是按题目预先分类，而是在 token 级别、沿着当前推理路径动态发生。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在 pretrain-posttrain 接口和 reasoning policy 学习上。它不是新的预训练目标，但它触及一个越来越关键的问题：后训练阶段如何塑造‘计算分配策略’，让模型把有限的生成预算用在真正不确定的位置。作者把 token-level entropy 视为局部不确定性信号，再结合路径正确性做条件化 shaping，这比单纯 length penalty 或统一鼓励探索更细。这个思路对长 CoT RL、test-time compute 学习、on-demand reasoning 都有参考价值。它的贡献量级更像‘对现有 DAPO/GRPO 系列 RL 的有效改造’，而不是独立的新范式；但如果你关心 reasoning 模型为什么会越训越啰嗦、怎样在不明显掉点的情况下压缩思维长度，这篇是有信息量的。","technical_approach":"方法建立在 DAPO 上，核心是 Conditional Entropy Shaping，简称 CES。作者观察到 reasoning trace 里少数高熵 token 往往是‘forking points’，也就是后续推理分支的决策点。过去一些工作对这些高熵 token 统一加奖励，目的是维持探索，结果常常是正确路径也被鼓励继续发散，输出越来越长。CES 的改动在于把 shaping policy 变成双向且条件化。\n\n具体地，训练时先根据 response-level reward 判断一条生成轨迹是正确还是错误，再结合每个 token 的生成熵来重塑 token-level advantage：\n1. 对正确轨迹，惩罚其中高熵 token。含义是：既然这条路径已经通向正确答案，那么在这些分叉点上应当学会更确定、更直接地走过去，而不是继续保留高不确定性和冗长展开。\n2. 对错误轨迹，奖励其中高熵 token。含义是：如果当前路径错了，那么这些分叉点恰好是最值得增加探索的地方，应该鼓励模型在这些位置尝试别的分支。\n\n这和统一 length penalty 的区别很大。长度惩罚只看结果长短，不知道哪些 token 是必要思考、哪些是无效铺陈；CES 试图只改动最关键的不确定位置。它和统一高熵奖励也不同，后者默认探索总是好事，而 CES 认为探索是否该被鼓励，取决于当前轨迹是否已经正确。\n\n从算法上看，CES 不是替换 DAPO，而是往 DAPO 的 advantage shaping 里加一个基于 correctness × entropy 的条件项。DAPO 本身是对 GRPO 的工程化增强，面向长 CoT RL 稳定训练；CES 则在这个框架里进一步控制探索-简洁性的平衡。全文还强调这是 token-level、conditional、bidirectional 的策略，而不是 query-level 的 hard routing 或后处理裁剪。","experiments":"实验覆盖 12 个数学 benchmark，主结论是 CES 相比 DAPO 在平均 accuracy-efficiency trade-off 上更好：既提升或维持准确率，又减少不必要的推理长度。摘要和引言没有给出完整数字表，但明确声称这种改动不是单纯压长度，而是在平均意义上同时改善准确率与效率。\n\n作者还做了两个方向的补充验证。第一是在更小的 1.5B backbone 上测试，说明方法不只在较大模型上成立。第二是 out-of-domain benchmark，说明它学到的不是某个训练集特定模板，而是更一般的‘按需分配思考’行为。\n\n从全文描述看，分析实验是这篇文章的重要部分。作者试图展示 CES 学到了一种 adaptive reasoning policy：简单题更短，难题或错误路径上保留更多探索。也就是说，长度变化不是全局统一缩短，而是按问题难度和当前路径状态分化。若主表里确实如引言所述在 12 个数学集上平均优于 DAPO，那么最值得看的不是单个 benchmark 的绝对分数，而是 accuracy 与 token cost 的联合变化，以及不同难度题上的长度分布。\n\n由于你给出的正文片段在方法处截断，没有完整结果表，无法可靠抄出具体提升点数、平均 token 降幅、训练步数或采样组大小。能确认的是：基线是 DAPO，评测覆盖 12 个数学 benchmark，并包含 1.5B 模型和 out-of-domain 泛化实验。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 里作者如何摆出两条已有路线的矛盾：高熵探索方法提升准确率但拉长 CoT，长度控制方法缩短输出但可能伤复杂题。理解这个张力后，再看 CES 的条件化设计，就能明白它为什么不是简单的 entropy regularization。\n\n方法部分重点看 advantage shaping 的公式和高熵 token 的选取方式。需要弄清三件事：correct/incorrect path 是如何判定的；高熵 token 是按阈值、top-k 还是比例选取；shaping 项如何与 DAPO 原始 advantage 合成。若这些细节设计得不稳，训练很容易出现模式坍缩，要么全都变短，要么继续无节制发散。\n\n实验部分优先看主结果表、长度分布图、按题目难度分桶的分析，以及 1.5B 和 out-of-domain 结果。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。若要深挖，建议重点检查两点：一是 CES 是否真的在简单题缩短、难题保留长度，而不是平均上做了一个折中；二是 token entropy 作为不确定性代理是否稳定，是否会把语言表述上的犹豫误当成推理分叉点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical tension in reasoning RL. To improve accuracy on hard problems, models are often encouraged to generate longer and more exploratory chains of thought. But that same training pressure makes them overthink easy problems, increasing latency and token cost. Existing methods split into two unsatisfying camps: entropy-based exploration improves reasoning but often lengthens outputs further, while length-control methods reduce tokens but can damage performance on problems that genuinely require deeper reasoning. The paper asks how to make a model more decisive on easy cases and more exploratory on hard or currently failing trajectories, without relying on a coarse query-level switch.","importance":"Its relevance is at the pretrain-posttrain interface, especially for learning reasoning policies rather than changing the base language modeling objective. The key idea is to treat token-level entropy as a local uncertainty signal and modulate it conditionally based on whether the current trajectory is correct. That is more targeted than a global length penalty and more disciplined than always rewarding exploration. For researchers interested in long-CoT RL, test-time compute allocation, or on-demand reasoning, this is a useful design pattern: shape where the model spends computation, not just how much it spends overall. The contribution is best viewed as a meaningful refinement of the DAPO/GRPO-style RL stack rather than a new paradigm.","technical_approach":"$3b","experiments":"$3c","reading_guide":"$3d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看熵按正确性分流的设计即可；结论方向合理，但没有规模和消融细节前，不应改训练栈。","en":"Read only the conditional entropy design; the direction is sensible, but without scale and ablation details it should not change a training stack yet."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Beijing Jiaotong University","Beijing Key Laboratory of Traffic Data Mining and Embodied Intelligence"],"authors":["Shuyu Wei","Jian Sun","Delai Qiu","Yining Wang","Shengping Liu","Jiaen Liang","Ying Fu","Wei Huang","Jitao Sang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLVR","entropy-regularization","reasoning","DAPO","length-control"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19358","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19358","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.19433","title":"Backtracking When It Strays: Mitigating Dual Exposure Biases in LLM Reasoning Distillation","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in complex reasoning tasks via long chain-of-thought (CoT), yet their immense computational overhead hinders real-world deployment. LLM reasoning distillation addresses this by transferring reasoning capabilities from formidable teacher models to compact student models. The same reward can be used in GRPO, where samples under the same prompt are normalized into relative advantages, or in DPO, where reward-ranked images form winner/loser pairs for diffusion-DPO training. Each rank-one component is independently gated, offering finer granularity than mixture-of-LoRA approaches that route entire modules. th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-05-20T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2605_21468.png","hf_upvotes":46,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究
