RLVR 很有用，因为它支持一种观点：RLVR 更像是在放大预训练模型中已有的成功推理模式，而不是从零学习复杂新能力。限制也清楚：实验集中在数学 RLVR 和若干 Qwen 系列模型上，能否扩展到代码、agent、多模态或长链路 RL，还需要验证。","technical_approach":"方法从 checkpoint 轨迹出发。设基础模型为 θ0，RLVR 第 t 步 checkpoint 为 θt，对每个参数张量计算 Δθt=θt-θ0。然后把同一张量在多个训练步的 delta 展平成向量并堆叠成矩阵 M∈R^{T×mn}，对 M 做 SVD。作者发现 rank-1 方向已经捕获主要的下游有效更新；把 checkpoint 投影到这个主方向后得到的标量系数 ct 随训练步近似线性，很多张量的线性拟合 R² 超过 0.98。RELEX 基于这个现象：只运行前 15% 到 20% 的 RLVR，利用早期 checkpoint 估计 rank-1 子空间，对投影系数做线性回归，再外推未来步数的权重。它不训练额外预测器，也不改变 GRPO 目标。一个关键观察是更高 rank 和非线性时间模型没有带来稳定收益，rank-1 投影反而像谱域去噪，保留稳定任务信号，丢掉一部分随机优化噪声。","experiments":"实验使用 GRPO 风格的 RLVR，并在 88.5%。作者还报告 RELEX 在 5 个 OOD 数学 benchmark 的平均表现超过完整 RLVR。分析部分显示 rank-1 重构 checkpoint 能在多个训练步接近 oracle RLVR checkpoint；rank-1 系数随 step 近似线性；增加 rank 或使用非线性外推不能进一步改善结果。","reading_guide":"建议泛读到半精读，预计 35 数、非线性拟合和谱去噪解释。读的时候要留意三个风险点：早期轨迹是否受学习率 warmup 影响，外推步数超过平台期后是否会失效，以及该低秩结构是否只是数学 RLVR 的特例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether the future weight trajectory of RLVR can be predicted from a short prefix of training, so that one can obtain a near-final RLVR checkpoint with only a small fraction of the RL compute.","importance":"The main contribution is a training-dynamics observation with practical implications for post-training cost. The paper does not propose a new RL objective. Instead, it shows that, for math RLVR, parameter updates appear highly low-dimensional: most useful change in each tensor lies along one dominant direction, and training mostly changes the scalar magnitude along that direction. This supports the view that RLVR often amplifies reasoning behaviors already present in the base model rather than learning them from scratch. The evidence is still scoped: the experiments focus on math RLVR and Qwen-family backbones, so transfer to code, agentic tasks, multimodal RL, or long-horizon RL remains open.","technical_approach":"The analysis starts from checkpoint trajectories. Given a base model θ0 and RLVR checkpoints θt, the authors compute deltas Δθt=θt−θ0 for each parameter tensor. For a tensor, the deltas across training steps are flattened and stacked into a trajectory matrix M∈R^{T×mn}, followed by SVD. The key empirical finding is that a rank-1 component captures most of the downstream-relevant update, and the projected coefficient ct along this dominant direction evolves almost linearly with training step, with R² above 0.98 for many tensors. RELEX uses the first 15–20% of RLVR training to estimate this rank-1 subspace, fits a line to the projected coefficients, and extrapolates future checkpoints. It requires no learned predictor and does not alter GRPO. The paper also argues that higher-rank components mostly capture less stable dynamics, while rank-1 projection acts as a spectral denoising step.","experiments":"The experiments use GRPO-style RLVR on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base. The paper also reports stronger average performance than full RLVR on five out-of-domain math benchmarks. The ablations indicate that rank-1 checkpoint reconstruction tracks oracle RLVR checkpoints well, the coefficient dynamics are close to linear, and increasing the rank or using nonlinear temporal models does not yield consistent gains.","reading_guide":"Skim with selective deep reading in about 35 minutes. However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T16:28:53+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20075.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"CopT 处理的是 CoT 推理顺序带来的冗余：很多问题上模型可能已经能给出可用答案，但标准 CoT 仍要求先生成完整思考轨迹，导致延迟和 token 开销。论文把流程改成“先答、再按需反思”，核心难点是判断草稿答案是否可信，以及在后续思考中如何避免草稿答案误导模型。","importance":"这不是预训练方法，而是一个训练无关的推理范式。对基座研究的启发主要在两个点。第一，它把 latent CoT 中的连续表示从“用于生成”改成“用于验证”，避免了连续隐状态直接生成时的分布外表示、退化和漂移问题。第二，它给出一种利用 next-token distribution 中不确定性做答案可靠性估计的方式，可作为研究 reasoning model 内部置信度、反思触发策略、推理时计算分配的参考。","technical_approach":"CopT 先让模型生成 draft answer，然后用离散 token 输入和连续 embedding 输入对同一批已生成 token 的支持度做对比。连续 embedding 由在线缓存的 next-token distribution 构造，保留了模型在生成时没有显式采样出来的不确定性。论文用序列级 reverse KL estimator 衡量草稿答案可靠性：如果连续空间和离散空间对答案 token 的支持差异较小，草稿更可能可信；如果差异较大，则触发后续 on-policy thinking。触发思考后，CopT 不是简单把草稿答案全程暴露给模型，而是在 thinking chunk 内周期性计算第二个 KL estimator，动态决定草稿答案是否可见。这样做的取舍很明确：保留显式 CoT 的可读性，同时利用连续分布携带的不确定性；不把连续 embedding 当作生成轨迹，降低 latent generation 的稳定性风险。论文还给出 latent-state 解释：在局部 mixture-prefix 视角和 mixture-linear 假设下，估计量的期望对应 latent reasoning state S 与 answer token A 的互信息 I(S;A)，因此它测的是答案相关不确定性，而不是隐状态总熵。","experiments":"给定全文内容未包含完整实验表格和具体数值，不能确认各 benchmark 上的准确提升幅度。可确认的实验目标应包括三类：一是草稿答案直接接受时的准确率与 token 节省；二是触发 on-policy thinking 后的纠错能力；三是草稿可见性控制对 agentic reasoning 和一般推理任务的影响。消融上应重点看 reverse KL estimator 与简单置信度、logprob、entropy 等基线的差异，以及 chunk-level visibility control 是否比始终展示或始终隐藏草稿更稳。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 20–30 分钟。优先看方法部分中两个 KL estimator 的定义、连续 embedding 的构造方式、accept/rethink 阈值策略，以及 chunk-level visibility control。理论部分可重点看 mixture-prefix 解释，它决定了这个分数到底是在测答案可靠性，还是只是在测分布熵。实验部分若有完整版本，重点核对 token 节省是否以准确率为代价，以及在错误草稿上是否会出现确认偏误。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"CopT targets an inefficiency in standard chain-of-thought reasoning. Many queries may already have a plausible answer early, yet the usual pipeline forces the model to produce a full reasoning trace before answering. The paper reverses the order: answer first, then think only when needed. The two hard parts are deciding whether the draft answer is reliable, and deciding how much that draft should influence later reflection.","importance":"This is an inference-time reasoning method rather than a pretraining method. Its main value for foundation-model research is the use of continuous representations as verifiers instead of generators. That is a useful shift: it keeps explicit reasoning readable while using the uncertainty stored in next-token distributions. It also provides a concrete signal for studying confidence, reflection triggering, and adaptive test-time compute in reasoning models.","technical_approach":"$3e","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the complete experimental tables, so exact benchmark gains cannot be verified. The relevant experimental checks should be: accuracy and token savings when accepting drafts early; correction rate when on-policy thinking is triggered; and the effect of draft-visibility control in both general reasoning and agentic settings. The most important ablations are the reverse-KL estimator against simpler confidence signals such as log probability or entropy, and chunk-level visibility control against always showing or always hiding the draft.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. \\textbf{MixRea},","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T17:15:08+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20128.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的问题很具体：当任务指令把模型注意力强烈拉向“显式信息”时，模型是否会系统性忽略上下文里不显眼但决定答案的“隐式线索”。作者把这个现象定义为 explicit-implicit reasoning 失败，并借用人类认知里的 inattentional blindness 来描述。和常见的阅读理解或多跳推理不同，这里难点不在于信息量大，而在于模型需要在明确任务目标下，主动把看似无关的背景线索纳入推理链。全文想证明的不是“模型不会推理”，而是“模型在目标导向推理时，对潜在线索的检索与整合能力存在结构性短板”。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在评测和训练信号诊断两层。第一，它把“是否能整合隐式上下文”从泛化的 reasoning 能力里单独剥离出来，形成了一个更细颗粒度的失效模式刻画。这个角度对预训练很重要，因为它指向的不是单纯 CoT 长度不够，而可能是预训练语料中的显式监督偏置、注意分配偏置，以及模型在目标条件下的检索习惯。第二，结果显示强推理模型在这个设置下仍然很弱，说明现有后训练把答案组织得更像推理，不等于真正提升了对潜在线索的敏感度。贡献量级偏中等：它不是新训练范式，也没有改变基座建模，但提供了一个对 pretrain/post-train 都有诊断价值的 benchmark 和一个有一定效果的 prompting baseline。","technical_approach":"方法上分两部分。第一部分是 MixRea benchmark 构造。每个样本由三块组成：含隐式信息的故事上下文、给出显式约束的假设性问题、以及四个候选 event sets。每个候选由两个事件组成，正确答案必须同时满足显式问题和隐式背景。数据基础来自 Possible Stories，这个底座的优点是同一 context 下有多个问题和共享候选项，便于重组出“显式-隐式混合约束”的题型。作者先筛掉同一 context 下答案冲突的 question pair，再借助 GPT-4o 做重写、配对、验证和质量控制，最终得到 2246 道多选题，覆盖 9 类 reasoning type，并系统控制显式/隐式信息分布。\n\n第二部分是 PRCP，Potential Relation Completion Prompting。它不是让模型泛泛地“多想一步”，而是显式要求模型先补全潜在关系，再做最终选择。作者的错误分析认为，模型常见失败不是最后比较选项时算错，而是在前面没有把 latent causal relation 提取出来。PRCP 的设计就是把这一步前置并外显化，迫使模型枚举上下文中可能与任务目标相关、但未被问题直接点名的关系。和普通 CoT 相比，它更像一个 relation elicitation prompt；和 one-shot 相比，它减少了模型直接套模板答题的倾向。全文的重点不在 prompt engineering 本身，而在于用 prompt 干预去验证失效来源：如果显式要求补全潜在关系后性能能稳定上升，说明瓶颈确实在隐式线索检索与整合，而不只是最终决策。","experiments":"实验主体评测了 21 个模型，覆盖 7 个家族，包括 Gemini 2.5 Pro/Flash、DeepSeek-R1/V3、GPT-4o/4o-mini、Claude 3.7、Qwen 系列、LLaMA 3.x、Gemma 在构造流程里承担了哪些角色；这里关系到数据是否真的在测“隐式线索整合”，还是只是模板扰动。接着看主结果表，关注 reasoning 与 non-reasoning 模型的差距是否稳定，以及 consistency 指标的定义。最后看 PRCP 和 error analysis，这部分是全文最有启发的地方：它把失败拆成“没看到潜在线索”与“看到了但没用好”两类。预计 30-45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果只想判断是否值得纳入内部评测集，优先看数据构造、指标定义和附加任务消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a narrow but important failure mode: when a task explicitly directs the model toward ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-13T03:14:09+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18838.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要回答的问题不是“能力随规模怎么涨”，而是“不同能力之间的相互作用是否随规模发生结构性变化”。具体聚焦在 reasoning（以 HellaSwag 代表）与 truthfulness（以 TruthfulQA 代表）之间的耦合：在同一模型家族内扩参时，推理提升到底会促进还是损害真实性；以及这种关系是否存在一个类似相变的临界尺度，并且能否被训练配方（数据、架构、宽度等）系统性移动。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值在于把“对齐税/小模型更容易胡说”从经验叙事变成可测量的 N 的函数。作者区分了两类指标：\n- population coupling r(B1,B2)：跨一组模型的皮尔逊相关；\n- local coupling γ12：家族内随规模变化的局部导数，用来定位相变点。\n经验规律是：γ12 在小规模为负（推理涨、真实性掉），到达家族相关的临界尺度 Nc 后穿零转正，并在 log10(N/Nc) undergo a regime change in *interactions* between capabilities, not just in each capability’s own curve. Concretely: within a model family, as size increases, does improved reasoning tend to harm or help truthfulness—and is there a family-dependent critical scale where this coupling flips sign? It further asks whether that critical point is an inherent property of “intelligence” or an engineerable outcome of architecture, width, data curation, and training recipe.","importance":"For pretraining research, the main contribution is turning the “alignment tax” narrative into a measurable scaling object: capability coupling can be tracked, compared across recipes, and (the authors claim) extrapolated, much like loss scaling. The practical implication is that small-to-mid scale models (roughly 1B–7B, where many deployments live) may sit in a regime where pushing reasoning can systematically degrade truthfulness—unless the recipe shifts the critical scale. This reframes data curation and architectural choices as levers that can move the model from a competitive to a cooperative capability regime, and challenges the common implicit assumption that benchmark trajectories are independent.","technical_approach":"$41","experiments":"$42","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes):\n1) Read the definitions of local coupling γ12 and population coupling r, and the empirical law γ12(N,D)=γ0(D)·log10(N/Nc(D)).\n2) Inspect the main figure showing sign flips of γ12 across families and how Nc varies.\n3) Focus on the “engineerability” section: Qwen generation comparison, Gemma-4 and Phi examples, and the width effect—this is where the paper makes actionable claims for pretraining.\n4) If you care about mechanisms, read the analysis supporting “no competing heads” and the argument that the bottleneck is at the output projection.\n5) For robustness, check how they compute Δ scores between adjacent sizes, how they handle sparse family grids, and the setup of the OLMo calibration point and the ODE cross-prediction (split protocol and error metric).","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"结论很大胆但证据需打折；只看耦合定义、宽度归一化和Qwen/Phi对照，能给pretrain eval设计一个新轴。","en":"The claim is bold and needs discounting; read the coupling metric, width normalization, and Qwen/Phi comparisons as a new eval axis for pretraining."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ZEHEN Labs"],"authors":["Adil Amin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["scaling-analysis","truthfulness","reasoning","capability-coupling","base-model-evaluation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.18838","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.20033","title":"A Nash Equilibrium Framework For Training-Free Multimodal Step Verification","abstract":"Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Learned critics need extensive labeled data and show inconsistent performance across different tasks. Meanwhile, existing training-free methods simply average scores from different sources, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasonin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T15:54:22+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_20033.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.GT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是多模态逐步推理里的 step verification，但切入点不是训练一个更强的 process reward model，而是在没有标注、没有额外训练的条件下，怎样把多个冻结 verifier 的分歧信息利用起来。作者认为现有 training-free 聚合方法把多个 judge 的分数直接平均，会把“高置信一致”和“强烈冲突后的中间值”混为一谈，导致错误步骤被误接受。核心问题因此是：如何把 verifier 之间的 disagreement 变成可操作的拒绝信号，而不是把它在平均过程中抹平。","importance":"它和预训练的直接距离较远，更接近推理期控制与过程监督替代方案，因此只适合泛读。启发点主要有两个。第一，冻结模型之间的分歧可以作为一种廉价监督信号，这对 process supervision、self-verification、甚至后续做无标注 reward 建模有参考意义。第二，论文把多 verifier 聚合从启发式平均改成显式博弈均衡，说明“如何聚合模型判断”本身可以是一个建模问题，而不只是 prompt engineering。对基座预训练研究员来说，价值主要在 post-train 接口：如果未来想用无标注方式构造 step-level 偏好或拒绝数据，这类 disagreement-aware 机制可能比简单投票更稳。","technical_approach":"方法很直接。给定图像、问题和当前部分推理链，基础 MLLM 生成候选下一步 reasoning step。然后用三个冻结 judge 分别从视觉支撑、逻辑连贯、上下文相关三个角度打分，输出 [0,1] 置信度。不同于直接平均，这里把每个 judge 看成一个博弈参与者。每个参与者既想和其他人保持一致，又不愿偏离自己的原始判断太多。作者用一个二次效用函数刻画这种张力：一项惩罚与其他 judge 平均意见的偏离，另一项惩罚与自身初始分数的偏离，系数 λ 控制自我坚持程度。求这个博弈的 Nash equilibrium 后，得到一组“考虑过他人意见后的最终分数”，再据此决定接受还是拒绝当前步骤。\n\n这个框架的关键不是博弈论标签，而是它把 disagreement 当成结构化信号。若某一步在视觉上看似成立，但逻辑上不连贯，均衡解不会像简单平均那样给出一个模糊的中间高分，而会反映出系统难以协调的状态。论文强调 closed-form solution，因此推理时开销主要来自调用 judge，而不是求解复杂优化。整个方案是 plug-in 式的，不改 base model，也不训练 verifier。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个 benchmark，任务集中在空间推理和视觉 grounding。作者声称相对 learned critics 和若干 training-free baseline 都有稳定提升。全文给出的重点应是两类比较：一类是与简单平均、方差、熵等聚合方式对比，证明均衡分数更能区分“共识高”和“冲突大”的步骤；另一类是接入不同 MLLM 推理流程后的最终任务表现，说明 step-level 拒绝/重采样确实能减少错误传播。由于给出的正文片段没有完整数字，能确认的是方法不依赖额外标注，也不修改基础模型，收益来自 verifier 聚合方式本身。阅读时应特别留意 λ 的设定、judge prompt 的固定格式、以及 reject-resample Instead, it asks how to exploit disagreement among multiple frozen verifiers in a training-free setting. Existing aggregation methods typically average verifier scores, which collapses two very different cases into similar values: strong consensus and strong conflict. That makes subtly wrong reasoning steps look moderately acceptable. The core problem is therefore how to turn verifier disagreement into an actionable rejection signal rather than washing it out through naive averaging.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper and sits closer to inference-time control and process supervision, so it is only a skim for a pretraining-focused reader. The useful idea is that disagreement among frozen models can serve as a cheap supervision signal. That has some relevance for process supervision, self-verification, and possibly for constructing step-level preference or rejection data without annotations. A second useful point is methodological: score aggregation itself is treated as a modeling problem rather than a prompt-level heuristic. For researchers thinking about the pretrain–posttrain interface, the paper is interesting mainly as a possible ingredient for unlabeled step-level filtering.","technical_approach":"$43","experiments":"Experiments span six benchmarks focused on spatial reasoning and visual grounding. Second, against end-to-end reasoning performance with and without verification, to show that rejecting unstable intermediate steps reduces downstream error propagation. The excerpt provided does not include the full numeric tables, so the main confirmed takeaway is that the gains come from the aggregation mechanism itself rather than additional supervision or model updates. Confidence estimation offers a diagnostic signal, yet existing methods are restricted to final answers or require internal model access. In this paper, we introduce Stepwise Confidence Attribution (SCA), a framework for closed-source LLMs that assigns step-level confidence based only ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T00:57:51+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19228.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.IT","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是黑盒 LLM 多步推理里一个很具体但长期缺位的问题：当最终答案错了，究竟是哪一步开始偏了；当答案对了，哪些中间步骤是真正承载正确推理的关键锚点。已有方法要么依赖逐步人工标注训练 step verifier，要么让另一个 LLM 充当裁判逐步打分。前者成本高，后者会把 judge model 的偏置和不稳定性一并带进来。作者把问题改写成 stepwise confidence attribution：只利用模型生成的多条推理轨迹和最终答案正确性，在黑盒条件下给每个 reasoning step 分配置信度。关键难点不在于找字面相似，而在于区分‘正确推理的合法多样性’和‘真正导致失败的偏离’。同一道题的正确解法可能顺序不同、表述不同、细节粗细不同，但通常会经过一些共享的逻辑中间态；错误轨迹则会在这些逻辑锚点附近发生漂移。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于提升一个 benchmark 分数，而在于提供了一种更细粒度的训练信号和诊断接口。它把传统 answer-level confidence estimation 往过程级别推进了一层，而且不要求 logits、hidden states 或白盒访问，这使它更像一种可移植的 post-hoc 过程监督工具。对预训练和后训练接口而言，这类 step-level 置信度有三类价值。第一，它能更准确地定位 reasoning trace 中的脆弱位置，比只看 final answer 更适合做 self-correction、rejection sampling、过程数据清洗。第二，它提供了一种弱监督方式去近似 process reward，不必完全依赖昂贵的人类逐步标注。第三，它提出了一个值得注意的建模观点：正确推理轨迹之间存在可压缩的共识结构，这种结构可以作为 correctness 的代理信号。这个观点对 process supervision、latent reasoning 以及多样推理轨迹聚合都有启发。不过量级上看，它更像一个有方法新意的诊断与利用框架，而不是会直接改写预训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心建立在 Information Bottleneck。作者把一条推理轨迹 X 看成由多个步骤组成的输入，把带有 step confidence 的压缩表示 Z 看成从轨迹中筛出的关键信息，把最终 correctness Y 作为监督目标，目标是保留与正确性最相关的步骤，同时压掉表面变体和冗余细节。文中给了两个实现。\n\n第一类是非参数方法 NIBS。它先收集同一问题上的多条正确解轨迹，从中抽取‘共识步骤’或‘逻辑不变量’，再衡量待评估轨迹的每一步与这些共识结构的对齐程度。高对齐步骤得到高置信度，缺失于共识或明显偏离的步骤得到低置信度。这里的关键不是简单 n-gram 匹配，而是试图在不同表达、不同顺序下找到功能等价的 reasoning milestone。按作者的设定，这相当于用正确轨迹集合近似 IB 里的相关信息项，用稀疏保留的共识步骤近似压缩表示。\n\n第二类是 GIBS，Graph IB for Stepwise Confidence。作者认为纯序列视角对 step permutation、局部重排和图式推理不够稳，因此把 reasoning trajectory 表示成图，再通过可微分 mask 选择与 correctness 最相关的子图。随后用子图与正确性信号对齐，得到更灵活的 step-level confidence。这个版本更适合 Graph-of-Thought 或结构变化较大的推理格式，也更接近‘逻辑骨架提取’而不是‘线性步骤打分’。\n\n从全文表述看，作者还把 SCA 用在下游 self-correction：不是只告诉模型‘答案可能错了’，而是把低置信度步骤作为修正重点，引导模型局部回溯和重写。这个设计解释了为什么 step-level CE 能比 answer-level feedback 更有效，因为后者只给一个全局失败信号，无法告诉模型该从哪里改。","experiments":"实验重点有四块。第一，step-level confidence 的诊断准确性。作者在多步推理任务上比较 SCA 与若干基线，包括 answer-level CE、基于采样分歧的置信度、以及依赖 judge 或监督标注的方案。结论是两种 IB 方法都能更稳定地识别错误步骤，尤其在正确轨迹存在较大表述差异时，图结构版本更稳。摘要和正文都强调它们在黑盒设定下仍保持较好的 step attribution 质量。\n\n第二，self-correction 效果。作者把 step-level 置信度用于引导纠错，相比只给 final-answer 级别反馈，correction success rate 最高提升 13.5%。这是全文里最有分量的结果，因为它说明 stepwise signal 不只是解释性更强，而是能转化成可用的性能增益。\n\n第三，消融。文中验证了共识建模、IB 约束、图表示等组件的作用。去掉共识结构后，方法更容易把表面相似当成正确逻辑；不用图表示时，对步骤重排和结构变体更敏感。作者还分析了正确解样本数量、标签可用性条件等因素，说明方法并不要求大规模逐步标注，但需要一定数量的正确轨迹来形成稳定共识。\n\n第四，鲁棒性与泛化。作者测试了不同 reasoning format、domain shift 和标签稀缺条件。结论是 SCA 对 CoT 与更结构化的推理形式都有一定适配性，GIBS 在结构变化更大的场景下更占优。就论文定位而言，这些实验比单一 benchmark 提升更重要，因为它们支撑了‘共识逻辑锚点’这一核心假设。\n\n给定你提供的全文片段里没有完整表格数字、模型规模和数据规模，能确认的硬数字主要是 self-correction 相对 answer-level feedback 最高提升 13.5%。其余更细的参数量、样本数和具体分数需要对照正文实验表读取。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先抓三件事。第一，看 Introduction 和 Problem Statement，确认作者到底在定义什么任务：不是 step correctness classification，也不是让 judge model 打标签，而是用 final correctness 锚定 step-level confidence。这个定义决定了它和 process supervision 的边界。\n\n第二，看方法部分里 NIBS 与 GIBS 的差别。重点不是公式推导本身，而是作者如何把‘正确轨迹的共识结构’落成可计算对象。若时间有限，优先理解非参数版本，因为它最能体现方法直觉；再看图版本解决了哪些序列方法处理不了的结构变体。\n\n第三，看 self-correction 实验和鲁棒性分析。前者回答‘这个信号有没有用’，后者回答‘它是不是只在特定格式下成立’。如果只打算花 20 到 30 分钟，建议读引言、方法总览图、主结果表、self-correction 表和 ablation。若要进一步深挖，值得关注两个问题：一是共识结构是否会把少数但正确的非主流解法误判为低置信度；二是这种基于正确轨迹聚合的信号，能否反过来用于构造 process reward 或训练 verifier。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a missing capability in black-box LLM reasoning: locating where a multi-step solution starts to go wrong, and identifying which intermediate steps actually carry the essential logic when the final answer is correct. Existing step-level diagnosis usually relies on either expensive human step annotations or another LLM acting as a judge. The authors instead define Stepwise Confidence Attribution (SCA): assign confidence to each reasoning step using only generated traces and final-answer correctness, without logits, hidden states, or model internals. The central difficulty is not surface matching. Correct solutions can differ in order, wording, and granularity, yet still pass through shared logical milestones. Errors, in contrast, tend to drift away from those latent anchors.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is mainly in the training signal it exposes, not in a headline benchmark jump. The work pushes confidence estimation from answer level to process level under a black-box setting. That matters for three reasons. First, step-level confidence is more actionable than answer-level confidence for self-correction, rejection, and process-data filtering. Second, it offers a weakly supervised route toward process reward signals without requiring dense human annotations. Third, it advances a useful modeling view: correct reasoning traces contain a compressible consensus structure, and that structure can serve as a proxy for correctness. This is relevant to process supervision, trajectory aggregation, and reasoning-data curation. The contribution is meaningful, though it is better seen as a diagnostic and post-hoc supervision framework than as a change to the pretraining paradigm itself.","technical_approach":"$44","experiments":"$45","reading_guide":"$46","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"如果你在做 reasoning trace 过滤或过程监督，可以读；先看方法假设是否把“多数结构”误当“正确结构”，这会决定它是工具还是噪声源。","en":"Worth reading if you work on trace filtering or process supervision; first check whether it mistakes majority structure for correct structure—that determines whether it is useful or just another noise source."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xiaoou Liu","Tiejin Chen","Dengjia Zhang","Yaqing Wang","Lu Cheng","Hua Wei"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning","confidence-estimation","black-box-llm","process-supervision","error-localization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19228","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19228","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.19908","title":"Where Does Authorship Signal Emerge in Encoder-Based Language Models?","abstract":"Authorship attribution models fine-tuned with the same pretrained encoder, data, and loss can differ four-fold in performance depending only on their scoring mechanism. We use mechanistic interpretability tools to explain this gap. Stylistic features such as word length, punctuation density, and function-word frequency are equally available at every layer in every model, including in an off-the-shelf control encoder, hence the gap not coming from representation quality. Instead, causal intervent","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T14:37:51+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19908.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究一个很具体但很有代表性的问题：在同一个预训练 encoder、同一份数据、同一种对比学习损失下，为什么仅仅更换 scoring / pooling 机制，就会让 authorship attribution 的性能出现约四倍差距。作者要区分两种解释：一种是不同 scorer 让 encoder 学到了不同质量的内部表示；另一种是底层风格特征其实各层都有，差距主要来自 scorer 如何迫使这些特征在网络中被整合、并在何处变成对最终决策因果必要的信号。论文的结论偏向后者：特征可读性并不是瓶颈，真正决定性能的是 scorer 改变了梯度结构和训练动态，从而改变 authorship signal 在 encoder 中出现和巩固的层位。","importance":"这不是基座预训练论文，但对做预训练和表征学习的人有明确启发。它提醒人不要把“下游性能差异”直接归因于 backbone 表征质量。很多时候，预训练模型早已编码了足够多的低层统计与风格特征，真正的差别来自 readout 头如何组织梯度、如何决定哪些层必须承担信息压缩与整合。这个观察对 pooling 设计、contrastive fine-tuning、retrieval scorer、甚至多模态对齐头都有参考价值。更重要的是，论文把 feature availability 和 feature use 分开讨论，这个框架对分析预训练表示是否“学到了某种能力”很有用：能线性读出，不等于模型在当前目标下会因果性地使用它。","technical_approach":"$47","experiments":"从摘要和正文片段可确认的关键结果有几类。\n\n1. 性能差距：在同样 backbone、数据和损失下，仅 scorer 不同就能带来约四倍性能差距。这是整篇论文的起点。\n\n2. Feature availability：线性 probe 显示，词长、标点、功能词频率等风格特征在所有层都可读，而且在 off-the-shelf control encoder 中也存在。这说明性能差距不能简单解释为“某个 scorer 学到了更好的风格表示”。\n\n3. Causal layer localization：residual stream patching 显示，mean pooling 模型的 authorship signal 在早层到中层就已变成因果必要；LI 模型则把关键贡献推迟到更晚层。这个结果比 probe 更重要，因为它区分了“可读出”与“被使用”。\n\n4. Patch pooling 作为中间态：bigram patch（n=2）在行为上位于 mean pooling 和 LI 之间，支持作者关于“梯度结构决定整合层位”的解释，而不是偶然的架构差异。\n\n5. 训练动态：alignment / uniformity 分析表明，不同 scorer 在 fine-tuning 过程中走出不同轨迹。虽然正文片段没有给出具体数值，但作者明确强调，差异体现在何时开始压缩作者信号、以及表示空间何时失去局部细节。\n\n这篇论文的实验价值主要在机制证据链完整：probe 证明特征一直都在，patching 证明何处被因果使用，梯度分析解释为什么会这样，训练动态说明这种差异是如何形成的。","reading_guide":"这篇适合做机制分析类泛读，重点不在任务本身，而在分析框架。\n\n先看 Introduction，抓住作者要区分的两个命题：representation quality 差异，还是 readout-induced consolidation 差异。这个问题在很多 encoder fine-tuning 场景都成立。\n\n然后看 Background 里三种 scorer 的定义，尤其是 mean pooling、late interaction、patch pooling 的数学形式。要想清楚它们对梯度传播路径的约束分别是什么。\n\n正文里最值得看的部分是三块：\n1. 线性 probe 结果：确认 feature availability 基本不变。\n2. Residual stream patching：这是整篇最关键的因果证据，建议仔细看图和实验设置。\n3. 梯度结构与训练动态分析：这里解释“为什么 signal emergence 的层位会不同”，也是最能迁移到别的任务的部分。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟左右可以读完核心逻辑。若要深挖，建议思考两个延伸问题：第一，这种 scorer-induced depth shift 是否也会出现在 retrieval、reranker、VLM 对齐头中；第二，预训练阶段若显式鼓励某种 pooling 友好的结构，是否会改变 fine-tuning 时的最优 scorer 选择。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise but broadly relevant question: why can authorship-attribution models, trained with the same pretrained encoder, the same data, and the same contrastive loss, differ by roughly 4x in performance solely because of the scoring mechanism? The authors separate two hypotheses. One is that different scorers induce different internal representations in the encoder. The other is that stylistic features are already present throughout the network, and the real difference is where the scorer forces those features to be consolidated into causally necessary authorship signal. The paper supports the second view: feature availability is not the bottleneck; the scorer changes gradient structure and training dynamics, which in turn changes where authorship signal emerges and becomes necessary.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is useful for anyone working on representation learning, contrastive fine-tuning, or readout design. The main lesson is that downstream performance gaps should not be attributed too quickly to backbone representation quality. A pretrained encoder may already encode the relevant low-level statistics and style cues; the decisive factor can instead be how the scoring head organizes gradients and where it forces information compression. That insight matters beyond authorship attribution: it is relevant to pooling design, retrieval scoring, rerankers, and multimodal alignment heads. The paper is especially valuable for its distinction between feature availability and feature use. Linear decodability does not imply causal use under a given objective.","technical_approach":"$48","experiments":"$49","reading_guide":"$4a","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不在 LLM 主航道，但 setup 很干净：它提醒你别把“表征里有信息”误当成“优化能把信息用出来”；看梯度结构分析那部分就够。","en":"Not core LLM work, but the setup is clean: it is a good reminder that ‘information exists in the representation’ does not mean optimization can use it; read the gradient-structure analysis."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Francis Kulumba","Guillaume Vimont","Laurent Romary","Florian Cafiero"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","training-dynamics","representation-learning","gradient-structure"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19908","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19908","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.18808","title":"Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect","abstract":"We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steerin","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-11T23:01:27+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18808.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 instruction-tuned LLM 内部是否存在可分离、可操控、可组合的“文学写作原语”，例如 first-person self、情感命名、show-don’t-tell、defamiliarization 等。它不是在做预训练方法改进，而是在做机制分析：这些风格与情感能力究竟是弥散在网络里，还是能被 sparse autoencoder 提取成相对清晰的 feature 轴。作者尤其关心“组合性”——某些情绪不是单一 feature 就能触发，而是 gate × self × style modulator 的组合结果。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接影响有限，因为它不涉及训练目标、数据配比或优化；但对理解 instruction tuning 如何重塑内部表征有一定启发。价值主要在两点。第一，它把 SAE interpretability 从常见的实体/概念特征，推进到更抽象的写作技法和情感构成，说明对齐后的模型内部可能形成了较稳定的风格控制轴。第二，它提出了一套低算力、三阶段验证流程，用来从候选 SAE feature 中筛出真正有因果作用的 feature，而不是只靠相关性或 decoder 投影做解释。对做机制分析、风格控制、对齐副作用研究的人会有参考意义；对预训练主线研究员来说，更适合作为泛读材料。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者在 Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT 的中层 residual stream 上训练 sparse autoencoder，然后用一套“对比最小语料 + 三阶段验证”的流程找 feature。所谓对比最小语料，是手工构造只在某一文学轴上不同的 prompt pair，例如 show vs tell、Deep-POV vs ostranenie，以及覆盖 Cowen-Keltner 27 类情绪的场景集合。三阶段验证包括：第一阶段用 logit lens 看 SAE decoder direction 对词表投影，快速找候选 feature；第二阶段用 LLM judge 做语义纯度打分，过滤掉看起来像目标词但语义不纯的 feature；第三阶段直接对 feature 做 steering，再用 5 个 LLM 组成的 judge panel 做因果验证，确认该 feature 真能稳定改变输出行为。\n\n全文提出了四类 feature：naming-gates，主要推动某类情感词或相关词汇的发射；self cluster，围绕第一人称和身份语气；stylistic modulators，例如 show-don’t-tell 和 defamiliarization；以及 compositional emotions，即必须多 feature 联动才出现的情绪。作者还强调跨架构对齐不是靠 decoder cosine，而是靠行为对齐：两个模型里的 feature 向量未必几何相似，但在同一写作任务上表现出相同控制作用。这个视角比单纯做 feature matching 更务实。","experiments":"实验主体是 feature catalog 和 steering 验证，而不是传统 benchmark 分数。作者声称在 Llama 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 9B-IT 中共整理出 24 个 paper-grade canonical features。具体结果里，show-don’t-tell 在两个模型中都能由单一 feature 控制翻转：Llama 的 f32213，Gemma 的 f15097。关于 self features，作者在每个模型里都找到 11 个与 first-person register 相关的 feature，其中一个被描述为最强 RLHF-loaded self feature：Llama 的 f96419、Gemma 的 f70443。对情绪覆盖，Llama 通过 24 个 singleton gates、1 个 partial 和 2 个 multi-feature composition 达到 Cowen-Keltner 27/27 全覆盖；Gemma 达到 23/27，严格失败的类别是 adoration。作者还报告了 12 个 Gemma singleton gates 在 judge 层面达到 8/8 cross-lingual transfer。另一个有意思的发现是 joy、adoration 这类情绪往往不是单 feature，而要通过 gate × self × SDT 的组合才能稳定诱发。\n\n从结果形态看，这篇文章更像“机制目录 + 因果操控案例集”。它的证据强弱取决于 judge 可靠性、steering 幅度是否稳健、以及手工构造语料是否足够覆盖目标概念。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确：先看方法部分的三阶段验证流程，再看 show-don’t-tell、self feature、27-emotion coverage 这三节。这样能最快判断这篇文章的证据链是否站得住。Figure 或表格里如果有 feature purity、steering 成功率、judge 一致性，优先看这些，因为它们比叙述性案例更重要。\n\n预计 20 分钟左右可以抓住主旨。若只想判断是否值得深读，重点看两点：一是作者如何排除“decoder 投影看起来像目标词，但 steering 后并不产生目标行为”的假阳性；二是多 feature composition 的实验是否真的显示了非线性组合，而不是简单叠加 prompt bias。对预训练研究员而言，不必逐个 feature 细读，抓住方法和证据边界即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether instruction-tuned LLMs encode literary craft as separable and steerable internal primitives, rather than as diffuse correlations. The target objects are not ordinary concept features but higher-level writing components: first-person self features, affect naming gates, stylistic modulators such as show-don’t-tell and defamiliarization, and emotions that only emerge through feature composition. knoc","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T12:56:32+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19792.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的不是“VLM 能不能做定位”，而是“VLM 在内部究竟靠什么机制完成定位”。更具体地说，作者想拆开两个常被混在一起的问题：一是对象类别是如何被识别出来的，二是边界框是如何从视觉 token 中被组织出来的。全文的重点在于用机制解释工具，分析 LLaVA-1.5 与 InternVL-3.5 这两类 ViT→adapter→LLM 架构中，定位信号存放在哪里、如何被 LLM 读取、分类与定位之间是并行还是串行关系，以及多视角输入时全局图与局部高分辨率图各自承担什么角色。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在于提出了新的预训练方法，而在于把一个常见但模糊的现象讲清楚：当前主流 VLM 的定位能力并不是建立在精细像素级几何理解上，而更像是从 patch token 中提取“容器式”空间边界，再由少量关键注意力头把分类结果传递给定位过程。这个结论对预训练有两层启发。第一，若视觉 backbone 主要来自 CLIP 这类全局对比学习模型，那么后续 VLM 的空间 grounding 可能天然受限，instruction tuning 只能部分补救。第二，若定位依赖极少数稀疏头和较弱的位置残差信号，那么改进视觉 tokenizer、adapter 的位置编码保真度、以及训练时显式加入空间监督，可能比单纯扩大语言模型更有效。它更像一篇机制分析论文，而不是方法论文，但对理解“为什么现有 VLM 定位不稳”是有帮助的。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者做了几件互相补强的事情。第一，选了两种代表性架构：LLaVA-1.5 作为单视图、无压缩、token 与 patch 一一对应的可解释基线；InternVL-3.5 作为带 pixel shuffle 压缩和 global/local 多视图输入的更复杂系统。这样可以比较“简单映射”与“压缩+多视图”下定位机制是否一致。第二，数据集不是直接拿 COCO 原始标注，而是先做标注修正和质量过滤，最后保留 3560 张图、6403 个对象标注，并额外构造控制集来压制背景上下文导致的幻觉检测。这一步很关键，因为机制分析对噪声很敏感。\n\n具体分析工具包括：\n1. token ablation：遮掉或替换部分视觉 token，观察预测框和类别变化，用来判断哪些 token 真正承载定位信息。\n2. controlled perturbation：在保持对象边界不变的前提下打乱对象内部语义排列，或改变边界外区域，测试模型到底依赖“内部语义布局”还是“边界容器”。作者据此提出 containerization：只要对象对应 token 的空间范围还在，内部语义排列对框预测影响很小。\n3. positional decoding：从 adapter 后或 LLM 内部表征中解码二维位置，分析模型如何从一维 token 序列恢复近似二维网格。结论是即便显式二维结构被弱化，残留的位置模式和角点锚点仍足以让 LLM 重建粗略行列结构。\n4. attention knockout：逐头屏蔽注意力头，测量分类与定位性能下降，找出任务关键头。作者发现真正起因果作用的头非常少，而且 LLaVA 主要集中在早中层，InternVL 主要在中后层。\n5. causal mediation analysis：进一步区分哪些头直接影响定位，哪些头先影响分类再间接影响定位。结果显示分类关键头与定位关键头只有部分重叠，但定位在因果上依赖分类相关头，支持“先识别对象，再做空间 grounding”的串行机制。\n\nInternVL 的多视图分析也有具体结论：global thumbnail 提供主要空间信号，local high-res tiles 更多用于补充细粒度语义和小目标分类。两者不是冗余关系，而是空间与语义分工不同。这一点对多模态预训练设计是有信息量的，因为它说明更高分辨率输入未必直接改善定位，前提是全局空间框架先建立起来。","experiments":"实验部分最值得看的不是单一指标，而是几组干预实验给出的稳定现象。数据上，作者在修正和过滤后的 COCO 验证集上做分析，规模为 3560 张图、6403 个对象实例。模型包括 LLaVA-1.5 7B、13B，以及 InternVL-3.5 8B。\n\n关键结果可以概括为几条：\n1. 定位信息确实已经编码在视觉 token 中，而不是主要靠语言侧“猜”出来。对对象相关 token 做消融会显著破坏框预测；对无关区域 token 的影响小得多。\n2. 对象内部语义排列被打乱后，只要对象覆盖的 token 边界还在，预测框相对稳定。这支持“containerization”而不是“精细部件几何推理”。\n3. 在 InternVL 中，去掉 global view 会比去掉 local views 更明显地伤害定位；相反，小目标识别和细粒度分类更依赖 local high-res crops。这说明全局图承担坐标系和粗空间布局，局部图承担语义补充。\n4. 位置恢复实验表明，LLM 可以从线性化后的视觉 token 中恢复近似二维网格结构，尤其依赖角点和行边界等弱锚点，而不是完整显式二维编码。\n5. 注意力头分析显示，只有极少数头对分类和定位有显著因果贡献。LLaVA 的关键头偏早中层，InternVL 偏中后层；两任务关键头大体分离，但定位会经过分类关键头。这一点比“哪些头重要”更重要，因为它说明任务分解在网络内部是有阶段性的。\n\n摘要里没有给出完整数字表，全文的价值主要在机制证据链，而不是追求某个 benchmark 提升幅度。阅读时应重点看干预前后性能变化的相对趋势、不同层头的重要性分布、以及 global/local ablation 的对比。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议逐页细抠所有附录。优先看四部分。\n\n第一，看引言和方法里对两类架构的拆解，先建立 token 流动图。LLaVA 与 InternVL 的差别决定了后面很多结论为什么会不同。\n\n第二，看 token ablation 和 controlled perturbation 对应的主图。这部分是全文最核心的证据，决定“containerization”这个结论是否站得住。阅读时要特别注意作者如何控制对象边界、内部语义排列和背景上下文三个变量。\n\n第三，看 InternVL 的 global/local 分析。若关心多视图 VLM 预训练，这部分比单纯的定位机制更有启发，因为它回答了高分辨率 tile 到底在帮什么。\n\n第四，看 attention knockout 与 causal mediation。这里不要只记“少数头很重要”，更要看分类头与定位头的层分布、重叠程度，以及作者如何论证定位依赖分类。\n\n预计 30 到 45 VLMs internally localize objects? More precisely, it separates object identification from box formation and studies where localization signals live, how the LLM recovers spatial structure from visual tokens, whether classification and localization are parallel or sequential, and how these mechanisms differ between a simple single-view model (LLaVA-1.5) and a more complex multi-view model (InternVL-3.5).","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is diagnostic rather than methodological. The main takeaway is that current VLM localization does not look like precise geometric reasoning over pixels. We introduce $S$-$D$ attention as a diagnostic parameterization that disentangles routing from filte","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-12T19:00:17+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_18826.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"标准Transformer的注意力交互矩阵QK^T纠缠了两种独立计算：负责位置间信息定向转移的路由（斜对称分量）和负责评估互相关度的滤波（对称分量），二者资源需求不同但无法被区分，导致没有可测量的原则指导注意力层的资源分配，现有混合架构（如Jamba、Nemotron-H）的层间结构只能通过网格搜索确定，无法泛化到不同参数规模。","importance":"为预训练架构设计提供了直接的方法学启发：首次明确拆分注意力的两个核心功能，证明路由分量天然是低秩的，为低秩/线性注意力替代方案的层间选择提供了可量化的依据，不需要盲目搜索层间混合比例。","technical_approach":"首先对任意注意力头的QK^T做唯一的对称-斜对称分解，分离出滤波（对称分量，无向相似度）和路由（斜对称分量，定向信息转移）两个分量。随后提出S-D注意力作为诊断参数化方式，将无约束的QK^T替换为L=S-D，其中S是可学习的斜对称矩阵，D是可学习的正对角矩阵，该构造保证所有特征值的实部≤0，不需要层归一化就能稳定训练。","experiments":"对5个预训练Transformer的共1776个注意力头做分解，发现路由在真实文本上的实际秩远低于权重核分配的容量，且滤波分量占主导。训练355M参数的S-D注意力模型，不需要任何归一化就能稳定训练；路由会自发形成谱级联：第一层秩为2，随深度逐渐升高，该模式在7M到355M参数规模下均存在；滤波分量则随深度从高秩收敛到每个头一个标量。","reading_guide":"泛读重点关注路由-滤波分解的数学原理、对预训练注意力头的统计分析结果，以及S-D注意力的自组织谱级联现象，预计阅读时间20分钟；核心是理解注意力两个内在功能的分离，及其秩随深度的变化规律，不需要深入理论证明细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The standard Transformer attention interaction matrix QK^T entangles two distinct computations: routing (skew-symmetric component) that directionally transfers information between positions, and filtering (symmetric component) that evaluates pairwise mutual relevance. The two functions have different resource requirements but cannot be separated, leaving no measurable principle to guide resource allocation across attention layers. Existing hybrid architectures such as Jamba and Nemotron-H rely on grid search to determine layer structures, which does not generalize across parameter scales.","importance":"It provides direct methodological insights for pretraining architecture design: it explicitly separates the two core functions of attention for the first time, and proves that the routing component is inherently low-rank. This offers a quantifiable basis for selecting layers to replace with low-rank or linear attention mechanisms, eliminating the need for blind search of inter-layer mixing ratios.","technical_approach":"First, it decomposes any attention head's QK^T into a unique symmetric-skew symmetric pair, separating filtering (symmetric component, undirected similarity) and routing (skew-symmetric component, directional information transfer). Then it proposes S-D attention as a diagnostic parameterization, replacing the unconstrained QK^T with L = S - D, where S is a learnable skew-symmetric matrix and D is a learnable positive diagonal matrix. This construction guarantees Re(λ) ≤ 0 for all eigenvalues, enabling stable training without layer normalization.","experiments":"Decomposition of 1,776 attention heads across 5 pretrained Transformers shows that the effective rank of routing on real text is far lower than the capacity allocated by the weight kernel, and filtering dominates the interaction matrix. A 355M parameter S-D attention model trains stably without any normalization. Routing self-organizes into a spectral cascade: rank starts at 2 in the first layer and increases with depth, a pattern consistent across scales from 7M to 355M parameters. Filtering converges in the opposite direction, from high-rank relevance landscapes to a single scalar per head.","reading_guide":"Focus on the mathematical principle of routing-filtering decomposition, statistical analysis of pretrained attention heads, and the self-organized spectral cascade phenomenon of S-D attention. tool-using LLM agents: models often invoke a tool even when the right behavior is to ask for missing information or answer without a tool. The target is the gating decision between call and no_call, not the syntax or execution quality of tool calls.","importance":"This is mainly a mechanistic interpretability and intervention paper. Its relevance to pretraining and post-training lies in the training signal: tool-use data and rewards can induce a persistent call bias rather than merely stronger call-related activations. The finding suggests that no-call behavior may need explicit boundary calibration, loss balancing, or reward design, instead of relying only on prompt instructions or additional negative examples.","technical_approach":"The authors train TopK sparse autoencoders on residual-stream activations for six models: Qwen3.5 4B/9B, Gemma-3-it 1B/4B, and Ministral-3-Instruct 3B/8B. The SAE dictionary has size M=8d and keeps K=floor(d/32) active features. Training uses a two-stage curriculum: around 50M OpenWebText2 tokens for broad residual-space coverage, followed by around 10M When2Call tokens for tool-use adaptation. At the action boundary, the residual activation is encoded into SAE features. Features are ranked by their association with the model’s observed call or no_call behavior, then validated with linear probes. The central test compresses call and no_call feature bases into a signed activation margin and asks whether equal activation produces a neutral decision. It does not: the neutral boundary is shifted, which supports an activation-independent call offset. The paper then applies Adaptive Margin-Calibrated Steering, a closed-form shift along SAE decoder directions, to cancel the diagnosed offset.","experiments":"On When2Call, all six models show high call accuracy but much lower no-call accuracy, with overall accuracy in the 55%–70% range. The recovered SAE feature bases predict call/no_call behavior close to the residual-stream probe ceiling. Margin geometry and logistic estimation both indicate a shifted boundary: the model becomes neutral only when no_call activation exceeds call activation. With AMCS, no-call accuracy improves by 4–17 points on five of six models, and overall accuracy improves by up to about 5 points, while valid tool-call accuracy is mostly preserved. This provides a causal check that the diagnosed offset is behaviorally relevant.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes. t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-19T15:33:16+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2605_19999.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章讨论的不是如何检测污染，而是更前一步：能不能把 benchmark 设计成“即使被抓进预训练语料，也学不会；但在推理时仍然能正常作答”。作者把这个目标称为 contamination-resistant dataset。全文的核心问题有两个层面。第一，现有 benchmark 在大规模抓取、蒸馏、持续预训练环境下几乎不可避免会泄漏，事后去重、私有化、动态更新都各有明显代价。第二，文本 unlike 图像，传统 unlearnable example 方法很难直接迁移，因为文本是离散符号，且现代 LLM 对噪声、改写、回译有很强恢复能力。作者因此把问题转成一个更结构性的命题：是否可以利用 Transformer 训练与推理之间的不对称性，让某种数据表示在 teacher-forcing 训练中不可学习，但在自回归或条件推理时仍保留任务可解性。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在评测方法论，而不是直接给出可落地的新 benchmark 构造算法。它的重要性在于把“数据污染”从数据工程问题提升成“训练目标与表示设计”的问题。这个视角对基座模型研究有两点启发。其一，benchmark 设计不能再默认明文公开样本是中性的；在 trillion-token 语料和多轮蒸馏环境下，公开即高风险。其二，训练可学性与推理可用性未必绑定，存在通过表示层或架构不对称来分离二者的可能。这个想法如果做实，不只影响 benchmark，也可能影响数据授权、模型水印、训练时访问控制等方向。量级上，它更像一篇研究议程设定型 position paper，而不是已经改变评测实践的技术论文。","technical_approach":"技术上，文章主要做三件事。第一，形式化 contamination resistance。作者给出一个抽象定义：对原始数据 D 施加变换 φ 后，模型在 φ(D) 上仍能完成推理任务，但把 φ(D) 纳入训练并不能有效提升模型对该任务的学习。这里强调的是 inference utility 与 training unlearnability 同时成立。第二，作者把可行性建立在 Transformer 的训练—推理不对称上。训练时通常采用 teacher forcing，模型在每个位置看到完整前缀并优化 next-token likelihood；推理时则是基于已生成上下文逐步解码。作者认为可以构造某种表示，使得训练阶段的局部 token 预测信号被破坏、梯度难以对齐到稳定模式，但推理阶段借助更高层的结构约束、外部规则或可逆映射仍能恢复任务信息。第三，文章讨论 interoperability，也就是这种变换不能只对单一模型家族有效，否则 benchmark 只会变成“对某模型不可学、对另一模型可学”的脆弱防护。作者借用了模型间表示互操作和架构迁移的一些数学讨论，试图说明 contamination resistance 需要对一类训练管线而非单个 checkpoint 成立。\n\n需要注意，全文更偏概念论证，没有给出一个成熟的文本 CRD 构造器，也没有像 D3PM、SEDD 那样给出完整训练算法。作者提到现有文本扰动、shortcut、poisoning 方法不适合，是为了说明为什么不能简单照搬 unlearnable image 的套路。真正的创新点是提出“support inference, resist training”这一目标函数式的 benchmark 设计原则，以及把它和 Transformer 的 teacher-forcing 非对称联系起来。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，这篇文章几乎没有实证部分，至少没有完整的 benchmark 构造实验、污染注入实验或跨模型验证结果。文中主要引用外部工作来建立问题严重性：例如 GPT-3 评测时某些 benchmark 超过 90% 样本被标记为污染；Llama 2 在 MMLU 中超过 16% 子集存在污染；多语 benchmark 的污染比例可高到 91.8%；GSM8K 的 clean mirror set 可让一些模型家族准确率下降约 13%。这些数字说明污染确实会显著抬高分数，但它们不是本文方法的实验结果。若按研究成熟度看，这篇文章目前停留在 formalization + motivation + feasibility argument，缺少三个关键证据：1）具体变换 φ 的实例；2）加入预训练后确实学不会的定量结果；3）在不同模型、不同 tokenizer、不同训练 recipe 下仍保持推理可用的稳健性验证。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看 Introduction 和“Contamination-Resistance: Motivation and unlearnability 的困难讲得比较清楚：离散 token、语义保持改写、LLM 的去噪能力，都会让简单扰动失效。第二，interoperability 这一节虽然偏抽象，但它指出了真正难点：如果变换只骗过某一类训练器，就不算可靠 benchmark 设计。预计阅读时间 15–25 分钟。若要继续深挖，建议顺着文中引用去看 benchmark contamination 检测、clean mirror benchmark、以及 unlearnable examples 在文本域失败的原因。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not about detecting contamination after the fact. It asks a more fundamental question: can we design benchmark datasets that remain usable for inference, yet become ineffective as training data even if they are absorbed into pretraining corpora? The authors call this contamination resistance. The full argument has two layers. First, benchmark leakage is becoming structurally unavoidable under web-scale scraping, derivative datasets, distillation, and continual pretraining. Second, standard “unlearnable examples” ideas do not transfer cleanly to text, because text is discrete and modern LLMs are strong denoisers that can recover semantics through paraphrase or normalization. The paper therefore reframes the problem around a training–inference asymmetry: can one exploit the mismatch between teacher-forced Transformer training and downstream inference so that a transformed benchmark remains solvable at test time but does not provide a useful learning signal during training?","importance":"Its value is methodological rather than immediately operational. The paper elevates benchmark contamination from a data curation issue to a representation-and-objective design issue. That matters for pretraining research in two ways. First, public plaintext benchmarks are no longer neutral artifacts once training corpora reach trillions of tokens and model development includes repeated distillation and refresh cycles. Second, learnability during pretraining and usability during inference may be separable properties. If that separation can be engineered, the idea would matter not only for benchmarking, but also for data licensing, controlled disclosure, and possibly training-time access control. In terms of contribution type, this is a position-setting paper that proposes a research direction. It does not yet provide a validated construction that changes benchmarking practice.","technical_approach":"$51","experiments":"There is little to no direct empirical validation in the provided full text. The paper relies mostly on prior studies to establish the severity of contamination: GPT-3-era audits reportedly flagged over 90% contamination for some benchmarks; Llama 2 showed contamination in more than 16% of MMLU subsets; multilingual benchmark contamination has been reported as high as 91.8%; and a clean mirror version of GSM8K reduced accuracy by roughly 13% for some model families such as Mistral. These numbers motivate the problem, but they are not evaluations of the proposed CRD framework. From a research maturity standpoint, three pieces are still missing: a concrete transformation phi for text, quantitative evidence that including phi(D) in pretraining does not improve task performance, and robustness checks across model families, tokenizers, and training recipes while preserving inference utility.","reading_guide":"$52","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"对抗基准污染的思路很重要，值得了解框架方向，但目前是纯理论呼吁，无具体实现","en":"Important concept for benchmark design against contamination; worth understanding the direction, but currently a pure theoretical proposal without implementation."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Pennsylvania State University"],"authors":["Ali Al-Lawati","Jason Lucas","Dongwon Lee","Suhang Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["benchmark","contamination","evaluation","transformer"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.19999","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2605.18830","title":"In-Context Learning Operates as Concept Subspace Learning","abstract":"Regression and Bayesian accounts of in-context learning (ICL) explain how demonstrations can induce predictors, while mechanistic analyses often identify compact activation directions that steer prompted behavior. using LLM judges to evaluate long-form reports, evidence use, tool calls, and multi-step research trajectories, one must know whether the judges themselves can detect concrete failures. Existing protocols based on human preference agreement or verifiable tasks do not give enough diagnostic signal for open-ended research executions.","importance":"The work is not a direct contribution to pretraining, but it is relevant to automated evaluation, reward modeling, and the quality of supervision for agent training. Deep research outputs are long, open-ended, and evidence-heavy. A single overall judge score can hide whether the model retrieved weak evidence, misused tools, cited unsupported sources, or produced a fluent but poorly grounded report. Reflect turns judge evaluation into fine-grained failure detection with verifiable labels, which is a useful direction for process-level rewards, citation-aware supervision, and tool-use preference data.","technical_approach":"Reflect formulates judge meta-evaluation as a paired comparison. Each instance contains a quality-screened reference agent execution and a perturbed alternative with a controlled, localized failure. An execution consists of the input query, the research trajectory, and the final long-form answer. A reliable judge should assign higher quality to the reference. The perturbations are drawn from a failure taxonomy covering process-level and outcome-level errors in reasoning, tool use, evidence gathering, and synthesis. The design avoids the need for a single canonical answer to an open-ended research question. Instead, labels are verifiable by construction because the failure is injected in a known location and category. The paper compares holistic judging protocols with fine-grained, step-level protocols, where the latter can reveal whether the judge misses retrieval errors, citation-support errors, reasoning errors, or synthesis errors.","experiments":"The experiments evaluate multiple LLM judges under both holistic scoring and fine-grained step-level evaluation. The reported findings show substantial reliability gaps: different judges fail on different failure types, and no single aggregate score captures judge reliability. Fine-grained evaluation is more effective than holistic scoring, especially for macro-level structural failures that require tracing information across stages, such as weak evidence collection that later leads to an unsupported but fluent final report. The paper also studies the trade-off between reliability and cost, noting that finer protocols improve diagnostics but require more judge calls and more complex evaluation pipelines. The provided content does not expose the full model list or exact accuracy numbers, so the safest reading is about methodology and failure patterns rather than absolute ranking.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. The most useful parts are the failure taxonomy, the controlled intervention procedure, and the comparison between holistic and fine-grained judging. 解决的是长上下文 Agent 在反复访问同一外部语料、代码库或文档集合时缺少“上下文方位感”的问题。现有方法保留聊天轨迹、压缩历史、检索原文或积累任务策略，但很少维护一个关于外部上下文本身的、可复用的小型结构化记忆。","importance":"这篇论文不是预训练方法，但对长上下文和 Agent 训练有方法论意义。它把长期记忆从“保存更多历史”转向“保存可迁移的上下文地图”：语料里有什么、结构如何、哪些 schema、常量、实体和证据位置反复有用。对基座研究的启发在于，长上下文能力不只取决于窗口长度，也取决于模型能否形成稳定的外部环境索引。未来如果要训练长期工作流模型，这类 orientation knowledge 把抽取结果转成结构化编辑，更新 map；Evictor 在固定预算下按优先级淘汰低价值内容。论文强调抽取与编辑要分离，否则容易把一次性任务事实、对话噪声和重复内容写入 map。相比 RAG，PEEK 不只是被动取回片段；相比 prompt learning，它维护的不是通用任务策略，而是某个固定外部上下文的方位知识。","experiments":"实验覆盖两类长上下文任务：reasoning accuracy。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"PEEK addresses a recurring-context problem in long-context LLM agents. When an agent repeatedly works over the same document collection, repository, or corpus, existing systems preserve chat history, retrieve raw material, compact transcripts, or learn task-level prompts. They usually do not maintain reusable orientation knowledge about the external context itself.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to long-context model research and agent training. The key idea is to preserve a compact context map rather than more conversation history. The map records what the external context contains, how it is organized, and which entities, schemas, constants, or evidence locations have been useful. For foundation-model work, the implication is clear: long-context performance is not only about context length or retrieval quality. Models and agents also need stable, reusable indexes over their working environment. This can inform future data construction, process supervision, and evaluations for persistent workflows.","technical_approach":"PEEK maintains a fixed-budget context map inside the system prompt. The map is not a KV-cache and not a transcript summary. It is a curated semantic index over a recurring external context. The system has three modules. The Distiller extracts transferable contextual knowledge from execution trajectories. The Cartographer converts extracted knowledge into structured map edits. The Evictor enforces the token budget through priority-based eviction. The separation between extraction and editing is important, because direct updates from trajectories tend to leak task-specific facts, duplicate entries, and noisy observations into the map. Compared with RAG, PEEK actively maintains orientation knowledge rather than only retrieving passages. Compared with prompt learning, it stores context-specific knowledge rather than task procedures.","experiments":"The paper evaluates PEEK on reasoning-and-aggregation tasks and context-learning tasks. On OOLONG, PEEK improves over baselines by 6.3–34.0%. On CL-bench, it improves solving rate by 6.0–14.0% and rubric accuracy by 7.8–12.1%. Against a strong prompt-learning baseline, PEEK gains 10.7% on OOLONG and 6.0% / 9.9% on CL-bench solving rate / rubric accuracy. It is also more efficient: on OOLONG it uses 93–145 fewer iterations than that prompt-learning baseline, and costs 1.7–5.8x less; on CL-bench it costs about 1.4x less. The gains transfer across models, including GPT-5.5 and Qwen3-Coder-Next-FP8, and across agent backbones such as OpenAI Codex.","reading_guide":"A 30–40 minute skim is appropriate. Start with Figure 2 to understand the distinction between Agent/Task State and External Context State, and between active and passive context management. Then read the method description of the Distiller, Cartographer, and Evictor, with special attention to how map updates and evictions are controlled. Section 4.5 is useful because it explains why simply summarizing trajectories into the map is not enough. 计费和未解决实例惩罚打分。这个设计的关键区别是：静态轨用于快速开发和训练，动态轨用于验证离线路由策略是否能在真实闭环中成立。","experiments":"静态轨提供毫秒级确定性评分，不需要在线 evaluator-side LLM judge。作者报告一个用静态标签训练的 logistic router 在所评估策略中取得较好的成本—成功率折中。论文还给出一个诊断结果：Claude Opus leaderboard bill 设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses evaluation for per-step model routing in long-horizon LLM agents. Existing router benchmarks mostly score one-shot prompts, so they do not tell us whether replacing a model at an intermediate agent step will preserve downstream task success. TwinRouterBench treats the router input as the actual prefix visible before each LLM call: conversation history, tool outputs, retrieved passages, shell traces, and partial code edits.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining method, but it is useful for studying capability stratification in agentic systems. It provides a way to measure when a cheaper model is sufficient under realistic trajectory prefixes, using execution outcomes rather than evaluator-side LLM judgments. For pretraining and mid-training research, the main signal is diagnostic: step-level difficulty labels can expose where models differ in tool use, code repair, long-context retrieval, and summarization. The main limitation is that the static labels are approximate, since exhaustive counterfactual execution over all model choices and all steps is combinatorially infeasible.","technical_approach":"TwinRouterBench has two tracks. The static track contains 970 router-visible prefixes from 520 instances across SWE-bench, BFCL, mtRAG, QMSum, and PinchBench. Each prefix is paired with an execution-verified tier label: the cheapest capability tier that can handle the step under the observed prefix. Labels are built with a downgrade-and-cascade protocol, tier-pool cascade verification, mixed-prefix reconstruction, and manual audit for multi-step executable workloads. The dynamic track runs live software-agent routing on SWE-bench Verified. At each model request, a router selects a concrete model from a locked pool, and scoring uses the official SWE-bench resolution predicate, realized provider cost, cache accounting, and a penalty for unresolved instances. The separation between a fast static track and a live dynamic track is the main design choice.","experiments":"The static benchmark supports deterministic millisecond-level scoring and can also be used for training. A logistic router trained on the static labels gives the strongest cost-success trade-off among the policies evaluated by the authors. A useful diagnostic result is that a single-template Claude Opus 4.6 router predicts high tier for only 7 of 147 verified-high SWE-bench steps and fails every SWE trajectory, showing that frontier-model-as-router is not automatically reliable. 亿条视频元信息中做低成本粗筛，先排除和 GUI 操作无关的视频，降低存储和计算压力。第二步用视觉评分模型细筛视频内容，评估画面清晰度、教程完整性和任务相关性。之后从原始视频中抽取任务指令、操作时间戳、动作细节以及动作背后的推理信息，再在对应时间点截取屏幕图像，并把动作精确映射到屏幕坐标。这个设计比直接从视频帧做端到端标注更稳，因为视频压缩和帧间变化会让坐标 grounding 变得不可靠，先定位操作时间再做空间 grounding 可以降低噪声。","experiments":"产出的 WildGUI 覆盖网站、移动端和桌面三类平台，包含 1,500 多个应用和网站、1,270 万条 GUI 操作轨迹、1.245 亿张截图，平均每条轨迹 9.7 个 turn。表格中给出的规模明显大于 MiniWoB++、MIND2WEB、AITW、AndroidControl、GUI-Net 等已有公开数据集。实验部分使用 WildGUI 对 Qwen2.5-VL 和 Mimo-VL 做 continual pre-training，再用人工整理的开源数据做 post-training，并在 GUI grounding、离线 agent benchmark 和在线 agent benchmark 上评估。给出的结论是不同模型架构上都有稳定提升，但当前提供内容没有列出各 benchmark 的具体分数和消融数字。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25 分钟。重点看三部分：第一，Video2GUI 的筛选和自动标注流程，尤其是视觉评分、时间戳定位和坐标 grounding 的实现细节；第二，WildGUI 的数据统计和平台覆盖，判断它和已有 GUI 数据集的差异；第三，continual pre-training 与 post-training 的训练 recipe 和消融，尤其要确认提升来自规模、数据多样性，还是来自更干净的 action grounding。若只关心 LLM 预训练，可跳过具体 GUI benchmark real GUI interaction trajectories are expensive to annotate, small in scale, and usually limited to narrow platforms, which hurts generalization to unseen applications and environments.","importance":"Its main value is in scalable data construction rather than model design. The paper treats unlabeled internet tutorial videos as a source of weak supervision for multimodal agent pretraining. For pretraining researchers, the useful point is that GUI data is not just image-text data; it must bind visual states, actions, screen coordinates, task intent, and temporal structure into one trajectory format.","technical_approach":"Video2GUI uses a coarse-to-fine pipeline. It first filters 500 million video metadata entries using titles, descriptions, and keywords to remove videos unrelated to GUI operations at low cost. It then applies a visual scoring model to assess screen quality, instructional completeness, and task relevance. For selected videos, it extracts task instructions, operation timestamps, action details, and action rationales, samples screenshots at the identified timestamps, and performs spatial grounding to map actions to screen coordinates. This staged design is more robust than direct frame-level annotation because video compression and temporal ambiguity make coordinate grounding noisy.","experiments":"The resulting WildGUI dataset covers websites, mobile apps, and desktop software, with more than 1,500 applications and websites, 12.7 million trajectories, 124.5 million screenshots, and an average trajectory length of 9.7 turns. The authors continually pretrain Qwen2.5-VL and Mimo-VL on WildGUI, then post-train on curated open-source GUI data.
